### [1] [CORAL-KV: Compact Cross-Layer Prediction and Residual Coding for Autoregressive Video KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12514)
*Albert Ge,Akash Haridas,Utkarsh Saxena,Ruijing Yang,Ian Colbert,Frederic Sala,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Coral-KV 利用视频扩散模型跨层 KV 缓存依赖，用块对角加低秩预测器做残差编码量化，显著压缩长视频生成的 KV 缓存并提升重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中 KV 缓存持续增长，显存成为瓶颈。现有基于聚类的量化虽能压缩缓存，但重建误差会随视频长度累积放大。作者发现除了层内相似性外，缓存层间还存在强依赖，未被现有方法利用。

Method: 提出 Coral-KV 框架：用各层缓存状态预测后续层的 key/value，再对预测残差量化。构造紧凑的 block-DPLR 预测器——块对角部分建模单注意力头内的通道关系，全局低秩项建模头间依赖；提供可复用配置与针对当前视频轻量重拟合的适配配置两种保真-内存权衡模式。

Result: 在 HY-WorldPlay 与 MAGI-1 上评估：可复用配置相对 QVG-4 分别提升 PSNR 0.32 dB 与 4.95 dB，缓存减少 48% 与 37%；视频适配配置相对 QVG-Pro 在 MAGI-1 上 PSNR 提升 3.77 dB，缓存减少 33.7%。长视频生成中保持更高重建保真度，减少时间不连续，生成延迟相当。

Conclusion: 跨层缓存依赖是视频扩散模型中被忽视的压缩信号，Coral-KV 通过预测-残差编码实现高效 KV 压缩，为长视频生成提供可扩展的显存方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models maintain a growing attention key-value (KV) cache, making memory a key bottleneck for long video generation. Recently, clustering-based quantization has emerged as an effective method to compress video KV caches by grouping similar key and value vectors then quantizing residual errors. However, when later frames attend to the compressed history, small reconstruction errors can compound as the video grows. Beyond the within-layer similarity exploited by clustering, we find strong dependencies across cache layers in video models. This motivates Coral-KV - Calibrated Offline, Residual-coded, and Adapted Locally - a KV-cache compression framework that uses cache states in each layer to predict keys and values in subsequent layers, then quantizes the remaining prediction residuals. We construct compact block-diagonal-plus-low-rank (block-DPLR) predictors: block-diagonal components model channel relationships within each attention head, while a global low-rank term models dependencies between heads. Coral-KV exposes a flexible fidelity-memory trade-off: it can reuse its predictors across videos or refit lightweight block-diagonal predictors to the current video for higher fidelity. Across extensive evaluations on both HY-WorldPlay and MAGI-1, our reusable configuration improves PSNR over QVG-4 by 0.32 dB and 4.95 dB, respectively, while using 48% and 37% less cache memory. Against QVG-Pro, our video-adapted configuration improves MAGI-1 PSNR by 3.77 dB while using 33.7% less cache memory. Coral-KV also maintains higher reconstruction fidelity over extended generation and reduces temporal discontinuities while retaining comparable generation latency.

</details>


### [2] [TraceICL: Thread-Resolved Demonstration Curation with Consistency Calibration for Robust Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12516)
*Yigit Yilmaz,Elena Petrova,Mehmet Kaya,Lucia Rossi,Amir Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 AVA，将交错多模态上下文学习拆分为线程分解、影响力选择与弱到强一致性校准三个阶段，在五个基准上提升 4-shot 性能且抗噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错图文上下文学习（ICL）虽能让冻结的 LVLM 从少量示范中习得新任务，但存在示范与图像绑定错误、冗余样本挤占信息量、噪声答案被逐字复制等脆弱性问题。

Method: AVA 为免训练三阶段框架：(1) Thread-Resolved Decomposer 将交错上下文切分为逐样本线程并以显式边界重接，恢复图文绑定；(2) Influence-scored Demonstration Selector 用一次额外前向估计候选样本的边际影响，去冗余并按价值降序排列；(3) Weak-to-Strong Consistency Calibration 用弱同级模型的重建置信度打分并在解码时软性重加权。全程不计算梯度，LVLM 保持冻结。

Result: 在五个视觉语言基准的交错 4-shot 设置下，AVA 平均超越最强同设置基线 +1.7 分，VizWiz 上最高 +2.4 分，缩小 ScienceQA-IMG 的需视觉性能差距，30% 噪声示范下仍保留大部分增益，延迟开销 26% 且可被离线缓存摊销。

Conclusion: AVA 以模块化、免训练方式系统化多模态 ICL 的示范流水线，兼顾性能、鲁棒性与可接受的推理开销。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal in-context learning (ICL) lets frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) acquire new tasks from a handful of image-text-answer demonstrations, yet it remains fragile: demonstrations bind to the wrong images, redundant exemplars crowd out informative ones, and noisy answers are copied verbatim into predictions. We present AVA, a training-free framework that organizes the demonstration pipeline of multimodal ICL into three composable stages. A Thread-Resolved Decomposer (TRD) segments the interleaved context into per-sample threads and re-stitches them with explicit boundaries, restoring image-text binding. An Influence-scored Demonstration Selector (IDS) estimates each candidate's marginal influence with one extra forward pass, removes redundancy, and orders the survivors by descending value. A Weak-to-Strong Consistency Calibration (WSC) module scores each demonstration by a weak sibling model's reconstruction confidence and softly reweights its contribution during decoding. On five vision-language benchmarks with interleaved 4-shot prompts, AVA improves the strongest same-setting baseline by +1.7 points on average, with gains of up to +2.4 points on VizWiz, shrinks the require-visual performance gap on ScienceQA-IMG, and retains most of its gain under 30% noisy demonstrations, at a 26% latency overhead that an offline cache amortizes. No gradients are ever computed; the LVLM stays frozen.

</details>


### [3] [TerraAgent: Demonstration-Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Long-Context Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12517)
*Jingwen Huang,Yiming Fang,Hanxiao Wu,Boyuan He,Keyi Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 AVA，通过 GSD 分桶场景图检索、证据类型化子问题 DAG 与反事实视觉依赖审计，显著提升遥感 MLLM 的视觉依赖性并降低先验作答与幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多模态大模型（RS MLLM）主要以准确率评估，无法判别正确答案是否真正依赖图像证据；同时跨 GSD/传感器/城市纹理的检索示例匹配差、长视觉上下文稀释证据并传播误差、纯先验作答对标准指标不可见，这三大挑战亟需在评测与推理层面被显式解决。

Method: AVA 为免训练框架（不微调、包裹冻结 RS MLLM），含三模块：GDR 做 GSD 分桶、基于场景图的示例检索，并过滤答案不被自身图像支持的示例；MACE 将查询分解为按证据类型标注的子问题 DAG，路由到合适感知工具，并在共享证据账本中记录可验证出处，配合有界重感知；CVA 对引用证据做反事实扰动（消融与替换）审计最终答案，计算视觉依赖分用于置信度校准与证据强制重查询。

Result: 在 RSVQA-LR/HR、VRSBench、LHRS-Bench 上，AVA 相较同设置最强基线平均准确率最高提升 2.40 分；视觉依赖分从 0.28 升至 0.49，纯先验作答率从 46.2% 降至 21.7%，幻觉率从 17.1% 降至 9.8%；代价约为单次主干前向的 2.6 倍且无需微调。

Conclusion: AVA 表明在遥感 VQA 中，通过检索去偏、证据溯源推理与反事实审计，可在不训练的情况下同时提升准确率与视觉依赖性，为 RS MLLM 提供比精度更可诊断的评估与推理范式。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual question answering (RS-VQA) requires answers that truly depend on image evidence, yet current remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS MLLMs) are still evaluated mainly by accuracy, which cannot reveal whether a correct answer was produced without using the image. This setting introduces three challenges: demonstrations retrieved across different ground sample distances (GSD), sensors, and city textures may be poorly matched; real queries often involve long visual contexts from multi-scale tiles, multi-temporal frames, and retrieved knowledge, where evidence can be diluted and errors can propagate through multi-step reasoning; and prior-only answering remains invisible to standard metrics. We present AVA, a training-free framework that wraps a frozen RS MLLM with three modules. GDR performs GSD-bucketed, scene-graph-based demonstration retrieval and filters demonstrations whose answers are unsupported by their images. MACE decomposes each query into an evidence-typed sub-question DAG, routes sub-questions to suitable perception tools, and records verified provenance in a shared evidence ledger with bounded re-perception. CVA audits the final answer through counterfactual perturbations of cited evidence, including ablation and swap, and computes a visual-dependency score for confidence calibration and evidence-forcing re-query. On RSVQA-LR/HR, VRSBench, and LHRS-Bench, AVA improves average accuracy by up to 2.40 points over the strongest same-setting baselines, raises the visual-dependency score from 0.28 to 0.49, reduces the prior-only answer rate from 46.2 percent to 21.7 percent, and lowers the hallucination rate from 17.1 percent to 9.8 percent, with 2.6x the cost of a single backbone forward pass and no fine-tuning.

</details>


### [4] [MIRAGE: Metric Inference and Reliability-Aware Gaussian Estimation from Feed-Forward Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12568)
*Xinzhe Wang,Changjian Jiang,Kaiwen Song,Xudong Li,Kerui Ren,Ran Yi,Lizhuang Ma,Chunhua Shen,Linning Xu,Tao Lu,Mulin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将前馈视觉几何锚定到LiDAR-IMU度量运动，并按对应支持度与跨模态一致性调节在线位姿修正，实现度量精度与高保真高斯重建，并发布长时程室内单目基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D先验可从图像流高质量重建，但缺乏可靠度量尺度且长时程轨迹漂移；LiDAR-惯性估计度量准确却缺少图像空间一致性。作者希望统一二者以同时获得准确度量位姿与高质量重建，并填补大规模室内长时程单目基准的缺失。

Method: 提出MIRAGE：以前馈视觉几何为先验，锚定到LiDAR-IMU度量运动；依据对应点支持度与跨模态一致性对在线位姿修正进行调节（regulate），联合优化度量位姿与高斯泼溅重建。另搭建多模态采集平台，用MIRAGE生成新单目基准的6-DoF参考轨迹。

Result: 在Oxford Spires、M2DGR、INS上实现准确度量轨迹估计，优于COLMAP这一计算昂贵的视觉基准；在新采集的长时程室内数据上对近期单目大规模重建方法做了系统评测。

Conclusion: 跨模态锚定+一致性调节可兼得度量精度与高保真重建；长时程大规模室内单目重建仍存在明显性能瓶颈，需更系统的基准与评估。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D priors enable high-quality reconstruction from image streams but often lack reliable metric scale and suffer from long-term trajectory drift. In contrast, LiDAR--inertial estimation provides metrically accurate motion but lacks the image-space consistency needed for high-fidelity reconstruction. We propose $MIRAGE$, which anchors feed-forward visual geometry to LiDAR--IMU metric motion and regulates online pose corrections according to correspondence support and cross-modal agreement, jointly enabling accurate metric pose estimation and high-fidelity Gaussian reconstruction. Experiments on Oxford Spires, M2DGR and INS show that MIRAGE achieves accurate metric trajectory estimation, outperforming COLMAP, a widely used but computationally expensive reference for vision-only benchmarks. Motivated by the scarcity of long-horizon monocular benchmarks for large-scale indoor reconstruction, we further build a multimodal data-collection platform and use MIRAGE to establish accurate 6-DoF reference trajectories for a new vision-only benchmark. We benchmark recent monocular large-scale reconstruction methods on this dataset, providing a systematic evaluation of their performance in long-horizon indoor scenes.

</details>


### [5] [Scaling Multi-Teacher Distillation for Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12574)
*Sofiène Boutaj,Pierre Marza,Varun Belagali,Dimitris Samaras,Maria Vakalopoulou,Stergios Christodoulidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出标准多教师蒸馏(MTD)在教师数量增加时性能反而下降,提出通过学习"教师特定表征"(教师token、定制注意力、MoE层、对比损失)来提升可扩展性,从而用10个教师基础模型的蒸馏训练出10倍高效的紧凑学生编码器OMNI,在39个tile/slide级任务上匹配甚至超越更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师蒸馏可从多个教师网络向单一学生网络迁移知识,在数字病理等领域很有前景(近期已有许多强大基础模型)。但作者发现:标准MTD在教师数量增加时反应很差,难以扩展,因此需要新的机制来支撑"更多教师→更强学生"的规模化。

Method: 核心是学习教师特定表征:引入可学习的教师token、定制化的注意力方案、额外的混合专家(MoE)层以及对比损失。作者进行了充分实证验证,在tile级与slide级共39个任务上评估了10+个基础模型。

Result: 所提方法在教师数量上具有更好的扩展性,可训练出比大型教师基础模型最高高效10倍的紧凑学生编码器,在大规模tile级与slide级任务上性能匹配甚至超越教师。据此发布新模型集合OMNI,由10个SOTA基础模型蒸馏得到。分析表明学到的教师特定表征具有互补性,易在下游聚合。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模教师特定表征,MTD可以随教师数量增加而持续受益,从而以极低推理成本获得强性能。该范式具有可推广性,后续可用于更多基础模型的整合与高效部署。

Abstract: Multi-teacher distillation allows transferring knowledge from multiple "teacher" networks to a single "student" network. This method is promising in fields such as digital pathology, where many powerful foundation models were proposed recently. However, as we show in this paper, the standard multi-teacher distillation approach reacts poorly to an increase in the number of teachers used to train a student encoder. We demonstrate that learning teacher-specific representations is a key to scaling in MTD. Importantly, different design choices, such as learnable teacher tokens, a tailored attention scheme, additional mixture-of-experts layers and a contrastive loss, are proposed to better learn such teacher-specific representations. We show that our method better scales with respect to the number of considered teachers, allowing us to train compact student encoders, up to 10x more efficient than larger teacher foundation models, while matching their performance and even outperforming them on a large set of tile-level and slide-level tasks. We conduct a thorough empirical validation, evaluating more than 10 foundation models on 39 tasks spanning tile and slide levels. As a result, we release a new collection of strong and efficient foundation models, named OMNI, trained from the knowledge of 10 state-of-the-art foundation models. We finally conduct an analysis of the learned teacher-specific representations, highlighting their complementarity and explaining why they can be easily aggregated at downstream time.

</details>


### [6] [Mind the Temporal Gap: Evaluating Video Models for Temporal Understanding in Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12575)
*Shiva Sreeram,Erfan Aasi,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Alaa Maalouf,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控自运动任务评估视频-语言模型（VLM）在驾驶场景中的时序推理能力，发现各类提示方法不可靠且存在类别偏置，而轻量LoRA微调与中间视觉特征探针能恢复强运动判别性能，说明模型内部已含运动线索但最终视频到语言通路未可靠利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM日益被视为通用视频推理器，但其在驾驶等安全关键场景中是否稳健捕捉时序动态尚不清楚，需要实证检验。

Method: 设计受控自运动任务，从驾驶视频中识别动作；对比标准提示、视觉提示、few-shot、思维链提示与LoRA微调、基于中间视觉特征的简单探针。

Result: 提示方法表现不可靠、强依赖任务与模型、存在类别偏置（新模型few-shot有任务特定提升）；LoRA微调与视觉特征探针恢复强运动判别能力。

Conclusion: 驾驶场景下现有VLM仍是不可靠的时序推理器，更接近帧级、偏置驱动的识别器而非可信的时序世界模型。

Abstract: Modern video-language models are increasingly treated as general-purpose video reasoners, yet it remains unclear whether they robustly capture temporal dynamics in safety-critical settings such as driving. We study this question through controlled ego-motion tasks that require identifying actions from driving videos. Across video-language models, we find that standard prompting, visual prompting, few-shot prompting, and chain-of-thought prompting remain unreliable and highly task- and model-dependent, with persistent class biases, although newer models show substantial task-specific few-shot gains. In contrast, lightweight LoRA finetuning and simple probes trained on intermediate visual features recover strong motion-discriminative performance, indicating that motion-relevant cues are often present inside the models but are not reliably used by the final video-to-language pathway. These results suggest that, in driving, current video-language models remain unreliable temporal reasoners and often behave more like frame-level, bias-driven recognizers than dependable temporal world models.

</details>


### [7] [MemReg: Streaming Outdoor LiDAR Point Cloud Registration with Hybrid Memory Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12624)
*Jiuming Liu,Mengmeng Liu,Guangming Wang,Lihao Liu,Michael Ying Yang,Yunpeng Zhang,Francesco Nex,Hao Cheng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemReg：面向流式LiDAR序列的多帧室外点云配准网络，通过隐式特征记忆缓冲与显式位姿记忆缓冲存储并动态更新位姿相关时序特征，配合动态历史加权模块自适应融合当前与历史信息，在KITTI、nuScenes、Apollo-Southbay上超越成对方法与多帧SLAM系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外LiDAR配准方法受限于成对（pair-wise）输入范式，忽略了流式序列中固有的时序相关性。长时序LiDAR序列蕴含丰富的全局上下文，可用于补全稀疏测量、滤除离群点并缓解低重叠问题，从而提升配准性能，但此前方法未能充分利用该信息。

Method: 设计多帧室外点云配准网络，核心为两个混合记忆缓冲：隐式记忆特征缓冲与显式记忆位姿缓冲，用于存储并动态更新位姿相关时序特征；并提出动态历史加权模块，自适应融合当前与历史位姿相关特征。整体以流式序列为输入而非成对点云。

Result: 在KITTI、nuScenes、Apollo-Southbay三个室外数据集上达到SOTA，超越所有先前的成对方法及多帧SLAM系统；在3DMatch、3DLoMatch、ScanNet等多视图室内配准场景中同样表现良好，泛化性较强。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 将记忆缓冲机制引入流式LiDAR配准，验证了长时序全局上下文对稀疏补全、离群点滤除与低重叠问题的价值，且方法在室内多视图场景具备良好跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Prior outdoor LiDAR registration works are commonly limited by the pair-wise input paradigm, which neglects temporal correlations inherent within streaming sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel multi-frame outdoor point cloud registration network for streaming LiDAR scans enhanced with pose-related memory buffers. The key observation is that long-term temporal LiDAR sequences can provide rich global contextual information to complete sparse measurements, filter outliers, and address low-overlap problems, thereby boosting registration performance. Specifically, two hybrid memory buffers are designed, including an implicit memory feature buffer and an explicit memory pose buffer, to store and dynamically update pose-related temporal features. Moreover, a novel dynamic history weighting module is developed to adaptively fuse current and history pose-related features. Extensive experiments on three outdoor datasets, including KITTI, nuScenes, and Apollo-Southbay, demonstrate state-of-the-art performance of MemReg, surpassing all previous pair-wise methods and also multi-frame SLAM systems. Our method also generalizes surprisingly well to multiview indoor registration scenarios with rather competitive performance on 3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, and ScanNet. Code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [ForcingBooster: Adaptive Patch-wise Denoising for Fast Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12625)
*Zekai Gu,Peng Wang,Jiahao Lu,Yongsen Mao,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 \methodname{}：对自回归视频扩散模型中的每个 patch 动态分配去噪步数，易 patch 提前停止、难 patch 多步精修，在几乎不损失画质的前提下将总去噪步数减少约 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散在生成下一段视频时对所有 patch 采用统一的空间计算预算（相同去噪调度与 NFE），忽略了视频内容的空间异质性：静态、简单或重复区域易去噪，而复杂区域需更多精修或上下文。统一步数会造成易 patch 的冗余计算；若直接降低固定步数，则难 patch 精修不足。

Method: 对每个 patch 进行动态去噪步数路由：易 patch 提前停止，难 patch 获得更多精修步；引入轻量路由模块，仅需少量训练即可与现有多步/少步自回归扩散模型兼容。

Result: 在实验中跨视频 patch 的总去噪步数减少约 50%，视觉质量与四步基线相当，加速效果显著且质量退化可忽略。

Conclusion: 空间自适应的去噪预算分配是提升自回归视频扩散生成效率的有效通用手段，可与现有少步模型无缝结合。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have become a leading approach to real-time interactive video generation and video world modeling. However, existing methods typically allocate computation uniformly across space when generating the next video chunk, updating all patches with the same denoising schedule and number of function evaluations. This ignores the spatial heterogeneity of video content: static, simple, or recurring regions are often easier to denoise, while other regions require further refinement or additional context. A uniform budget thus leads to redundant computation on easy patches, whereas reducing the fixed step count can leave difficult patches insufficiently refined. We introduce \methodname{}, a general framework that dynamically allocates denoising steps to individual patches: easy patches stop early to improve efficiency, while difficult patches receive more refinement steps. This design accelerates generation with negligible quality degradation. Our method is compatible with most existing few-step autoregressive diffusion models, requiring only a lightweight routing module with modest training. Experiments demonstrate that \methodname{} reduces the total number of denoising steps applied across video patches by about 50\% while maintaining visual quality comparable to the four-step baseline.

</details>


### [9] [Feature Competition and Its Representational Origins in Vision Language Models: Evidence from the Stroop Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12650)
*Nurbüke Teker,Rui Xiao,Zeynep Akata,Shuchen Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将心理学Stroop范式迁移为VLM基准，发现文本与颜色冲突时模型系统性偏向文本；嵌入空间文本线索更可分，可解释并可通过激活引导操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM面对图中多个同等相关的特征时必须"选择"回答哪个，但这种选择机制未被研究。作者借Stroop冲突范式系统考察VLM的特征偏好及其内部来源。

Method: 构建Stroop式冲突图像基准（文字语义与颜色不一致），测试多种对比式与生成式VLM的行为；在嵌入空间分析文本与颜色线索的表征强度与可分性，并进行激活引导（steering）干预实验。

Result: 模型普遍偏好文本线索而非颜色；文本线索在嵌入空间中表征更强、更可分，该表征不对称可预测干预难度——导向文本方向比导向颜色方向容易得多。

Conclusion: 同一图像内不同特征对VLM行为影响不等，存在线索特异性显著性，需理解该显著性如何影响下游应用表现。

Abstract: When you ask a vision-language model (VLM) a question with an image, it answers your question based on the image. But to act means to choose: saying one thing means choosing to respond not say another. This becomes an issue when the image contains multiple features that equally relate to the question at hand: how would VLMs choose to respond? This problem is precisely studied by the classical Stroop task in psychology: among a set of deliberately designed images whose text and color conflicts (e.g., \textcolor{blue}{RED}), participants are instructed to report the color. We adapt this psychological paradigm to a benchmark and prompted a range of contrastive and generative VLMs on their behavior. Across models, we observe a persistent bias toward the feature of text in Stroop images: when text and color disagree, models preferentially generate responses centered to the text rather than color. We then opened the black box and examined the source of this bias and why certain features influences the model's behavior more than others. In the embedding space, we found that textual cues are represented more strongly and separably than color cues. This representational asymmetry predicts intervention success: steering models toward text-related directions is substantially easier than steering them toward color-related directions. Using a classical psychological paradigm, we demonstrate, at both behavioral and representational levels, that distinct features within the same image can exert unequal influence on VLM behavior. Our findings suggest that VLMs assign different degrees of saliency to different features within the same image, highlighting the need to understand how cue-specific saliency shapes their performance in widely used downstream applications.

</details>


### [10] [WoodBR-Cross: A public wood-image dataset and individual-tree-disjoint benchmark for species identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12676)
*Daniel Casanova,Alexandre Bahia Gontijo,Arnaldo Candido Junior,Silvana Nisgoski,Pedro Luiz de Paula Filho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建木材横切面图像基准 WoodBR-Cross，用树木个体互斥的数据划分（B2）评估树种识别模型，揭示同株树木重复出现会高估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有木材图像基准中，同一棵树的图像可能同时出现在训练集与测试集，导致性能估计过于乐观，无法反映对未见树木的泛化能力。

Method: 基于巴西林务局藏品，构建 4,875 张横切面图像、38 个操作分类类、303 棵树的 WoodBR-Cross；构造物种子集（3,406 图、20 类、214 棵树）。设计三种任务：A 全类别的重复感知图像划分、B1 物种子集的图像划分、B2 该子集的单树互斥划分（同一树全部图像保持在同一划分内）。用 ResNet-50、ConvNeXt-Tiny、ViT-B/16 做三折外层交叉验证、内部验证与五个训练种子，并加入手工特征、冻结特征、仅边缘及随机标签对照。

Result: 验证集选择的 ConvNeXt-Tiny 在 B2 上取得树级 macro-F1 0.8621±0.0317；冻结 DINOv2 特征达 0.9000±0.0722（三折、种子平均后的均值与标准差）。

Conclusion: 单树互斥划分能给出对未见树木更诚实的性能估计，为木材树种识别提供更可靠的评测协议。

Abstract: Wood-image benchmarks can yield optimistic estimates when images from the same tree occur in both training and test sets. WoodBR-Cross contains 4,875 transverse-surface images in 38 operational taxonomic classes, linked to 303 distinct trees identified by numeric catalogue witness codes (codigos-testemunho) in the Brazilian Forest Service collection. A species-level subset comprises 3,406 images, 20 classes and 214 trees. Three tasks compare duplicate-aware image partitions for all classes (A), image partitions for the species subset (B1), and individual-tree-disjoint partitions for that subset (B2). In B2, all images from each tree are kept together, so no tree is shared across training, validation or test within a fold. ResNet-50, ConvNeXt-Tiny and ViT-B/16 were evaluated with three outer folds, internal validation and five training seeds. Handcrafted and frozen-feature baselines complement border-only and randomized-label controls. Validation-selected ConvNeXt-Tiny achieved B2 tree-level macro-F1 of 0.8621 +/- 0.0317; frozen DINOv2 features yielded 0.9000 +/- 0.0722. Values are means and standard deviations across three seed-averaged fold means. Results estimate performance on previously unseen trees from this collection.

</details>


### [11] [Bridging Human and Model Attention: Explainability Analysis of CNN, Mamba, and ViT Architectures with Gaze-Based Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12703)
*Marco Stefani,Edoardo Parpaiola,Matteo Bino,Gianluca Caregnato,Francesco Roncolato,Daniel Fusaro,Loris Nanni,Alberto Pretto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对CNN、ViT与Vision Mamba三类视觉骨干做系统可解释性评测，用眼动注视数据作为人类视觉感知的真值，把事后归因图与注视热图统一归一化为空间概率分布，用JS相似度、卡方相似度与皮尔逊相关三个互补指标量化对齐程度，并分析骨干冻结与集成策略的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险深度学习应用需要精确刻画模型决策依据；现有研究多为定性可视化，缺乏与人类视觉注意的定量对齐评估，也缺少跨架构（CNN/ViT/ViM）的系统比较。

Method: 构建标准化归一化流程，将事后归因图与眼动注视热图映射为空间概率分布；在细粒度分类、医学影像与情绪识别任务上，采用架构专用的XAI方法计算归因；用Jensen-Shannon相似度、卡方相似度与皮尔逊相关三类估计量度量支持集重叠、概率质量差异与结构协变；并考察骨干冻结与集成策略对可解释性的影响。

Result: CNN倾向全局注意，显著图更弥散，在通用场景中与人类感知相关性较好；ViT呈细粒度patch级注意，常聚焦判别性微特征；Mamba表现偏置：医学任务可解释性强，自然图像中强度分布发散，且出现支持集近乎完全重叠而线性相关趋近于零的解离现象，指标体系使该解离显式可观测。

Conclusion: 架构与训练策略选择显著影响模型显著性与人类视觉注意的对齐程度；所提出的归一化流程与多指标评估可为跨架构可解释性提供可量化、可比较的分析框架。

Abstract: High-risk applications of deep learning require precise characterization of model decision-making. We conduct a systematic explainability evaluation across Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), and Vision Mamba (ViM). Unlike predominantly qualitative studies, we quantify the alignment between model attention and human visual perception using eye-tracking ground truth. We formalize this comparison via a standardized normalization pipeline mapping post-hoc attribution maps and gaze heatmaps onto spatial probability distributions. Alignment is evaluated across fine-grained classification, medical imaging, and emotion recognition using architecture-specific XAI methods. We measure fidelity using Jensen-Shannon similarity, Chi-square similarity, and Pearson correlation, three complementary estimators capturing support overlap, mass discrepancy, and structural co-variation. Our results reveal distinct attention dynamics: CNNs tend to exhibit a more global focus, with spatially more diffuse saliency maps that correlate well with human perception in general contexts, while ViTs demonstrate fine-grained, patch-level attention, often focused on discriminative microfeatures. The Mamba architecture shows biased behavior: it achieves strong explainability in medical tasks but exhibits divergent intensity distributions in natural images; our analytical characterization of the metrics makes this dissociation explicit, since near-unit support overlap is attained simultaneously with a vanishing linear correlation. We further analyze the effect of backbone freezing and ensemble strategies on model interpretability. The results highlight how architectural and training choices influence the alignment between model saliency and human visual attention.

</details>


### [12] [Zebra3D: Efficient Hybrid Foundation Models For Scalable 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12727)
*Utkarsh Saxena,Ruijing Yang,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Akash Haridas,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zebra3D 提出层间稀疏-线性混合注意力框架，对预训练前馈3D基础模型（VGGT、HunyuanWorld-Mirror、π³）进行免训练转换，在保持高重建与位姿精度的同时实现高注意力稀疏度与最高7.1倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D基础模型依赖全局注意力的二次复杂度，难以扩展到大量输入视图。作者观察到不同深度层对注意力替换的容忍度不同（浅层容忍线性化，中后层注意力更集中），因而希望在不从头训练的前提下提升预训练模型的效率。

Method: 按层分配注意力类型：对容忍层使用线性注意力，对敏感层使用tile-sparse注意力，并用landmark summaries保留未选中上下文；通过闭式仿射拟合实现免训练转换，另提供可选的后转换训练，结合任务监督与输出/关系特征蒸馏。

Result: 在三个骨干上均取得高稀疏度下的良好重建与相机位姿精度，免训练版本接近稠密模型；端到端加速随视图数增加，1024视图下最高7.1×。经后转换训练，Zebra3D-VGGT在94%平均注意力稀疏度下达到80.5% RealEstate10K AUC@30，与同数据同调度微调的稠密VGGT相差1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 深层注意力结构具有可利用的异质性，层间混合注意力可高效地将预训练前馈3D基础模型扩展至大视图集，且免训练或轻量后训练即可保持性能。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct scenes from unposed images, but the quadratic cost of global attention limits their scalability to large view sets. We present Zebra3D, a layerwise sparse-linear hybrid attention framework that improves the efficiency of pretrained 3D foundation models without training from scratch. Across VGGT, HunyuanWorld-Mirror, and $\pi^3$, we observe a broadly consistent depth-wise pattern: early stages are relatively tolerant to linear replacement, while sensitive middle-to-late stages tend to exhibit more concentrated attention. Zebra3D exploits this structure by assigning linear-centered attention to tolerant stages and tile-sparse attention to sensitive stages, using landmark summaries to preserve unselected context. Closed-form affine fitting enables training-free conversion, while optional post-conversion training combines task supervision with output and relational feature distillation. Across all three backbones, Zebra3D achieves strong reconstruction and camera-pose accuracy at high attention sparsity, with training-free variants remaining close to their dense counterparts. End-to-end speedups increase with input view count, reaching up to $7.1\times$ at 1,024 views. After post-conversion training, Zebra3D-VGGT reaches 80.5\% RealEstate10K AUC@30 at 94\% mean attention sparsity, within 1.1 percentage points of dense VGGT fine-tuned on the same data and schedule.

</details>


### [13] [Reference-Free Food Volume Estimation from Egocentric Stereo Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12735)
*Vivan Kumar,Gautham Vinod,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用可穿戴双目相机的自校准基线与VIO位姿，对单段第一视角立体视频进行零样本立体匹配、食物分割与TSDF融合，实现无需参照物、无需多视角拍摄的真实尺度食物体积估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于立体视觉的食物份量估计存在使用负担：需用手机拍摄多张图像，或在场景中放置参照物进行尺度标定。作者面向被动式饮食摄入监测，希望仅凭可穿戴设备的单次立体录像自动完成物理尺度重建。

Method: 以可穿戴第一视角立体视频为输入：用 FoundationStereo 做零样本立体匹配，用 SAM 2 做食物分割，在若干关键帧上做 TSDF 融合，得到带真实尺度的 3D 网格。尺度来源不依赖场景参照物，而是利用可穿戴设备的工厂标定双目基线与设备端 VIO 估计的相机位姿，实现全自动重建。

Result: 在 5 个录制食物项上给出初步体积估计结果，平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）为 9.1%；重建网格在视觉与度量上均较为准确。

Conclusion: 将视觉基础模型（零样本立体匹配、分割）与可穿戴设备固有的标定基线和 VIO 位姿结合，可免参照物、免多视角交互地实现真实尺度食物体积估计，是迈向被动饮食监测的一步。但评测规模很小（仅 5 项食物），尚需更大规模验证。

Abstract: Current stereo vision-based approaches for food portion estimation are hindered by the user burden of using a mobile phone to capture multiple images or placing a reference object in the scene for scale calibration. As a step towards passive dietary intake monitoring, we introduce a method that uses stereo video from a wearable egocentric device to reconstruct food items in true physical scale to accurately estimate volume. By utilizing FoundationStereo for zero-shot stereo matching, SAM 2 for food segmentation, and TSDF fusion over a set of keyframes, our method yields a visually and metrically accurate 3D mesh of any plated food item from a single stereo recording. We leverage the unique offerings of wearables, such as a factory-calibrated stereo baseline and camera poses estimated on-device via visual-inertial odometry (VIO), to perform fully automatic reconstruction without a scale reference object in the scene. We show strong initial volume estimation results across five recorded food items, achieving a mean absolute percentage error of 9.1%.

</details>


### [14] [Shift-Aware Identity-Guided Latent Refinement for Referring Audio--Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12783)
*Kun Li,Sami Sebastian Brandt,Michael Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Shift-Aware Identity-guided Latent (SAIL)精化框架，通过双token策略解耦帧级动态与全局语义锚点，显式建模目标漂移，并借助漂移感知精化模块与连续记忆更新的解码器，提升Ref-AVS任务中的时空一致性与身份一致性，在两基准上超越AVS/Ref-VOS/Ref-AVS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ref-AVS需在视觉、音频、语言三模态交互下分割指定目标，但现有方法在发生目标漂移（瞬态遮挡、声学衰减、身份歧义）时难以保持一致性，导致焦点错误转移。

Method: SAIL框架：1) 双token策略，将帧级动态与全局语义锚点解耦以建模漂移线索；2) 漂移感知精化模块，用领域特定证据对齐token以修正漂移；3) 解码器融合精化嵌入与连续记忆更新，维持时空与身份一致性。

Result: 在两个Ref-AVS基准上取得显著效果，超越现有AVS、Ref-VOS及Ref-AVS方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模目标漂移并引入身份引导的潜在精化与连续记忆，可有效缓解三模态指代分割中的时序不一致问题；代码将在发表后开源。

Abstract: Referring Audio--Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS) is an emergent multimodal task requiring the precise identification and segmentation of a specific object based on a natural language expression grounded in both visual and auditory cues. Unlike traditional AVS, which segments generic sound sources, or referring video object segmentation (Ref-VOS), which relies solely on visual--textual grounding, Ref-AVS necessitates fine-grained tri-modal interaction across vision, audio, and language. However, existing methods often fail to maintain consistency when subjected to target shift, a phenomenon where the model erroneously transfers its focus between objects due to transient occlusions, acoustic fading, or identity ambiguities. To address this, we propose a novel Shift-Aware Identity-guided Latent (SAIL) refinement framework. It introduces a dual-token strategy that decouples frame-level dynamics from a global semantic anchor to explicitly model target-shift cues. We design a shift-aware refinement module that rectifies drift by aligning tokens with domain-specific evidence. Finally, we leverage a decoder that integrates the refined embeddings with a continuous memory update to maintain spatio-temporal coherence and identity consistency throughout the video sequence. Extensive experiments conducted on two Ref-AVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method, significantly outperforming existing AVS, Ref-VOS, and Ref-AVS approaches. The code will be released after publication.

</details>


### [15] [Domain Adaptation of SAM 2.1 for Building-Component Segmentation in Image-Based 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12786)
*Ehsan Agha Ebrahimi,Yong-Cheol Lee,Keith G. Mills*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在HBD建筑数据集上微调SAM 2.1，提升建筑构件多类2D分割（mIoU 49.9%→69.6%），并将更准确的掩码用于GARField的NeRF 3D分组，改善建筑结构3D自动重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的3D建筑构件重建依赖2D建筑图像的准确分割；GARField等3D分组方法需要一致性的2D掩码前提，而通用视觉基础模型在建筑构件多类别（墙、窗、门、梁、板等）分割上精度不足，限制了3D分组的质量。

Method: 构建HBD数据集（42栋建筑、2679张标注图像、11类构件）；微调SAM 2.1以增强多类建筑构件分割；以SegFormer为基线对比逐类掩码；将掩码在NeRF中分组，比较预训练与微调SAM 2.1在新渲染图像上的分组效果。

Result: 微调后mIoU从49.9%提升至69.6%；逐类IoU/Dice显示分割改善；SegFormer在部分大表面积类上表现更优；微调后的SAM 2.1在用户选定分组尺度下改善了建筑构件的3D分组一致性。

Conclusion: 微调SAM 2.1可显著提升建筑构件2D分割精度，并为3D掩码蒸馏提供更可靠的来源，推动GARField在建筑结构3D自动重建中的应用。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of building components using image-based scanning requires appropriate segmentation of the objects in the 2D building images. In order to benefit from consistent grouping of the 2D masks in 3D environments, vision foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model have been utilized by Group Anything with Radiance Fields (GARField) to perform 3D segmentation. This study fine-tuned SAM 2.1 on the HBD dataset, consisting of 2,679 annotated images from 42 buildings, to enhance multi-class segmentation of 2D building images, covering 11 classes (Wall, Window, Ceiling, Door, Column, Beam, Floor, Opening, Curtain Wall, Roof, and Lift). The model was evaluated using per-class IoU, Dice, and mIoU metrics, and the results show an improvement in mIoU from 49.9% to 69.6%, indicating a meaningful improvement in class segmentation and providing a more accurate model for distilling the masks in 3D. SegFormer was then used as a baseline, generating per-class masks for comparison with SAM 2.1, which indicates improvement in some object classes with larger surface area. The masks in the building images were grouped in the neural radiance field, and the pretrained SAM 2.1 was compared with the fine-tuned SAM 2.1 in the newly rendered images. This comparison shows that fine-tuning improves the grouping of building components at a user-selected grouping scale. This advances the application of Group Anything with Radiance Fields (GARField) in 3D, enabling better segmentation of building structures for 3D auto-reconstruction.

</details>


### [16] [Spend Where It Matters: Particle Search with MH Refinement for Budget-Constrained Audio-Visual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12811)
*Shivanshu Shekhar,Raghavendra Addanki,Tong Zhang,Uttaran Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SWIM (Spend Where It Matters)，一个两阶段、免训练、奖励无关的推理时对齐框架，将粒子搜索与 Metropolis-Hastings (MH) 精炼结合，在固定算力预算下提升音视频联合生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导的推理时对齐可提升音视频联合生成，但多目标优化需大量昂贵模型评估；粒子搜索受有限种群限制产生估计误差，MH 可局部精炼却依赖强初始化。作者希望在固定算力下兼顾全局探索与局部精炼。

Method: 两阶段免训练框架。第一阶段利用在线方差估计，在去噪检查点间自适应分配粒子，最小化估计方差并把算力集中在最有用处；该搜索为 MH 提供优质候选。第二阶段在已完成输出上使用局部 noise-and-denoise 提议做 MH 精炼，从强候选出发提升奖励，无需扩大粒子数或全局探索。

Result: 在等算力条件下，粒子搜索+MH 精炼的奖励高于单独任一方法。JavisBench-Mini 上，搭配 JavisDiT++ 相对 BoN 平均相对增益 30.7%、相对 EvoSearch 14.1%；搭配 LTX-2 相对 BoN 9.2%、相对 EvoSearch 17.0%（七项指标平均）。SD1.5 图像生成上自适应粒子分配较均匀分配提升 AestheticScore 2.2%、ImageReward 6.0%。

Conclusion: 自适应粒子分配 + MH 精炼可在固定算力下统一全局搜索与局部精炼，为多目标奖励对齐提供通用、免训练且 reward-agnostic 的方案，并具跨模态（音视频、图像）迁移性。

Abstract: Reward-guided inference-time alignment can improve joint audio-visual generation, but optimizing multiple alignment objectives requires many expensive model evaluations, making existing methods costly under limited compute. Particle-based search enables global exploration but incurs estimation error with a finite population, while Metropolis-Hastings (MH) can refine candidates locally but benefits from a strong initialization. We introduce \textbf{S}pend \textbf{W}here \textbf{I}t \textbf{M}atters (\modelname), a two-stage, training-free, reward-agnostic framework that combines particle search with MH refinement under a fixed compute budget. In the first stage, we adaptively allocate particles across denoising checkpoints using online variance estimates, minimizing estimation variance while concentrating compute where it is most useful. This search provides a promising candidate for MH refinement, which uses local noise-and-denoise proposals on completed outputs to further improve reward. By starting from a strong candidate, MH can recover additional reward without requiring a larger particle population or costly global exploration. We show that, at matched compute, combining particle search with MH refinement achieves higher rewards than either approach alone. On \textsc{JavisBench-Mini}, at closely matched compute, \modelname achieves mean relative gains across seven metrics of $30.7\%$ over BoN and $14.1\%$ over EvoSearch with JavisDiT++, and $9.2\%$ over BoN and $17.0\%$ over EvoSearch with LTX-2. Beyond joint audio-visual generation, we also demonstrate the benefits of adaptive particle allocation on SD1.5 image generation, improving AestheticScore by $2.2\%$ and ImageReward by $6.0\%$ over uniform allocation.

</details>


### [17] [Structure-Aware Matching for Robust UAV Thermal-to-Satellite Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12835)
*Chun Liu,Yuxin Wang,Zhenxiang Huo,Linlin Zhu,Tao Xu,Henggang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知匹配框架SAMF，通过方向结构增强模块(DSEM)与模态感知仿射重映射模块(MARM)利用跨模态不变结构线索，实现无人机热成像到卫星影像的地理定位，并构建Thermal-UAV-Paired基准，在Boson-nighttime等数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机热成像-卫星地理定位可在GNSS拒止与低能见度下实现自主导航，但热成像与卫星影像在颜色、亮度、视角上存在巨大跨模态差异，传统基于外观的方法难以建立可靠对应。现有方法多处理全局模态偏移，忽视了热成像与卫星视图间天然共享的不变场景结构。

Method: SAMF框架：1) 方向结构增强模块(DSEM)，通过捕捉方向性与低频空间模式强化鲁棒结构表征；2) 模态感知仿射重映射模块(MARM)，对增强特征施加仿射校正以促进跨模态对齐。同时由Thermal-UAV数据集派生出Thermal-UAV-Paired基准用于评测。

Result: 在Boson-nighttime与Thermal-UAV-Paired数据集上，SAMF达到SOTA性能；消融实验验证了DSEM与MARM各自的有效性及其协同互补性。代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 显式利用跨模态稳定结构线索是提升UAV热成像地理定位的有效途径，DSEM与MARM的组合可显著改善跨模态对齐，为GNSS拒止下的无人机自主导航提供支撑。

Abstract: UAV thermal-to-satellite geo-localization localizes UAVs by matching real-time thermal images against georeferenced satellite images, enabling autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied and low-visibility conditions. However, substantial cross-modal disparities in color, brightness and viewpoint hinder conventional appearance-based methods from establishing reliable correspondences. While existing approaches predominantly address global modality shifts, they overlook the invariant scene structures inherently shared between thermal and satellite views. To bridge this gap, we propose SAMF, a Structure-Aware Matching Framework that explicitly exploits stable cross-modal structural cues to improve UAV thermal geolocalization. Specifically, the Directional Structure Enhancement Module (DSEM) strengthens robust structural representations by capturing directional and low-frequency spatial patterns, and the Modality-Aware Affine Remapping Module (MARM) subsequently applies affine correction to these enhanced features, further promoting cross-modal alignment. Additionally, we introduce the Thermal-UAV-Paired benchmark derived from the Thermal-UAV dataset. Extensive experiments on both the Boson-nighttime and Thermal-UAV-Paired datasets show that SAMF achieves state-of-the-art performance. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness and synergistic complementarity of DSEM and MARM. The code and dataset are released at: this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [APEX: Asynchronous Prefix Denoising for Extreme Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12843)
*David Dinkevich,Nisan Chiprut,Yoav HaCohen,Dani Lischinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APEX 提出分层潜空间与按通道组分段的异步去噪，缓解极端潜空间压缩下重建与生成的矛盾，在 ImageNet-512 上以更少 token 超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更少的潜 token 意味着更低的扩散成本，但激进压缩带来"重建-生成两难"：增加通道数可改善重建，却使生成更难。作者希望在 64x/128x 极端压缩下同时保持重建质量与生成质量。

Method: 提出 APEX（Asynchronous Prefix denoising for EXtreme compression）：自编码器将通道前缀按"结构→细节"排序形成分层潜空间；单一共享去噪器在不增加 token 的前提下，对通道组在错开的局部时间步上异步去噪（prefix denoising）。

Result: 在 ImageNet-512 上，APEX 在所有评估的通道数下均优于现有方法（64x 与 128x 压缩）；在匹配训练与引导采样计算量时，每个评估预算下均优于 256-token 模型，且计算量越低增益越大。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通道维度的分层排序与异步去噪可在不增加 token 的情况下解耦重建与生成的冲突，为极端潜空间压缩扩散模型提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Fewer latent tokens promise cheaper diffusion. Yet aggressive latent compression creates a reconstruction-generation dilemma: adding channels improves reconstruction but makes generation harder. We introduce APEX, short for Asynchronous Prefix denoising for EXtreme compression, which pairs a hierarchical latent space with asynchronous denoising over channel groups. Our autoencoder orders channel prefixes from structure to detail, while one shared denoiser generates them at staggered local times without adding tokens. On ImageNet-512, APEX outperforms existing methods at every evaluated channel count under 64x and 128x compression. At matched training and guided sampling compute, it also outperforms a 256-token model at every evaluated budget, with larger gains at lower compute. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [19] [CaptchaAgent: Verifiable Visual Reasoning for CAPTCHA Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12866)
*Shangquan Sun,Ruoyu Chen,Zhirui Liu,Xiang Liu,Chenghao Qian,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaptchaAgent 通过构建显式视觉中间表示（V-IR）、ASCII 符号协议强制确定物体集合与计数、局部证据精化与反事实一致性验证，将交互式 CAPTCHA 求解从端到端动作预测转为结构化推理，在 Open CaptchaWorld 和 Oedipus-data 上显著提升 Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推理型 CAPTCHA 需要细粒度感知、组合关系推理与基于浏览器的落地交互，端到端 VLM 智能体在此场景中很脆弱：漏检一个物体、计数错误或关系混淆会直接传播到最终动作。作者希望用结构化视觉推理替代直接的动作预测，以抑制误差传播。

Method: 提出 CaptchaAgent 结构化视觉推理框架：1) 先构建视觉中间表示 V-IR，显式记录实体、属性及空间/拓扑关系；2) 使用仅含符号的 ASCII 协议，强制解析器提交确定的物体集合与计数；3) 在该全局表示上做局部证据精化，以更高有效分辨率重访模糊区域；4) 反事实一致性验证，检验动作在保持任务语义的变换下是否稳定。

Result: 在 Open CaptchaWorld 上，跨三个专有骨干模型比同骨干的商业浏览器智能体基线提升 Pass@1 21.3–25.8 个百分点；在离线 Oedipus-data 基准上，跨三个匹配骨干比 Oedipus 求解器平均提升 Pass@1 9.2–12.3 个百分点。中间轨迹显示精确计数、连续几何和有序执行仍是主要自动化难点。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示加符号协议、证据精化与一致性验证能有效缓解端到端智能体的误差传播，为设计更具鲁棒性的组合式 CAPTCHA 提供实践指导。该工作本质上属于通用智能体的结构化推理与验证方法，而非物理 AI/机器人核心方向。

Abstract: Modern reasoning CAPTCHAs require fine-grained perception, compositional relation reasoning, and grounded browser interaction. End-to-end vision-language model agents remain brittle in this setting because a missed object, incorrect count, or confused relation can propagate directly to the final action. To address this problem, we present CaptchaAgent, a structured visual reasoning framework for interactive CAPTCHA solving. Instead of predicting an action directly from a screenshot, CaptchaAgent first constructs a visual intermediate representation (V-IR) that explicitly records entities, attributes, and spatial or topological relations. A symbol-only ASCII protocol further requires the parser to commit to a concrete object set and count. Building on this global representation, localized evidence refinement revisits ambiguous regions at higher effective resolution, while counterfactual consistency verification tests whether the resulting action remains stable under task-preserving transformations. Across three proprietary backbones on Open CaptchaWorld, CaptchaAgent improves Pass@1 by 21.3-25.8 percentage points over a commercially available browser agent baseline evaluated with the same backbones. On the separate offline Oedipus-data benchmark, it further improves average Pass@1 by 9.2-12.3 percentage points over the Oedipus solver across three matched backbones. The intermediate traces show that exact counting, continuous geometry, and ordered execution remain major sources of automation difficulty for the evaluated agents, providing practical guidance for designing more resilient compositional CAPTCHAs.

</details>


### [20] [Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI): A Mamba-based Framework for Rotational Fruit Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12868)
*Hao Tian,Jie Hu,Guoqi Shan,Fan Wang,Wenzhuo Zhang,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出低成本轻量级果蔬分拣系统S²FI，用3D卷积编码器+Mamba建模果蔬旋转视频的时空特征，并配套双传送带+弧形轨道硬件与6,460样本数据集，在准确率、效率与实用性间取得最佳权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球果蔬产业中小规模种植者面临采后分拣困境：大型智能分拣系统成本过高，人工分拣则效率低、标准不一致、人力需求大。作者希望填补"昂贵设备"与"低效人工"之间的空白，面向中小种植者提供低成本、高效率、轻量化的深度学习分拣方案。

Method: 提出Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S²FI)架构：3D卷积编码器结合Mamba模块，高效提取时空特征，在果蔬旋转过程中自适应地为信息量大的帧加权，并以线性复杂度建模长程依赖，从而缓解运动模糊与遮挡；硬件采用双传送带与弧形轨道设计，支持家用电源、快速部署与轻量嵌入部署以降低成本；同时构建了基于传送带的果蔬质量视频数据集（6,460样本）。

Result: 在留出测试集上，该方法在准确率、效率与实用性之间取得最佳权衡，性能优于对比基线，实现了面向真实分拣场景的准确率、速度与实用性的良好平衡。

Conclusion: S²FI为中小规模种植者提供了一种低成本、可快速部署的深度学习果蔬分拣方案，验证了选择性时空建模与轻量硬件结合在实际分拣中的可行性，但摘要未给出具体量化指标与代码/部署细节。

Abstract: The global fruit and vegetable industry confronts severe post-harvest sorting challenges for small and medium-scale growers. Large intelligent sorting systems are too costly, while manual sorting suffers from low efficiency, inconsistent standards, and high labor demand. To bridge this gap between expensive equipment and inefficient manual work, this study develops a low-cost, high-efficiency, lightweight fruit sorting system driven by deep learning, suitable for small and medium growers. We propose the Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI) architecture, which combines a 3D convolutional encoder with a Mamba-based this http URL efficiently extracts spatiotemporal features, adaptively weighs informative frames during fruit rotation, and models long-range dependencies with linear complexity, reducing motion blur and occlusion. The hardware uses a dual-conveyor and curved rail design, supporting household power, fast deployment, and lightweight embedded deployment to improve affordability. We also built a conveyor-based fruit quality video dataset with 6,460 samples. Experiments on the held-out test set show that our method achieves the best trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and practicality, outperforming the compared baselines on the test set, achieving an excellent balance of accuracy, speed, and practicality for real-world sorting.

</details>


### [21] [ROSETTA: Rotation Subspace Estimation for Training-free Transmodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12871)
*Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROSETTA：一种免训练校准层，利用EM估计白化特征空间中编码器间的局部旋转，实现跨传感器/跨编码器检索对齐，且查询时无需共观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同传感器或不同编码器独立训练，在特征空间各自施加不同坐标系，缺乏统一参考系；现有方法未完全弥合该差距，且通常需要查询时的共观测数据。

Method: 从理论上论证两个独立编码器特征系统间的相对变换是白化特征空间中的保距旋转，其角速度呈白噪声特性；利用编码器输出间互协方差有效秩低、仅约束少量旋转参数的性质，通过EM估计局部旋转，构成免训练、查询时无需共观测的校准层。

Result: 在三个公开驾驶数据集上验证，覆盖LiDAR、相机、热成像与事件模态，在跨模态、跨设备、跨表示与跨骨干设置下均有效，每查询开销为毫秒级。

Conclusion: 跨编码器特征对齐可由低秩约束下的局部旋转估计高效解决，ROSETTA以免训练方式实现实用对齐，为多模态/多设备检索提供轻量方案。

Abstract: When a query must be matched against a database built with a different sensor or processed by a different encoder, the independently trained encoders impose distinct coordinate systems on the feature space. No canonical reference frame exists to which all encoders can be aligned, and no single training procedure has fully closed this gap. We show that the relative transformation between any two such systems is a spatially preserving rotation in whitened feature space, whose angular velocity exhibits white-noise characteristics: large in norm, temporally uncorrelated, and unpredictable from the features themselves. However, the cross-covariance between encoder outputs has low effective rank, constraining only a small fraction of the rotation parameters, therefore local alignment is tractable. ROSETTA is a training-free calibration layer that estimates the local rotation via an expectation-maximization procedure, requiring no coobservation at query time. We validate ROSETTA on three public driving datasets spanning LiDAR, camera, thermal, and event modalities in cross-modal, cross-device, cross-representation, and cross-backbone settings, with milliseconds overhead per query.

</details>


### [22] [Win-Win Fusion: Multi-Modal Image Fusion with Visual-Semantic Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12887)
*Hao Zhang,Xuhui Zuo,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出W2F，一种面向多模态图像融合(MMIF)的视觉-语义互增强范式，通过分割引导融合损失、嵌入信息恢复先验、交互门控混合注意力(IGM-Att)与渐进耦合注意力(PC-Att)，以及耦合视觉与语义融合的协作机制(特征搜索+梯度校正)，缩小视觉与语义域的优化鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMIF方法在视觉域与语义域之间存在优化鸿沟，导致感知能力提升受限，视觉融合结果难以有效支撑下游语义任务。

Method: 三项设计：1) 特征互补增强——分割引导融合损失为视觉融合引入目标感知结构引导，嵌入信息恢复先验提升退化条件下视觉融合为语义分支提供可靠感知证据的能力；2) 多模态信息交互机制——IGM-Att与PC-Att从局部与全局视角保障场景表征完整性；3) 视觉-语义耦合协作机制——特征搜索模块提取两任务互补关键特征，梯度校正策略从优化层面通过一致性学习促进双方性能提升。

Result: 大量实验表明W2F在视觉观感与决策精度两方面均超越现有最先进方法，代码已公开。

Conclusion: W2F验证了视觉-语义互增强范式在MMIF中的有效性，为融合任务的感知与决策一致性提供了可复用框架。

Abstract: Existing multi-modal image fusion (MMIF) methods generally face an optimization gap between the visual and semantic domains, limiting the enhancement of perception capabilities. To overcome this long-standing barrier, this work proposes W2F, a new visual-semantic mutual reinforcing paradigm for MMIF. First, we reinforce the foundation for visual-semantic collaboration by amplifying feature complementarity through two targeted designs. Specifically, the segmentation-guided fusion loss introduces object-aware structural guidance into visual fusion, while the embedded information restoration prior strengthens the ability of visual fusion to provide reliable perceptual evidence for the semantic branch under degraded conditions. Then, we introduce a strong multi-modal information interaction mechanism composed of interactive gated mixed attention (IGM-Att) and progressive coupling attention (PC-Att). These modules ensure comprehensive scene representation from both local and global perspectives, providing informational support for visual-semantic collaboration. Finally, we construct a collaborative mechanism to couple visual fusion and semantic fusion. It employs a feature search module to extract complementary key features from both tasks, enriching the representational potential of visual and semantic information. Meanwhile, a gradient correction strategy ensures collaboration from the optimization perspective, enhancing performance for both visual and semantic fusion through consistency learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that W2F surpasses state-of-the-art methods in both visual appeal and decision accuracy. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [MeshBind: Point-to-Mesh Binding for Non-Rigid Scene Flow Automatic Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12895)
*Yinqi Chen,E Li,Shuaijun Wang,Weiyuan Peng,Meiying Zhang,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshBind 用多视角重建的铰接网格模型自动生成非刚体（行人）逐点场景流标注，并发布 SFB 基准；微调后三向 EPE 降低 2-3 倍，非刚体 EPE 追平刚体水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶与机器人依赖场景流估计，但行人等非刚体目标的真值场景流极难获取，现有标注手段难以提供逐点非刚体监督。

Method: 三部分：1) 从六路环视相机重建铰接网格并配准到 LiDAR 坐标系；2) 局部坐标系绑定，用逐三角面切向-法向框架把点绑定到网格，对刚体运动不变、对铰接形变自适应；3) 三层流配准管线（自车运动补偿、box binding、mesh binding）生成全场景逐点流。配套 SF-Studio 交互标注工具与 SFB 基准（41k 帧，Rigid/Semi-Rigid/Non-Rigid 评测协议）。

Result: 在 SFB 上微调使三向 EPE 下降 2-3 倍，非刚体 EPE 降至与刚体同等水平。

Conclusion: 基于网格的自动标注可有效解决非刚体场景流真值缺失问题，兼具工具与基准价值。

Abstract: Scene flow estimation is essential for many autonomous driving (AD) and robotic applications, but obtaining ground-truth scene flow for non-rigid objects such as pedestrians is highly difficult. We present MeshBind, an automatic annotation method that uses mesh models to generate per-point scene flow for non-rigid objects. The novelty of this work is threefold: 1) developing a multi-view module that reconstructs an articulated mesh model from six surround cameras and registers it to the LiDAR frame; 2) developing a local-frame binding technique that attaches each point to the mesh via a per-triangle tangent-normal frame, invariant under rigid motion yet adaptive to articulated deformation; 3) developing a three-layer flow registration pipeline that combines ego-vehicle motion compensation, box binding, and mesh binding to produce full-scene per-point flow. We implement MeshBind in SF-Studio, an interactive annotation tool that visualises flow and allows human mesh corrections with automatic recomputation. We also release SFB (SUScape-Flow Benchmark), a large-scale benchmark built on the public SUScape dataset, covering 41k frames with per-point non-rigid pedestrian annotations and a Rigid/Semi-Rigid/Non-Rigid evaluation protocol. Experiments show that fine-tuning on SFB reduces three-way endpoint error (EPE) by 2-3x, and brings non-rigid EPE to the same level as rigid EPE.

</details>


### [24] [AD-SRF: Attack-Defense Semantic Reinforcement for Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12897)
*Hao Zhang,Zhiqian Zheng,Linfeng Tang,Xinyu Xiang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出攻击-防御语义强化框架AD-SRF，解耦视觉质量与语义属性的优化再无损重组，在多模态图像融合中同时提升视觉效果与下游分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图像融合方法中，视觉质量与语义属性的优化目标存在内在冲突，难以同时兼顾；作者希望对二者解耦优化后再无损重组，从而在不牺牲视觉质量的前提下注入丰富语义。

Method: 框架分两部分：(1) 基于Transformer的复原式视觉融合网络，作为强视觉基线，配备跨模态检索模块与信息复原-融合协同损失，在退化条件下仍生成高可见度融合图像；(2) 引入攻防博弈机制增强语义属性——攻击者学习可损害下游分割的扰动模式，防御者从原始输入中恢复缺失语义并通过不可感知的语义图重新融入融合图像，迭代对抗使语义属性不断增强。

Result: 大量实验表明，AD-SRF在视觉质量与语义属性两方面取得双赢（win-win）表现，下游分割性能提升且不损害视觉质量；代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过解耦-重组与攻防博弈，可在多模态图像融合中统一视觉质量与语义属性，为融合图像服务于下游感知任务提供新思路。

Abstract: Existing image fusion methods struggle to balance visual quality and semantic attributes due to the inherent conflict between their optimization objectives. To address this challenge, we propose an attack-defense semantic reinforcement framework for multi-modal image fusion, termed AD-SRF. By decoupling the optimization of these two aspects and then losslessly reintegrating them, this framework injects rich semantic attributes into the fused image without compromising its visual quality. First, we develop a transformer-based restorative visual fusion network as a strong visual baseline. Equipped with the cross-modal retrieval module and the information restoration-fusion collaborative loss, it produces highly visible fused images even under degraded conditions. Second, we introduce an attack-defense game mechanism to strengthen the semantic attributes of the fused images. The attacker learns perturbation patterns that impair downstream segmentation, whereas the defender restores missing semantics from the original inputs and reintegrates them into the fused image through an imperceptible semantic map. Through iterative adversarial interaction, this mechanism drives the defender to continually strengthen the fused image's semantic attributes, boosting downstream segmentation performance without compromising visual quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AD-SRF achieves a win-win performance in both visual quality and semantic attributes. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [A Lightweight Velocity Correction Framework for Improving Instruction Following in Few-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12899)
*Yating Gao,Zhenlong Zhu,Wen Zihao,Lenan Zhang,Jiexiong Wang,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang,Yixiong Zou,Takuya Maekawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级速度残差修正框架，在完全冻结的6B少步文生图生成器上仅训练<20M参数修正器，保持原采样计划与前向次数不变，显著提升复杂提示的指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步文生图生成器虽能少量前向得到高质量图像，但在多主体、交互、空间关系、计数与属性绑定等复杂提示上易出错。作者希望在不改变生成器参数、不增加前向次数、不做奖励优化或重蒸馏的前提下降低这些错误。

Method: 在冻结生成器上添加<20M参数修正器，学习选定采样步上的速度残差调整；单个修正器跨步共享并与生成器并行运行。核心是On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision：由于早期修正会改变后续步的状态，该方法在实际到达的状态上推导残差目标，仅训练修正器。

Result: 在目标复杂场景分布上，与冻结的Z-Image-Turbo相比，成对指令遵循评估取得+17.60个百分点NetWin，端到端延迟仅增加26.9 ms（0.84%），主干模型评估次数不增加。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了对完全冻结少步文生图生成器的轻量级速度残差适配，同时保持原始采样计划与前向次数，以极低延迟代价显著改善复杂提示的指令遵循。

Abstract: Few-step text-to-image generators produce high-quality images with few network forward passes, yet struggle with complex prompts involving multiple subjects, interactions, spatial relations, counting, and attribute binding. Can these errors be reduced without changing the generator's parameters or increasing its forward-pass count? We propose a lightweight correction framework for a frozen 6B-parameter generator. The generator predicts velocities that determine how its intermediate image representation is updated; a corrector with fewer than 20M parameters learns residual adjustments at selected sampling steps. Because earlier corrections change the states encountered at later steps, our On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision derives residual targets at the states actually reached during corrected generation. A single corrector is shared across selected steps and runs in parallel with the generator. Only the corrector is trained, requiring neither reward optimization nor re-distillation. To our knowledge, this is the first framework for lightweight velocity-residual adaptation of fully frozen few-step text-to-image generators that preserves their original sampling schedule and forward-pass count. On our target complex-scene distribution, the framework achieves a +17.60-percentage-point NetWin over frozen Z-Image-Turbo in pairwise instruction-following evaluation, with only 26.9 ms (0.84%) additional end-to-end latency per image and no increase in backbone evaluations.

</details>


### [26] [JMT-YOLO: Multi-task Domain Adaptation via Segmentation-Guided Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12901)
*Zhenhua Yu,Jiahao Zhang,Wenchao Zhang,Ou Ye,Hengxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JMT-YOLO：面向域自适应目标检测的联合多任务框架，在Mean Teacher上引入辅助语义分割与掩码一致性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAOD在源-目标分布偏移下性能下降，现有方法细粒度特征建模不足、实例级对齐有限，在极端天气和遮挡等复杂环境下效果差。

Method: 基于Mean Teacher架构，加入辅助语义分割任务与掩码机制；设计多尺度上下文感知分割头（密集连接结构）捕获局部与全局上下文，缓解细粒度特征退化；对目标域图像施加随机掩码并构造掩码一致性损失，引导结构上下文学习，增强实例级特征对齐。

Result: 在Pascal VOC→Clipart1k、Cityscapes→Foggy Cityscapes、KITTI→Cityscapes上分别取得46.9%、57.3%、61.0%的检测性能，优于现有SOTA与基线。

Conclusion: 联合多任务学习（分割+掩码一致性）可有效提升复杂环境下的域自适应目标检测性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) aims to reduce performance degradation due to distribution shifts between training (source) and testing (target) datasets. However, owing to insufficient modeling of fine-grained features and limited instance-level alignment, existing approaches become less effective under complex environmental conditions, such as extreme weather conditions and object occlusion. We overcome these challenges by proposing JMT-YOLO, a novel joint multi-task learning framework for DAOD. JMT-YOLO is based on the Mean Teacher architecture. It introduces an auxiliary semantic segmentation task and a masking mechanism to enhance the modeling of foreground-background semantic boundaries in the target domain. Specifically, fine-grained feature degradation is reduced by introducing a multiscale context-aware segmentation head equipped with a dense connection structure to effectively capture local and global contextual features. A mask-consistency loss is formulated by applying random masking operations to target images, thereby guiding the network to learn structural context and improving instance-level feature alignment. Extensive experiments on three benchmark domain adaptation tasks (Pascal VOC to Clipart1k, Cityscapes to Foggy Cityscapes, and KITTI to Cityscapes) demonstrate that JMT-YOLO achieves detection performances of 46.9%, 57.3%, and 61.0%, respectively. These results demonstrate superior performance over existing state-of-the-art and baseline methods, thereby validating the effectiveness and generalization of JMT-YOLO.

</details>


### [27] [BLEND: Balancing Personalization vs. Generalization in Federated Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12907)
*Payam Abdisarabshali,Fardis Nadimi,Kasra Borazjani,Naji Khosravan,Richeng Jin,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLEND 是一个联邦视觉语言模型（FedVLM）微调框架，通过"全局共享适配器 + 客户端个性化投影适配器"的参数选择与聚合策略，以及融合全局/个性化输出的损失函数与零样本锚点正则，同时提升个性化与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调 VLM 时，模型既要在各客户端数据分布上实现个性化，又要在未见分布/任务上保持泛化，二者存在此消彼长的张力；现有 FedVLM 方法难以兼顾，因此需要一种能在参数层面与损失层面同时平衡这两种目标的设计。

Method: 提出 BLEND 框架：(i) 参数划分上，跨客户端共享全局视觉/文本适配器，同时为每个客户端保留个性化视觉投影适配器；(ii) 聚合上采用选择性微调与选择性聚合，仅对共享部分做联邦平均；(iii) 损失上融合全局适配器与个性化适配器的输出，并以零样本 VLM 投影头作为有原则的锚点进行正则，抑制个性化带来的泛化退化。

Result: 在多种系统设置（不同数据异构度、客户端数量/参与度等）下，BLEND 一致优于现有最先进基线，表明其在个性化与泛化之间取得了更好的折衷；论文提供了开源代码。

Conclusion: BLEND 说明在联邦 VLM 微调中，通过分离"共享参数"与"个性化参数"并配合锚点式损失设计，可有效缓解个性化-泛化权衡，为 FedVLM 提供了一种通用且可扩展的解决范式。

Abstract: Federated vision-language models (FedVLMs) represent one of the emerging frontiers of machine learning, aiming to integrate federated learning within the fine-tuning pipelines of vision-language models (VLMs). In this work, we introduce BLEND, a new framework designed to jointly enhance the personalization and generalization capabilities of FedVLMs. BLEND proposes a selective VLM parameter fine-tuning and aggregation strategy that consists of (i) global vision and text adapters shared across clients, and (ii) a personalized vision projection adapter tailored to each client. BLEND further introduces a novel loss function that blends the outputs of the global and personalized adapters to achieve personalization, while simultaneously promoting generalization through a principled anchor design implemented via the projection heads of the zero-shot VLM. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BLEND consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting its ability to effectively balance personalization and generalization across diverse system settings. (Code is available at: this https URL )

</details>


### [28] [What Egocentric Video Embeddings Encode and How to Combine Them with Inertial Sensors for Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12926)
*Kansuke Habano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 分析第一视角视频嵌入与惯性传感器信号的互补关系，提出在去除主导方向后的视频图上传播惯性预测，将跨被试宏F1从0.648提升至0.852。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频与可穿戴惯性传感器常被同时采集，视频通常用预训练模型编码，但这些嵌入在头戴视角下究竟表示什么、以及与惯性信号如何结合，尚缺乏研究。

Method: 在VideoMAEv2、CLIP、DINOv2与第一视角VC-1上分析嵌入主方向，结合Ego-Exo4D、WEAR数据用头部朝向与场景布局做回归解释；随后去除时间顺序、每1秒片段随机取一肢体惯性信号，在去除主导成分的被试内视频图上传播惯性预测，并与单分类器融合、加入惯性边等方案对比。

Result: 嵌入主方向由相机朝向主导，头部朝向在Ego-Exo4D第一视角可解释R^2 0.49-0.70，非第一视角不成立，WEAR由场景布局解释；惯性可预测的部分能区分不同人的活动，集合级探针只能区分同一被试内不同训练组。视频图传播使被试间交叉验证宏F1从0.648升至0.852，优于模态融合与惯性边方案。

Conclusion: 视频嵌入与惯性信号承担分工角色：视频负责聚类同一组片段，惯性分类器负责打标签，二者在去除视频主导方向后的图传播中结合效果最佳。

Abstract: Egocentric video and body-worn inertial sensors are increasingly recorded together, and the video is usually encoded by pretrained models. What these embeddings represent when the camera sits on the head, and how they should be combined with inertial sensing, has received little attention. Across VideoMAEv2, CLIP, DINOv2 and the egocentric VC-1, the leading principal directions of the embedding are dominated by where the camera looks. Measured head orientation explains them on Ego-Exo4D for egocentric (R^2 0.49-0.70) but not exocentric cameras, and the scene layout explains them on WEAR. The part that the limbs' inertial signals predict separates activities across people. In a set-level probe, the rest separates exercise sets within a participant but not activities across people. This is consistent with separate roles: the video finds the clips of the same set, and the inertial classifier labels them. On WEAR, with temporal order removed and one random limb per one-second clip, we propagate inertial predictions over a within-participant video graph whose leading components are removed. This raises subject-wise cross-validated macro-F1 from 0.648 to 0.852. In our comparisons, propagation helps only over the video graph, and fusing the modalities in one classifier, or adding inertial edges to the graph, performs worse.

</details>


### [29] [UniDeblur: Unified Motion Deblurring via Fourier Convolution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12941)
*Xintian Mao,Jiansheng Wang,Huaiyuan Wang,Qingli Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建多源去模糊数据集与数据清洗/质量控制流程，并提出基于傅里叶卷积学习的统一运动去模糊框架，在频域进行模糊自适应全局滤波以摆脱空间域核估计限制，在域内与OOD基准上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去模糊方法大多针对单一场景特化，面对不同相机与图像内容时性能显著下降，难以泛化到多样的模糊模式，因此需要一个能处理跨场景、跨运动的统一去模糊框架。

Method: 1) 构建多源数据集，并设计数据剪枝与质量控制流程；2) 提出统一去模糊框架，基于傅里叶卷积学习，在傅里叶空间进行模糊自适应的全局滤波，取代依赖像素级空间映射与有限空间支撑的核估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，该统一模型能有效处理多样来源与场景的运动模糊，在域内与OOD基准上均达到有竞争力的性能。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 通过频域全局滤波学习模糊的卷积本质，可为跨场景统一运动去模糊提供有效路径，具备良好的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Image deblurring is a fundamental task in computer vision, yet existing methods mainly specialize in isolated scenarios. Consequently, the deblurring performance of these models drops significantly across scenarios with different cameras and image contents. This motivates the development of a unified motion deblurring framework that can handle diverse blur patterns. To this end, we construct a multi-source dataset and develop a data pruning and quality control pipeline. Then, to restore blur images with various motion blur kernels across different scenarios, we propose a unified motion deblurring framework via Fourier Convolution Learning. Existing deblurring networks usually rely on pixel-level mappings and overlook the convolutional nature of blur. Thus, the network mostly fits the image content itself rather than learning the convolution process, making it difficult to handle multi-source blur images. Instead of relying on spatial mapping, we perform blur-adaptive global filter learning in Fourier space, which avoids the limited spatial support of kernel estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our unified model effectively handles motion blurs across diverse sources and scenes, achieving competitive performance across both in-domain and OOD benchmarks. Code will be released at this http URL .

</details>


### [30] [In-Context Learning Can Help Vision-Language Models Overcome Training Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12942)
*Kun Wang,Xindi Wu,Sanghyuk Chun,Olga Russakovsky,Esin Tureci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过"受控视觉上下文学习"（提供示例/demonstrations）让VLM聚焦视觉证据，发现这足以纠正常识先验导致的错误，说明模型本身具备被忽视的视觉接地能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM训练时学到强统计规律，当输入图像违反这些规律时模型会忽视视觉证据而犯错；难以区分这类"先验诱导错误"与"模型缺乏视觉能力"导致的错误，因此需要受控实验来解耦两者。

Method: 采用受控视觉in-context learning：在五个以视觉为中心的任务中，对"与先验冲突的反事实样本"和"与先验一致的样本"分别提供demonstrations，比较准确率变化；跨五个VLM家族验证泛化性；并分析视觉表征，考察attention是否转向grounding线索、减少对先验线索的依赖；同时测试底层视觉能力薄弱时的表现。

Result: 反事实样本上大幅提升（Qwen3-VL 235B +27.2%，32B +15.1%），而先验一致样本准确率几乎不变；五个VLM家族增益在+9.7%~+30.3%；表征分析显示demonstrations确实将attention重定向至grounding线索；当底层视觉能力弱或缺失时，in-context learning效果受限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM实际具备标准评测可能忽略的视觉接地能力，先验冲突错误多属"未聚焦视觉证据"而非"能力缺失"；诊断与改进模型能力需要受控的评测设计。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) learn strong statistical regularities during training, which can make them fail to properly respond to visual evidence when input images violate those regularities. It is challenging to disambiguate these prior-induced errors from errors caused by the model missing the underlying visual capabilities. In this work, we use controlled visual in-context learning to encourage the model to focus on the visual evidence, and demonstrate that this alone is frequently sufficient to correct the errors -- suggesting that the models in fact do possess the relevant visual capabilities. Specifically, we show that across five vision-centric tasks, demonstrations yield large gains on prior-conflicting counterfactual examples ($+27.2\%$ for Qwen3-VL 235B and $+15.1\%$ for Qwen3-VL 32B) while leaving accuracy on prior-aligned examples nearly unchanged. These findings generalize across five VLM families with gains from $+9.7\%$ to $+30.3\%$. We analyze visual representations and find that demonstrations indeed redirect attention toward grounding cues while reducing reliance on cues that support the learned prior. Finally, we find that in-context learning is limited when the underlying visual capability is weak or absent. Together, these results show that current VLMs possess visual grounding capabilities that standard evaluations may overlook, underscoring the need for controlled evaluation design in diagnosing and improving model capability.

</details>


### [31] [Video Codec-Guided Training-Free Token Compression for Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12959)
*Xihua Sheng,Ruijie Liu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用视频编解码信息（参考帧依赖、运动矢量、残差）作为低成本先验，引导VLLM的免训练视觉token压缩，在保持性能的同时大幅减少特征比较次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型依赖长视觉token序列理解时序内容，推理开销大；现有免训练压缩方法虽减少了送入LLM的token数，但压缩过程本身的视觉特征比较代价仍然高昂（帧内token保护与帧间时序合并）。

Method: 将压缩视频中已有的编码信息（参考帧依赖、运动矢量、残差）对齐到视觉token网格：残差生成紧凑候选池用于token保护；运动信息沿参考帧链传播，为稀疏采样帧间构建局部候选用于时序token合并；仅在候选内做局部视觉特征相似度比较以完成最终压缩决策，不修改基座VLLM。

Result: 在3个代表性VLLM和4个视频理解基准上，性能与SOTA免训练基线FlashVID相当，部分设置更优，同时显著降低了token保护与时序匹配所需的特征比较次数。

Conclusion: 编解码信息能有效改善视频token压缩的精度-复杂度权衡，为免训练VLLM推理加速提供低成本先验。

Abstract: Video large language models (VLLMs) rely on long visual token sequences to understand complex temporal content, which makes visual token compression important for efficient inference. Existing training-free compression methods mainly reduce the number of tokens sent to the language model, but the compression process itself can still require many visual feature comparisons, especially for intra-frame token protection and inter-frame temporal token merging. This paper proposes a video codec-guided training-free token compression method for VLLMs that uses coding information already available in compressed video as a low-cost search prior. The method aligns reference-frame dependencies, motion vectors, and residuals with the visual token grid. Residual information forms compact candidate pools for token protection, while motion information is propagated along reference-frame chains to construct localized candidates for temporal token merging between sparsely sampled frames. Local visual feature similarity comparison is then used within these candidates to make the final compression decisions, without modifying the base VLLM. Experiments on three representative VLLMs and four video-understanding benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art training-free baseline FlashVID, and in some settings surpasses it, while substantially reducing the feature comparisons required by token protection and temporal matching. These results demonstrate that codec information can effectively improve the accuracy-complexity tradeoff of video token compression.

</details>


### [32] [MVGraph: Benchmarking LVLMs on Evidence Composition across Multi-View Visual Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12961)
*Yingjie Zhu,Han Cheng,Xuefeng Bai,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVGraph：面向多图联合推理的LVLM基准，8k题+29项诊断任务（64k实例），揭示跨视图边对齐与关系级组合为瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的图推理证据常分散在多个来源图、时序快照或局部观测中，而现有图推理基准仅限单一自足结构，只考察图感知与推理，忽略了跨视图证据的对齐与组合。因此需要一个多视图、单视图不可解的多图推理基准，并诊断各阶段能力瓶颈。

Method: 构建MVGraph基准：8k实例、覆盖8类图论任务，抽象自"部分视图"与"时序视图"两种多图场景；每题程序化构造并验证单视图不可解。将多图推理分解为感知、对齐、组合、推理四项能力，设计29项诊断任务共64k实例，并提供黄金中间表示以评估中间过程。在15个LVLM上进行端到端与阶段导向评测。

Result: 15个LVLM整体表现暴露出多图推理难度；阶段诊断显示跨视图边对齐与关系级组合是主要瓶颈；时序约束与视图数量增加进一步暴露能力边界。

Conclusion: MVGraph提供了多图推理的多能力、可诊断评测框架，指明跨视图对齐与关系级组合为关键短板，并公开代码与数据。

Abstract: We introduce MVGraph, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models (LVLMs) on multi-graph reasoning where the evidence required by a query is distributed across multiple interrelated visual graph views. Although such evidence commonly resides in source-specific graphs, temporal snapshots, or local observations, existing graph reasoning benchmarks remain confined to a single self-contained structure, assessing only graph perception and reasoning while leaving the alignment and composition of evidence across views underexplored. MVGraph comprises 8k instances spanning 8 \textit{graph-theoretic} tasks, abstracted from two multi-graph scenarios (partial and temporal graph views). Each problem is programmatically constructed and verified to be unsolvable from a single view. Beyond end-to-end evaluation, we decompose multi-graph reasoning into four complementary capabilities---Perception, Alignment, Composition, and Reasoning---and design 29 diagnostic tasks with 64k instances to probe each one, along with golden intermediate representations for evaluating the intermediate process. Across 15 LVLMs, we reveal the difficulty of multi-graph reasoning and identify cross-view edge alignment and relation-level composition as prominent bottlenecks through stage-oriented diagnostics. Meanwhile, temporal constraints and increasing view counts expose further capability boundaries. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [33] [MotionCoach: Constructing Multimodal Guidance for Video Professional Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12963)
*Siyi Qian,Hanqing Yang,Weihuang Lin,Zihao Yang,Yichen Lu,Yuanhong Zheng,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionCoach：一个带多轮工具调用与自验证的多模态智能体，为视频编辑模型生成"接地提示词+参考片段"的双重引导，实现专业运动（如Stoppie）的精细编辑，无需重训视频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业运动编辑需要细粒度技巧与时间结构知识，但用户仅给出运动名称而缺少详细描述，现有视频编辑模型常生成错误动作（如把Stoppie的前轮也抬起）。核心动机是把外部专业知识以可靠多模态引导的形式注入冻结的视频编辑模型。

Method: 构建数据管线生成轨迹，冷启动SFT于MotionCoach-SFT-10k，再用GRPO在MotionCoach-RL-2k上做强化学习，奖励涵盖引导提示词、动作结果视频与输出格式；智能体多轮调用工具并自验证，产出接地提示词与参考片段。

Result: 在Seedance 2.0上总分由55.1提升至65.6，MiniMax-H3上由51.2提升至64.5；并发布VPMEBench，含314个专业运动编辑任务及细粒度参考与标注。

Conclusion: 学习构造多模态引导即可实现精确专业运动编辑，避免对视频编辑模型进行重训练，验证了智能体式引导作为外接专业知识接口的有效性。

Abstract: Video editing models achieve strong performance in modifying appearance, backgrounds, styles, and motions, empowered by large-scale training data. Beyond general scenarios, professional motions are significant in performance, sports, and martial arts. This paper focuses on professional motion editing, which involves fine-grained motion technique and temporal structure. Given professional motion names but lacking detailed descriptions, existing models often fail to generate professional motions, such as incorrectly lifting the front wheel under a Stoppie instruction. To address this challenge, we introduce MotionCoach, a trained multimodal agent that uses multi-turn tools and performs self-verification, producing a grounded prompt and a reference clip as reliable and sufficient multimodal guidance to inject external professional motion knowledge into video editing models. To supply training data, we build a data pipeline to construct trajectories. We cold-start the agent on MotionCoach-SFT-10k and apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on MotionCoach-RL-2k with rewards on the motion guidance prompt, the motion outcome video, and the format. For evaluation, we introduce VPMEBench, comprising 314 professional motion editing tasks with fine-grained references and annotations. Extensive experiments show that MotionCoach improves the overall score from 55.1 to 65.6 on Seedance 2.0 and from 51.2 to 64.5 on MiniMax-H3. Learning to construct multimodal guidance enables precise professional motion editing without retraining video editing models.

</details>


### [34] [Learning Representations to Guide Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12978)
*Zihao Pan,Mengting Chen,Cheng Shi,Taihang Hu,Zhengrong Yue,Zikang Wang,Yuan Wang,Wei Li,Sibei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究像素扩散Transformer中“何为好的生成表征”，指出表征质量高不等于生成效用高，并提出Representation Guidance（RG）：用低维瓶颈迫使生成相关信息形成紧凑内部表征，并通过修改flow-matching目标显式引导解码生成，在ImageNet 256x256上以459M参数RG-L/16超越更大模型，RG-XL/16达到FID 1.55，512x512达1.74，并扩展到文生图（GenEval 0.89、DPG-Bench 87.1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉表征主要为“理解”而设计，何种表征有利于“生成”尚不明确；作者发现外部对齐的语义在像素合成中会逐步丢失，且架构解耦加专用像素解码器只是重新分配语义与空间建模，未必得到更适合生成的表征。核心洞察是：有用的生成表征必须既被正确形成，又被主动利用。

Method: 在像素扩散Transformer中引入Representation Guidance：保持朴素Transformer架构，通过低维瓶颈将生成相关信息压缩为紧凑内部表征，再根据该表征诱导的预测偏移修改flow-matching目标，从而在解码阶段显式利用该表征进行引导。

Result: ImageNet 256x256上，仅459M参数的RG-L/16显著优于更大的像素扩散Transformer，RG-XL/16达到FID 1.55；512x512下FID 1.74；扩展至文生图任务，GenEval得0.89、DPG-Bench得87.1，且在参数和计算上高效扩展。

Conclusion: 生成表征的价值取决于其是否被正确形成并主动利用；RG通过紧凑瓶颈表征与显式引导，兼顾语义/空间结构学习与高效扩展，为像素扩散Transformer提供了优于单纯提升表征质量或架构解耦的生成范式。

Abstract: Visual representations are largely developed for understanding, yet what makes them useful for generation remains unclear. We study this question in pixel diffusion transformers and uncover two key insights. First, better representation quality does not necessarily translate into greater generative utility, as externally aligned semantics can be progressively lost during pixel synthesis. Second, architectural separation with specialized pixel decoders mainly redistributes semantic and spatial modeling, without necessarily yielding representations better suited to generation. These insights suggest that a useful generative representation must be both properly formed and actively exploited. Motivated by these insights, we introduce Representation Guidance (RG), which directly forms and exploits internal representations while retaining a plain Transformer architecture. A low-dimensional bottleneck forces generation-relevant information into a compact representation, which is then explicitly used to guide decoder generation by modifying the flow-matching target according to its induced prediction offset. RG learns clearer semantic and spatial structure while scaling efficiently in both parameters and computation. On ImageNet 256x256, RG-L/16 with only 459M parameters outperforms substantially larger pixel diffusion transformers, while RG-XL/16 achieves an FID of 1.55. RG further reaches an FID of 1.74 at 512x512 and extends to text-to-image generation, achieving 0.89 on GenEval and 87.1 on DPG-Bench.

</details>


### [35] [The Evidence Anchoring Boundary: Where Visual Interventions Matter and When They Help in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12981)
*Qiji Zhou,Junyi Hu,Peiyu Wang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作研究多模态大模型中视觉干预（视觉残差编辑）在解码器各层的有效性，提出"证据锚定边界"(EAB)，并设计校准式选择性路由以按问题类型决定是否干预，从而减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉干预是无训练地减少MLLM幻觉的手段，但某些层干预杠杆很小，且即使是改变了输出的干预也可能破坏原本正确的答案，因此需要弄清干预在何处仍有杠杆、何时才真正改善预测。

Method: 1) 在连续解码器层施加相同的视觉残差编辑，并按深度匹配其RMS强度，跨3个模型族共6个检查点观察干预效应的衰减，定义其操作性起点EAB；2) 比较存在性、属性、关系三类问题上的反事实减法效果；3) 基于上述观察提出校准式选择性路由：除非校准结果支持对该问题类型干预，否则保留原始预测。

Result: 校准查询上确定的边界，在不相交评测查询上EAB之前的留出效应是EAB之后的13.78×–26.10×；中间读出对比显示编辑可在答案偏好稳定之前就失去杠杆。选择性路由在三个检查点上把全集属性准确率提升1.56–1.81个百分点，而通用减法为中性或有害。

Conclusion: 视觉干预的效力随解码深度衰减存在明确边界（EAB），且不同问题类型对反事实减法反应不同；按问题类型进行校准式选择性路由可在避免破坏正确预测的同时稳定减少幻觉。

Abstract: Visual interventions provide a training-free way to reduce hallucinations in multimodal large language models. However, an intervention may have little leverage at some layers and may damage a correct answer even when it changes the output. We investigate where visual interventions retain leverage and when they improve predictions. First, we apply the same visual-residual edit at successive decoder layers, matching its RMS strength across depth. Across six checkpoints from three model families, we observe a persistent drop in intervention effect and define its operational onset for this edit as the \textbf{Evidence Anchoring Boundary (EAB)}. With boundaries fixed on calibration queries, held-out effects on disjoint evaluation queries before EAB are $13.78\times$--$26.10\times$ larger than those after it. Comparison with intermediate readouts shows that the edit can lose leverage before answer preferences stabilize. Second, we compare counterfactual subtraction across presence, attribute, and relation questions. Subtraction can correct a misleading preference, leave a decision unchanged, or remove a preference supporting the correct answer.
