However, existing methods provide limited control over material behavior beyond appearance-based inference and struggle to estimate physical properties from complex interactions between objects and semantic parts. Therefore, we present PhysForge, a Video Diffusion Model (VDM)-based framework for addressing these challenges, comprising two complementary modules for material-controllable physical fitting and componentlevel interaction modeling. The first, PhysFit, leverages material-conditioned VDM dynamics as references for physical-property fitting, introducing windowed fitting to focus on material-revealing interactions and Hallucinated Forcing to disentangle nonphysical motion hallucinated by the VDM from material responses. The second, PhysComp decomposes complex interactions into individual objects and semantic components, enabling their physical parameters to be optimized independently and flexibly composed for joint simulation. Experiments on PhysAssets and Google Scanned Objects demonstrate that, compared with existing methods, PhysForge provides stronger control over material-dependent physical responses while supporting component-level physical modeling for complex interactions. It achieves the best CLIP and physical controllability scores on both common and uncommon materials, with qualitative comparisons, component-level ablations, and a user study further supporting these improvements. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [102] [X-AVDD: Cross-Attentive Audio-Visual Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13687)
*Saumyaranjan Mohanty,Aravind Reddy,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 X-AVDD，一种基于 cross-attention 的音视频数据集蒸馏方法，通过解耦跨模态对齐与联合优化显著降低显存开销（端到端合成成本降低约 8 倍 GPU-hours），并在 VGGSound、AVE 等数据集上大幅提升小样本合成数据的训练效用与跨架构泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频数据集蒸馏方法（如 DM、AVDD）存在两大瓶颈：1）显存占用高，难以扩展到复杂架构与更高的 SPC 设置；2）难以保持强的跨模态语义对应关系。作者希望在保持训练效用的同时，让合成数据集既内存友好又具备良好跨模态对齐。

Method: 提出 X-AVDD：一种基于 cross-attention 的蒸馏方法，在合成过程中显式建模模态间关系；核心设计是将跨模态对齐从联合优化中解耦出来，从而在提升内存效率的同时产生高对齐度的合成数据。

Result: 在 VGGSound 上 SPC=10 时 top-1 从 8.2% 提升至 24.0%，SPC=20 时从 9.8% 提升至 26.5%；在 AVE 上 SPC=50 时对 ViT 目标的跨架构泛化从 25.5% 提升至 35.3%；可扩展至 AudioCLIP 等复杂架构，仅用 5.65% 数据达到 62.3% 精度（全数据为 72.4%），训练时间比全数据少 9 倍。

Conclusion: X-AVDD 通过 cross-attention 与对齐解耦，同时解决了音视频数据集蒸馏的显存瓶颈与跨模态语义保持问题，在合成效率、测试精度和跨架构泛化上均优于现有方法，并具备向复杂模型扩展的能力。

Abstract: Audio-visual dataset distillation aims to synthesize a small dataset from a large paired audio-visual dataset, while nearly preserving the training utility for neural networks. Existing approaches such as DM and AVDD face two critical limitations: 1) they are memory-intensive, making them difficult to scale to complex architectures and higher samples-per-class (SPC) settings, and 2) they often fail to preserve strong cross-modal semantic correspondence. To tackle these issues, we propose X-AVDD, a cross-attention-based distillation method that explicitly captures inter-modal relationships during synthesis. By decoupling cross-modal alignment from joint optimization, X-AVDD is substantially more memory-efficient, reducing end-to-end synthesis cost by $\sim 8\times$ GPU-hours, while producing highly aligned synthetic data. Empirically, X-AVDD significantly outperforms other audio-visual distillation methods. On VGGSound, an audio-visual dataset of short YouTube clips, X-AVDD improves top-1 test accuracy from $8.2\%\rightarrow24.0\%$ at SPC=$10$ and $9.8\%\rightarrow26.5\%$ at SPC=$20$. Furthermore, X-AVDD improves cross-architecture generalization (e.g., $25.5\%\rightarrow35.3\%$ for a ViT target on AVE dataset at SPC $=50$) and successfully scales to complex architectures such as AudioCLIP, achieving $62.3\%$ accuracy using only $5.65\%$ of the full dataset, against the full dataset accuracy of $72.4\%$ while completing in $9\times$ lower training time compared to the full dataset.

</details>


### [103] [Improving Semantic Coverage in AI Test & Evaluation through Generative Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13697)
*Connor Hashemi,Bharadwaj Ravichandran,Connor Anderson,Shitij Govil,Brandon RichardWebster,Brian Hu,Keith Fieldhouse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段生成式AI流水线，在无真值语义标签下检测并填补测试集的语义覆盖缺口（如昼夜、天气），用VLM零样本标注+嵌入聚类定位关键语义、扩散图生图扩展样本、嵌入统计分析验证增益，仅扩充测试集不重训练，在分类与检测任务上使T&E结论更接近全覆盖理想数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型测试评估（T&E）通常只能拿到held-out测试集，无法获知训练数据的语义构成。若关键输入条件（时段、天气、场景位置）在测试集中覆盖不足，T&E结果会因语义覆盖缺口而产生误导性结论。既有做法依赖人工重采集或真值语义标签，成本高且不可行。

Method: 三阶段生成式流水线：（1）用视觉语言模型对语义因子做零样本二分类标注，并通过嵌入空间聚类识别对下游模型行为影响最大的语义；（2）用基于扩散的图像到图像模型把已覆盖图像转换到欠代表语义域，保持场景内容、只改变高层外观；（3）对增强后图像嵌入做统计分析，在纳入数据集前验证合成图像确实提升覆盖率。合成数据仅用于丰富测试集，不进行任何模型重训练。

Result: 在图像分类与目标检测两类任务、昼夜语义缺口设定下验证全流程，增强后的测试集能更好地区分“接触过缺失语义”与“未接触过”的模型，使T&E结论更贴近理想全覆盖数据集的结论。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 无真值标签的生成式语义补全可有效缓解T&E中的语义覆盖偏差，且因不重训练模型而适合作为评估侧工具；方法框架可推广到其他可被VLM识别、可被扩散模型迁移的语义因子。

Abstract: In test and evaluation (T&E) of AI models, evaluators typically have access only to a held-out test dataset and no knowledge of the training data or its semantic composition. If critical input conditions---such as time-of-day, weather, or scene location---are underrepresented in this test dataset, T&E results can be misleadingly due to this semantic coverage gap. We propose a three-stage generative AI pipeline to detect and fill such semantic gaps without requiring ground-truth labels of these semantics. First, vision-language models (VLMs) perform zero-shot binary labeling of semantic factors, and an embedding-space clustering step identifies which semantics most affect downstream model behavior. Second, diffusion-based image-to-image models transform existing covered images into the underrepresented semantic domain, preserving scene content while augmenting high-level appearance. Third, statistical analysis of the augmented image embeddings verifies that the synthetic imagery improves dataset coverage before it is incorporated. Crucially, the synthetic data is used only to enrich the test set and no model retraining is performed for T&E. We validate the full pipeline on both image classification and object detection under day/night semantic gaps, showing that the augmented test datasets better distinguish models with and without exposure to the missing semantic, thereby yielding T&E conclusions that more closely match those of an ideal, fully covered dataset. Code available at this https URL

</details>


### [104] [GLIMPSE: Global-Local Indexing with Multi-Precision Spectral Encoding for Explorable Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13706)
*Zhufeng Xu,Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Yikang Ding,Xiaoqiang Liu,Guoxin Zhang,Hongbo Fu,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLIMPSE提出全局-局部记忆接口，用分带多精度坐标与几何条件光谱衰减降低对不准确投影地址的敏感性，在匹配检索与读取预算下提升相机控制视频世界模型的几何对应与光度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机控制的视频世界模型需要保证相机返回同一场景时的一致性。将存储特征重渲染到目标帧的记忆机制会把深度与位姿误差转化为鬼影或结构重复。位置传输（positional transport）通过移动历史patch的地址而非内容来避免重采样，但对不准确的投影仍赋予完整位置精度，导致对误差过度敏感。

Method: 提出GLIMPSE：一种全局-局部记忆接口。核心包括（1）分带多精度坐标（band-wise multi-precision coordinates），按频带为投影地址分配不同精度；（2）几何条件光谱衰减（geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation），依据几何可靠性抑制不准确地址对应的高频成分；（3）局部patch记忆提供视图相关外观，循环全局记忆提供场景上下文；（4）足迹引导检索（footprint-guided retrieval）在固定局部读取预算下挑选历史patch。

Result: 在匹配的检索与读取预算条件下，GLIMPSE在几何对应和光度一致性指标上均优于确定性的位置传输基线。

Conclusion: 通过多精度位置编码与几何条件光谱衰减，可有效缓解位置传输对投影误差的敏感性，为相机控制视频世界模型提供更一致的长期记忆机制。

Abstract: A camera-controlled video world model must keep a scene consistent when the camera returns to it. Memories that render stored features into the target frame can turn depth and pose errors into ghosting or duplicated structure. Positional transport avoids that resampling by relocating a historical patch's address rather than its content, but assigns full positional precision even to inaccurate projections. We present GLIMPSE, a global-local memory interface with band-wise multi-precision coordinates and geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation to reduce sensitivity to inaccurate projected addresses. Local patch memory supplies view-specific appearance, while recurrent global memory provides scene context. Footprint-guided retrieval selects historical patches under a fixed local read budget. Under matched retrieval and read budgets, GLIMPSE improves geometric correspondence and photometric consistency over deterministic positional transport.

</details>


### [105] [State-Isolated Vision Mamba via Update-Tendency Routing for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13720)
*Yan Luo,Laibin Chang,Zefan Feng,Yi'ang Chen,Kui Jiang,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Vision Mamba中"状态争夺"(State Contention)问题，并给出"状态隔离"(State Isolation)原则与IsoMamba架构：通过Δ引导的更新表示、无标签聚类与cluster–route–isolate路由到隔离的Mamba专家，配合通道动态重加权，在水下图像增强任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Mamba研究多聚焦于token序列化、扫描策略或专家路由，而忽略了状态层面的问题：空间异质的视觉复原任务应如何组织循环隐藏状态。作者认为，将更新行为异质的token强行作用于同一共享隐藏状态会造成持续的内容间干扰。

Method: 提出State Contention概念，主张按token兼容性而非token顺序组织状态演化；提出State Isolation原则，让状态更新倾向兼容的token在独立循环状态流中演化；实例化模型IsoMamba为Δ引导架构，构建模型内在的更新表示，通过无标签聚类发现更新兼容的token分组，按cluster–route–isolate范式路由至隔离的Mamba专家；另设计通道级动态重加权目标以补偿水下图像随波长变化的复原难度。

Result: 在多个水下图像增强基准上一致取得SOTA性能，并呈现出更干净的隐藏状态响应，验证了状态隔离式循环建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 状态组织的兼容性原则可作为Vision Mamba的一种有效改进方向；但本文验证局限于水下图像增强，未涉及机器人与具身智能任务，泛化到其他密集预测与决策任务尚待验证。

Abstract: Vision Mamba has recently emerged as an efficient alternative to convolutional and Transformer-based architectures by modeling long-range spatial dependencies through recurrent state evolution. Existing studies primarily improve token serialization, scanning strategies, or expert routing, yet largely overlook a state level question question: how should recurrent states be organized for spatially heterogeneous visual restoration? In this paper, we show that forcing tokens with heterogeneous update behaviors to recurrently modify a shared hidden state leads to persistent cross-content interference, which we term \textbf{State Contention}. We further argue that effective recurrent modeling should organize state evolution according to token compatibility rather than token ordering. Based on this observation, we introduce \textbf{State Isolation}, a new recurrent state organization principle for Vision Mamba,tokens with compatible state-update tendencies evolve within independent recurrent state streams. To instantiate this principle, we propose \textbf{IsoMamba}, a $\Delta$-guided architecture that constructs model-intrinsic update representations, discovers update-compatible token groups through label-free clustering, and routes them to isolated Mamba experts following a \textbf{cluster--route--isolate} paradigm. In addition, we develop a dynamic channel-wise reweighting objective to compensate for wavelength-dependent reconstruction difficulty in underwater images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IsoMamba consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks while providing cleaner hidden state responses, validating the effectiveness of state isolated recurrent modeling.

</details>


### [106] [LAPT-Scale: Scaling Embodied Policies with Latent Action Pre-Training on Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13732)
*Zhiyuan Xiang,Peidong Liu,Mingyang Li,Jiawei Li,Wenhow Li,Wenbo Ding,Xiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAPT-Scale 提出可扩展的潜在动作预训练范式：LAPT-Scale-LAM 从人类自我中心视频的帧间视觉转换中学习连续潜在动作作为监督信号，LAPT-Scale-Policy 经人类视频潜在动作预训练、目标机器人具身对齐和任务后训练得到 VLA 策略，在 23.3K 小时视频上训练，真机成功率从 63.3% 提升至 85.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略的规模化受限于具身交互数据的高采集成本；大规模自我中心人类视频虽丰富，却缺乏机器人动作标签，而现有动作标注方法在真实视觉复杂性和多样操作动力学下噪声较大。

Method: 方法分两部分：LAPT-Scale-LAM 通过学习帧间视觉转换来解释人类视频，得到连续潜在动作并作为人类派生的监督信号（相较此前向量量化 LAM，采用连续潜在空间以保留细粒度运动与交互动力学，并融合感知、语义与光流对齐信号以改进转换级监督，超越像素重构）；LAPT-Scale-Policy 依次进行人类视频潜在动作预训练、目标机器人具身对齐与任务特定后训练。

Result: 在 23.3K 小时自我中心视频上训练，LAPT-Scale-LAM 为策略预训练提供隐式动作监督，随标注人类视频从 1K 增至 5K 小时呈现一致的规模化增益，真机成功率由 63.3% 提升至 85.0%，且所学策略可泛化到未见视觉条件。

Conclusion: 以人类自我中心视频中的连续潜在动作作为可规模化的隐式监督，能够有效预训练 VLA 策略并带来稳定的规模化收益与真机性能提升，同时具备视觉条件泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling generalist robotic policies is limited by the high cost of collecting embodied interaction data. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer a rich alternative, but they lack robot action labels, and existing action annotation methods are often noisy under real-world visual complexity and diverse manipulation dynamics. We introduce LAPT-Scale, a scalable latent action pre-training paradigm with two components: LAPT-Scale-LAM learns continuous latent actions by explaining frame-to-frame visual transitions in human videos and uses them as human-derived supervision, while LAPT-Scale-Policy trains a VLA policy through human-video latent-action pre-training, target-robot embodiment alignment, and task-specific post-training. Unlike prior vector-quantized LAMs, LAPT-Scale-LAM uses a continuous latent space to preserve fine-grained motion and interaction dynamics. We further combine perceptual, semantic, and optical-flow alignment signals to improve transition-level supervision beyond pixel reconstruction. Trained on 23.3K hours of egocentric video, LAPT-Scale-LAM provides implicit action supervision for policy pre-training, yielding consistent scaling gains and improving real-robot success from 63.3% to 85.0% as annotated human video scales from 1K to 5K hours. The learned policy also generalizes to unseen visual conditions.

</details>


### [107] [KVeil: First-Place Solution to ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13733)
*Boheng Li,Yueming Xing,Yiming Li,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告给出 ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP "不可学习视频"挑战赛两个阶段的第一名方案：阶段1用参考容量崩塌损失与运动引导EOT实现隐蔽扰动下的面部视频保护；阶段2提出双层框架，分别保护人脸与背景区域以抵御微调式定制和未知生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视频生成技术使少量人脸视频即可被用于未授权身份复制，现有保护手段在有限且不可感知的扰动预算、"未知推理条件"以及基于微调的定制攻击下鲁棒性不足，需要一个统一扰动即可同时防御参考驱动与微调驱动两类定制威胁。

Method: 阶段1提出 Reference Capacity Collapse：一对互补损失，在削弱参考条件所携带信息的同时保持后续视频表示对其的依赖，并引入 Motion-Guided EOT 提升对未知推理条件的鲁棒性。阶段2提出双层框架，对人脸区域与背景区域分别施加保护，前者基于合成目标身份构造结构对齐扰动，后者强化阶段1的白盒破坏效果。方法均建立在作者近期工作 LETHE 之上。

Result: 在白盒参考驱动的定制场景下实现近乎完美的保护，并在其他定制范式、多种生成模型以及时序攻击下仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 该工作表明通过参考容量崩塌与分区域双层扰动设计，可在不可感知预算下对面向人脸视频的未授权定制形成强保护，为视频身份隐私防护提供了可扩展思路。

Abstract: The rapid development of personalized video generation has raised serious concerns over identity privacy, as a few facial videos of a person now suffice to reproduce their identity in synthetic videos. This report presents the first-place solutions to both phases of the ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos, which aims to protect facial videos from unauthorized customization under a limited and imperceptible perturbation budget. For Phase 1, we introduce Reference Capacity Collapse, a pair of complementary losses that degrade the information carried by the reference condition while keeping subsequent video representations dependent on it, together with a Motion-Guided EOT mechanism that improves robustness across unknown inference conditions. Phase 2 further considers tuning-based customization and unseen generative models. To address both threats within a single perturbation, we design a two-layer framework that protects the facial and background regions separately, constructing structure-aligned perturbations from a synthetic target identity in the former and strengthening the white-box disruption of Phase 1 in the latter. Our method achieves nearly perfect protection under white-box reference-based customization, and remains effective under other customization paradigms, various models, and temporal attacks. Both solutions are built upon our recent work LETHE. We hope our solutions can inspire future work on protecting facial videos against unauthorized customization. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [108] [Gaussian Splatting with Reflections Guided by What is Seen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13757)
*Yiming Liang,Tianyu Xiao,Hiroshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于2DGS的框架，利用输入图像中直接可观测的环境信息，结合混合反射外观表示与视角相关高光建模，提升近场反射场景的新视角合成质量与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF与3DGS在普通场景表现优异，但对视角相关高光（尤其近场反射）建模不足。现有基于环境贴图（仅表示无穷远光源）或环境高斯（仅依赖反射的间接监督）的方法不够准确。

Method: 以2D Gaussian Splatting为基座，直接利用输入图像中可观测的环境信息；引入混合反射外观表示，并加入视角相关高光建模。

Result: 在公开基准及新构建的近场反射数据集上，渲染质量与几何精度均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 直接观测的环境信息加混合反射表示可有效改善近场反射的视角相关建模；但实验主要在合成/自建数据集，真实场景泛化与实时性未验证。

Abstract: While novel view synthesis methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have shown impressive performance on common scenes, they often struggle with capturing view-dependent specular effects, particularly near-field reflections. Existing methods utilize environment maps, which only represent lights from infinity, or environment Gaussians, which rely solely on indirect supervision from reflections, are insufficient for accurately modeling such effects. Here, we propose a framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) that leverages directly observable environment information in input images. Our approach also integrates a hybrid reflection appearance representation and view-dependent specular modeling. Experiments on public benchmarks and a newly curated near-field reflection dataset demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [109] [Neurons++: A Unified Visual-Stream Framework for Image and Video Reconstruction from fMRI with Neural-to-Visual Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13763)
*Haonan Wang,Yuchen Wang,Jingyu Lu,Qixiang Zhang,Lehan Wang,Xuanqi Huang,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Neurons++ 是一个基于 fMRI 的视觉流重建框架，统一支持静态图像与动态视频重建，采用"皮层启发式串行解码"替代并行任务条件，并先做图像-fMRI 预训练再做被试特异微调，取得 SOTA 视频重建与有竞争力的图像重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往生成式 fMRI 解码方法虽能重建看似合理的图像/视频，但视觉合理性不等于输出忠实反映脑活动；同时既有方法多为视频单一任务或并行条件化，缺乏对图像-视频统一处理及神经表征层次化组织。

Method: 提出 Neurons++：视觉流重建框架。先用图像-fMRI 预训练获得稳定空间-语义初始化，再分别针对图像与视频做被试特异微调；将并行任务条件化改为串行、类皮层解码，把 fMRI 表征组织为显式空间、语义、语言层级与时间四类线索。

Result: 在标准基准上实现 SOTA 视频重建与具竞争力的图像重建；神经-视觉分析显示其在神经扰动下具有定性方向可控性、fMRI 编码器读出对刺激相关皮层特征敏感，并支持 ROI 引导的语义调制。

Conclusion: Neurons++ 兼顾图像与视频的视觉流重建，通过串行皮层启发解码提升重建保真度与神经可解释性，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from fMRI is important for understanding human perception and developing brain-computer interfaces. Recent generative decoding methods can reconstruct plausible images and videos, but visual plausibility alone does not ensure that the output is faithfully grounded in brain activity. In this work, we propose Neurons++, a visual-stream fMRI reconstruction framework for both static image and dynamic video reconstruction. Compared with our conference version Neurons, Neurons++ extends the paradigm from video-only reconstruction to visual-stream reconstruction for both images and videos, and replaces parallel task conditioning with serial cortex-inspired decoding. The model first uses image-fMRI pretraining to obtain a stable spatial-semantic initialization, and then performs subject-specific fine-tuning for image and video reconstruction separately. The serial design organizes fMRI-derived representations into explicit spatial, semantic, language-level, and temporal cues. Extensive experiments show that Neurons++ achieves state-of-the-art video reconstruction and competitive image reconstruction across standard benchmarks. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, neural-to-visual analyses indicate that Neurons++ exhibits qualitative directional controllability under neural perturbation, that its fMRI encoder read-out is sensitive to stimulus-relevant cortical features, and that it supports ROI-guided semantic modulation. Code and model weights are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [Shortcut Learning in Geospatial Foundation Model Embeddings for Cross-Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13766)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究地理空间基础模型（TerraMind、Prithvi-EO-2.0、DOFA、DINOv3）在灾害损伤评估中的"捷径学习"问题：当以整个灾害事件为单位留出（而非随机划分瓦片）时，xView2 上平均平衡准确率从 0.807 降至 0.638，BRIGHT 上从 0.818 降至 0.568。作者通过四组测试揭示预测所依赖的信息来源（事件/采集上下文、前/后时相影像、建筑数量、正确配对），并提出仅用训练灾害学习特征变换（监督对比学习、PCA）来提升对未见灾害的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型在灾害损伤评估上表现优异，但随机划分图像瓦片的评估方式可能导致"捷径学习"——模型依赖事件或场景特征（如灾害类型、成像条件、建筑密度）而非真正可迁移的损伤证据。这使得在熟悉灾害上的高精度不能反映对新灾害的鲁棒性，因此需要以"留出整个灾害事件"的方式重新评估模型，并探索能够提升跨灾害泛化的简单方法。

Method: 在 xView2 与 BRIGHT 两个多灾害数据集上，提取 TerraMind、Prithvi-EO-2.0、DOFA、DINOv3 四种地理空间基础模型的嵌入特征；采用"留出整个灾害"的域外评估协议，与随机瓦片划分对比；设计四组测试分别考察事件与采集上下文、前/后时相影像输入、建筑数量、正确图像配对对预测的贡献；最后测试仅用训练灾害数据学习特征变换（如监督对比学习、PCA）对未见灾害的泛化效果。

Result: 留出整个灾害时，xView2 平均平衡准确率由 0.807 降至 0.638，BRIGHT 由 0.818 降至 0.568。灾害前的影像本身即具预测性；仅输入标注的建筑数量，分类器即可达到 0.629（xView2）和 0.653（BRIGHT）的平衡准确率。前/后时相特征融合在 xView2 上平均最优，但在 BRIGHT 上未优于仅用灾前特征。在同一灾害、同一损伤类别内打乱图像配对仅造成轻微性能损失。基于训练灾害学习的特征变换能提升泛化，其中监督对比学习在 xView2 上最佳，PCA 在 BRIGHT 上最佳。

Conclusion: 随机瓦片划分会高估地理空间基础模型在灾害损伤评估中的真实能力，模型存在明显的捷径学习；在未见域（新灾害）上评估对衡量鲁棒性至关重要。简单、仅基于训练灾害的特征级变换即可有效提升跨灾害泛化，为无需目标域标注的稳健灾害评估提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can support disaster damage assessment, but high accuracy on familiar disasters may hide shortcut learning: prediction from event or scene characteristics rather than evidence that transfers to a new disaster. We study embeddings from TerraMind, Prithvi-EO-2.0, DOFA, and DINOv3 on the multi-disaster datasets xView2 and BRIGHT. Holding out an entire disaster, instead of randomly splitting image tiles, reduces mean balanced accuracy from 0.807 to 0.638 on xView2 and from 0.818 to 0.568 on BRIGHT. Four sets of tests examine what information supports prediction: event and acquisition context, before/after image inputs, building count, and correct image pairing. Pre-event images remain predictive, and a classifier given only the annotated number of buildings reaches balanced accuracy of 0.629 and 0.653. Combining before/after features performs best on average on xView2, but does not improve over pre-only features on BRIGHT. Shuffling image pairs within the same disaster and damage class causes modest losses. Motivated by these findings, we test whether feature transformations learned only from training disasters improve generalization to unseen disasters. These transformations improve performance, with supervised contrastive learning performing best on xView2 and PCA on BRIGHT. Our results highlight the importance of evaluation on unseen domains for assessing model robustness and show that simple feature-level transformations can improve generalization. Code and experiments can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [111] [READ: Reading the Selective Scan for Faithful Video Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13776)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Piotr Kubaty,Arkadiusz Lewicki,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 READ，一种面向选择性状态空间模型（如 Mamba）视频模型的免参数归因方法：在分类器读取的时序位置展开递归，将循环状态中的贡献回溯到视频 token，单次前向即可得到时空相关性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性状态空间模型适合长序列视频建模，但其递归计算使"哪些视频 token 驱动了预测"难以判定；现有归因方法需要额外反向传播或多次模型评估，成本远高于其所解释的前向推理。作者希望以零额外开销获得可信归因。

Method: READ 不引入可学习参数：在分类器读取（readout）的位置展开递归，锚定归因于分类器读出点，沿递归状态逐帧反向分解累积贡献，直接得到单一时空相关性图，可从中导出帧级与区域级重要性；全过程仅需一次前向传播。

Result: 在多个数据集与骨干网络上，单次前向的 READ 在忠实度与定位性能上可与成本高得多的归因方法竞争，避免了已有方法的系统性相关性偏置，并恢复出有意义的时间结构与类别特定结构。

Conclusion: 递归结构不仅是选择性状态空间模型高效推理的机制，也直接构成其预测可解释证据的来源；READ 为长序列视频模型提供了免参数、单次前向的归因方案。

Abstract: Selective state-space models are an efficient approach to long-sequence video modeling. However, their recurrent computation makes it difficult to determine which video tokens drive a prediction. Existing attribution methods often require additional backward passes or repeated model evaluations, making them substantially more expensive than the forward inference they aim to explain. In this work, we introduce READ, a parameter-free attribution method for selective state-space models that unrolls the recurrence at the position read by the classifier. READ traces the contribution accumulated in the recurrent state back to the video tokens that contribute to the final prediction. By anchoring attribution at the classifier's readout, it produces a single spatiotemporal relevance map from which frame- and region-level relevance can be obtained directly. Despite requiring only one forward pass, READ achieves competitive faithfulness and localisation compared with substantially more expensive attribution methods. Experimental results show that READ avoids the systematic relevance biases of existing methods while recovering meaningful temporal and class-specific structure across datasets and backbones. More broadly, our results show that the recurrence underlying selective state-space models is not only an efficient mechanism for inference, but also a direct source of interpretable evidence for their predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Detection of extremely rare dead cells in fluorescence microscopy images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13780)
*Prateek Verma,Benjamin Butter,Jian Zhang,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了启发式、分类（传统数值特征与深度学习图像特征）与异常检测三类方法在荧光显微图像中检测凋亡/死细胞的效果，发现ResNet-18与XGBoost表现最佳，但整体任务仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微表型筛选与药效评估需要自动检测死细胞，但信号变异、光学伪影、多通道共定位歧义、标记物时空不一致及累积动力学差异使通用阈值不可靠，故需系统评估各类计算方法。

Method: 在258张图像、65,527个细胞/核（含243个人工标注死细胞）的数据集上，对比三类方法：(1)基于强度阈值、分布、相关性与域引导的启发式；(2)分类方法，含数值特征(LR、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM)与图像特征(ResNet-18、EfficientNet-B0)；(3)异常检测，含数值特征(Isolation Forest、one-class SVM、PCA重构)与图像特征(卷积自编码器、Deep SVDD)。以PR-AUC、Precision、Recall、F1、macro-F1评估。

Result: 基于图像特征的深度学习分类与基于数值特征的传统分类效果最佳，ResNet-18与XGBoost显著优于人工启发式与异常检测方法。

Conclusion: 此类生物医学问题即便使用最先进的计算与学习技术仍极具挑战；研究为科研人员提供决策支持工具，可全自动或人在回路方式辅助领域科学家。

Abstract: Dead cell detection in fluorescence microscopy is essential for phenotypic screening and drug efficacy assessment. However, automated identification remains challenging due to signal variability, optical artifacts, and colocalization ambiguities across multiple channels. Markers that define nuclear boundaries do not neatly coincide spatially or temporally with apoptotic markers. Furthermore, marker accumulation kinetics vary across individual cells and apoptosis stages, making universal thresholds unreliable. We present a systematic comparison of several computational approaches for detecting apoptotic cells in nucleus-stained specimens: (1) heuristic methods using intensity thresholds, distributions, correlations, and domain guidance; (2) classification using numerical features (Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost and LightGBM) and image features (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0); and (3) anomaly detection, also using numerical features (Isolation Forest, one-class SVM and PCA reconstruction) and image features (convolutional autoencoders and Deep SVDD). Using a dataset of 258 images containing 65,527 cells/nuclei across multiple scenes with 243 manually annotated ground truth dead cells, we evaluate each method on metrics such as PR-AUC, Precision, Recall, F1-score, and macro-F1. Our results indicate that classification using image features (deep learning) and numerical features (traditional) achieved the best performance, with ResNet-18 and XGBoost significantly outperforming manually designed heuristics and anomaly detection methods. Our findings demonstrate that such biomedical problems remain highly challenging to solve even with the state-of-the-art computational and learning techniques. We aim to provide an effective decision-support tool to researchers while tackling such problems either fully-automatically or in a human-in-the-loop setting as an aide to domain scientists.

</details>


### [113] [SimVTA: Simulation-Grounded Video-Trajectory Alignment for Driving World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13781)
*Changhui Jing,Haochen Tian,Hongchen Li,Tianyu Li,Caojun Wang,Zihao Zhang,Yanhao Liu,Chengen Xie,Bailin Li,Hao Zhao,Zhenjie Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimVTA 提出一种基于仿真的驾驶世界动作模型（WAM）后训练框架：用 3DGS 重建真实场景，对候选轨迹渲染未来视频，为高奖励候选提供轨迹匹配的视觉监督，并用 GRPO 优化轨迹规划、流匹配损失更新共享表征，推理时无需仿真器调用。NAVSIM v1 PDMS 从 86.2 提升到 91.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界动作模型的后训练可用强化学习探索替代轨迹并用驾驶奖励改进规划，但驾驶日志只提供记录轨迹对应的未来视频，替代轨迹缺少匹配的视觉监督，导致视频监督与轨迹优化脱节。作者希望为高奖励候选轨迹补齐与轨迹一致的未来视觉监督，且不依赖模型自身预测。

Method: 采样候选轨迹，用 World Engine 等真实世界仿真器在 3DGS 重建场景中渲染高奖励候选的未来视频，得到轨迹匹配监督；在有限渲染预算下优先对渲染候选中误差较高的未来做视频监督；用 GRPO 优化轨迹规划，并对匹配视频施加流匹配损失以更新共享表征；推理阶段直接预测轨迹，不调用仿真器。

Result: NAVSIM v1 上以 WAM 初始化应用 SimVTA，PDMS 由 86.2 提升至 91.3（+5.1）；NAVSIM v2 达 88.2 EPDMS；HUGSIM 闭环评测平均 HD-Score 为 38.8。消融显示：用视频监督更新共享表征可提升规划，而在高奖励轨迹中优先监督高误差未来可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 将轨迹优化与仿真渲染的轨迹匹配视频监督结合，可有效弥补替代轨迹缺少视觉监督的问题，说明视频监督更新共享表征对规划有增益，且按误差优先分配渲染预算有效。

Abstract: Driving world action models (WAMs) learn from paired trajectories and future observations. Post-training with reinforcement learning allows the policy to explore alternative trajectories and improve planning using driving rewards. However, driving logs contain future videos only for the recorded trajectories, leaving these alternatives without corresponding visual supervision. Thus, we introduce SimVTA, a simulation-grounded post-training framework that combines trajectory optimization with video supervision. First, we sample candidate trajectories for each observation and use a real-world simulator (e.g., World Engine) to render future videos for high-reward candidates in real-world scenes reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). This provides trajectory-matched supervision independent of the model's own predictions. Under a limited rendering budget, we prioritize video supervision for higher-error futures among these rendered candidates. Finally, we optimize trajectory planning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and use a flow-matching loss on the matched videos to update shared representations. At inference time, the model predicts trajectories directly, without simulator calls. On NAVSIM v1, applying SimVTA to a WAM initialization improves PDMS from 86.2 to 91.3 (+5.1). It achieves 88.2 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and an average HD-Score of 38.8 in closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM. Ablations show that updating shared representations with video supervision improves planning, while prioritizing higher-error futures among high-reward trajectories further improves performance.

</details>


### [114] [OccludeBench: Can VLMs Find What's Missing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13790)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Ryo Mitsuhashi,Linrong Cai,Xingyu Fu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OccludeBench 是一个人工标注基准，用于评估 VLM 从残留视觉线索恢复完全被遮挡物体的能力，并配套四阶段诊断工具 OccludeTrace 定位推理瓶颈，提出 inpaint-then-confront 干预显著缩小直接线索与推论线索案例间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是 VLM 在"图像中缺失内容"的推理能力尚不清楚。作者区分两类场景：直接案例（依赖阴影、反射等具体物理线索）与推论案例（依赖更广场景上下文），以检验模型是否能真正利用物理线索进行遮挡恢复。

Method: 构建 OccludeBench 人工标注基准，含直接与推论两类遮挡恢复任务；评测五种 VLM 并与人类对比；提出 OccludeTrace 四阶段诊断流程定位推理失效环节；尝试扩展推理、工具调用、few-shot 提示，以及 inpaint-then-confront 干预（先生成候选隐藏物体，再对照可见物理线索评估该假设）。

Result: 模型在直接案例平均准确率 42.7%，推论案例 62.7%，存在 20.0 个百分点差距；人类分别为 63.4% 与 68.5%，无显著差距。瓶颈出现在需从全图投影中恢复相关物体：失败样例中仅 50% 成功，而正确样例达 91%。扩展推理、工具调用与 few-shot 提升不稳定。inpaint-then-confront 将差距降至统计不显著，对两类具备图像生成能力的模型均有效。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可操控、可诊断的评测框架，用于隔离 VLM 对图像缺失视觉内容的推理过程；指出物理线索恢复阶段是主要瓶颈，并给出有效缓解方向。

Abstract: How well can vision-language models (VLMs) reason about what is missing from an image? We study this question with OccludeBench, a manually curated benchmark for recovering fully occluded objects from the visual evidence that remains. OccludeBench distinguishes direct cases, where the hidden object must be recovered from a specific physical cue such as a shadow or reflection, from inferential cases, where it can be inferred from broader scene context. Across five VLMs, accuracy averages 42.7% on direct cases versus 62.7% on inferential cases, exhibiting a 20.0-point gap. Humans attain higher accuracies of 63.4% versus 68.5% for direct and inferential images, respectively, with no significant gap. To locate this failure, we introduce OccludeTrace, a four-stage diagnostic that isolates where the visual reasoning process breaks down. The bottleneck emerges when models must recover the relevant object from the projection within the full image: they succeed on only 50% of failed examples, compared with 91% of examples they answer correctly. Extended reasoning, tool use, and few-shot prompting yield inconsistent improvements and do not reliably eliminate the gap. In contrast, an inpaint-then-confront intervention, which prompts models to first generate a candidate hidden object and then evaluate that hypothesis against the visible physical cue, reduces the gap to a statistically non-significant difference for both models with image-generation capabilities. Together, our results establish a tractable, controlled diagnostic for isolating how VLMs reason about visual content that is missing from an image.

</details>


### [115] [SpatialASM: Internalizing Active Mentalization for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13832)
*Yuhong Liu,Shenxi Wu,Junyi Chi,Beichen Zhang,Xuanlang Dai,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialASM 提出通过训练时的空间仿真（全新视角生成 + 显式 ASM 轨迹 + 自动构建的视角转换任务）将主动空间心智化能力内化到 LVLM 中，在 7 个空间推理基准上提升 4 个骨干模型的精度并保持通用视觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在解决复杂空间推理任务时会主动从任务相关视角重访场景，并在脑中重建该视角下的空间布局；而当前 LVLM 缺乏这种主动空间心智化（ASM）能力。同时，空间思维链（CoT）监督数据稀缺，限制了激发模型主动视角探索与心理场景仿真。

Method: SpatialASM 框架：采用异中心（allocentric）全新视角生成器，将假设视角锚定在视觉证据上；生成两类互补监督：(i) 显式 ASM 轨迹，教模型何时、如何自适应调用视角规划与想象；(ii) 自动构建的视角采择任务，强化以视角为条件的场景想象。通过训练期空间仿真将该能力内化到 LVLM。

Result: 在 7 个从感知导向空间理解到多图、跨视角、视角变换推理的基准上，4 个 LVLM 骨干均取得空间推理性能提升，同时保留原有通用视觉能力，七基准平均准确率上升。

Conclusion: 结果证明该方法可有效提升 LVLM 的空间推理；ASM 轨迹的合成提供了一条可扩展路径，缓解空间 CoT 监督稀缺问题，从而激发主动视角探索与心理场景仿真。

Abstract: When solving complex spatial reasoning tasks, ordinary humans naturally revisit the scenes from task-relevant viewpoints and mentally reconstruct the spatial layouts from those viewpoints. But can Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) proactively think in the same way? We term this ability Active Spatial Mentalization (ASM), an intrinsic component of visual intelligence that remains substantially underdeveloped in current LVLMs. To bridge this gap, we introduce SpatialASM, a scalable framework to internalize ASM into LVLMs through training-time spatial simulation. SpatialASM employs an allocentric novel-view generator to ground hypothetical viewpoints in visual evidence and produces two complementary forms of supervision: i) explicit ASM trajectories that teach LVLMs when and how to adaptively invoke view planning and imagination during spatial reasoning, and ii) automatically-constructed perspective-taking tasks that further strengthens viewpoint-conditioned scene imagination. On seven benchmarks spanning relatively perception-oriented spatial understanding to more challenging tasks requiring multi-image, cross-view and viewpoint-transformation reasoning, SpatialASM yields performance gain on LVLMs of four LVLM backbones in spatial reasoning while retaining their original general vision capabilities. The experimental results demonstrate that our approach improves average accuracy across the seven spatial benchmarks. And the synthesis of ASM trajectories provides a scalable pathway to alleviate the scarcity of spatial Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision for eliciting active viewpoint exploration and mental scene simulation.

</details>


### [116] [Event-based Action Recognition by Pre-Training on Multi-Frame Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13856)
*Emma Norrby,Tomas Wilkinson,Per Nyblom,David Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多帧事件表示与稀疏度感知掩码自编码预训练ViT，在长时复杂事件序列动作识别上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机能捕捉快速运动且隐私性更好，但基于帧的事件表示在长时、复杂事件序列上判别能力急剧下降，需要更强的表示与预训练方法。

Method: 采用多帧事件表示替代单帧；以掩码自编码（MAE）框架预训练Vision Transformer，使用稀疏度感知掩码算法与token选择机制控制计算复杂度；解码器引入多个掩码token与位置感知注意力机制。

Result: 在长时复杂事件序列上表现突出，于THU-EACT-50 CHL与DailyDVS-200数据集上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 多帧表示结合稀疏度感知掩码预训练可显著提升事件动作识别性能，适用于长时复杂序列场景。

Abstract: Action recognition is a crucial task in domains like robotics, patient monitoring, or surveillance. Recently, event-based action recognition has attracted much attention due to the event camera's ability to capture fast motions and its improved privacy over conventional vision cameras. While frame-based event representations work well for short event sequences, their discriminative ability declines sharply with longer and more complex event sequences. In this work, we introduce a multi-frame representation that greatly improves recognition performance over its single-frame counterpart. We adopt a masked auto-encoding framework to pre-train a Vision Transformer using a sparsity-aware masking algorithm and token selection mechanism to improve downstream performance and control computational complexity. We also introduce multiple mask tokens for the decoder with a position-aware attention mechanism, leading to further improved performance. Our proposed method shows impressive performance for challenging long-duration and complex event sequences, yielding state-of-the-art performance on THU-EACT-50 CHL and DailyDVS-200.

</details>


### [117] [Revisiting the Task of Building Damage Assessment by Satellite Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13857)
*Anthony Cioppa,Virgile Blua,Sébastien Piérard,Pietro Ceccato,Marc Van Droogenbroeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文（章节）从第一性原理重新审视建筑物损毁评估（BDA）任务，围绕数据、评估和方法三大支柱展开，主张采用传感器无关、基于实例且容忍缺失震前影像的任务定义，并指出当前以单一聚合分数为主的评估实践理论脆弱，提出基于排序的多分数评估协议，最后讨论如何据此组织BDA挑战赛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震、热带风暴、洪水等自然灾害造成大规模建筑损毁，快速可靠的损毁制图对应急响应至关重要。尽管深度学习推动BDA研究近十年并发布了多个大规模数据集，但该领域仍缺乏共享基础：任务定义不统一、评估指标单一且脆弱、方法在未见事件与区域上的泛化能力不足。作者因此希望从第一性原理重建该领域的共同框架。

Method: 属于立场性综述/章节，非新方法论文。核心工作是：(1) 辨析候选任务形式，论证传感器无关、基于实例、允许缺失震前影像的定义；(2) 沿传感模态、时序配置、地理覆盖三个维度梳理现有数据集；(3) 分析主流单一聚合评分的理论缺陷，构建基于排序的多分数评估协议；(4) 综述从任务专用深度网络到地球观测基础模型的方法谱系；(5) 讨论交叉验证、挑战赛、私有数据共享等组织方案。

Result: 论文为方法论与评估框架层面的分析与论证，不包含实验或定量结果。主要"结果"是提出并论证了一套更合理的任务定义、覆盖多维度的数据集分类体系、以及更信息量的排序式多分数评估协议；并指出对未见事件与区域的泛化仍是未解难题。

Conclusion: BDA领域亟需统一的任务定义与评估标准；传感器无关、基于实例且容忍缺失震前影像的定义更具实用性，单一聚合分数不足以刻画方法优劣，排序式多分数协议能提供更丰富信息；地球观测基础模型虽有潜力，但跨事件、跨区域泛化仍待突破；通过合理的挑战赛与数据共享机制可在开发者与用户间建立信任。

Abstract: Natural disasters such as earthquakes, tropical storms, and floods cause extensive damage to buildings, and rapid, reliable damage maps are essential to guide emergency response. Automating this task, referred to as building damage assessment (BDA), from satellite imagery, has become an active research topic at the intersection of remote sensing, computer vision, and machine learning, particularly since supervised deep learning methods were shown to outperform earlier handcrafted approaches. Yet, despite a decade of intense activity and the release of several large-scale datasets, the field lacks a shared foundation. This chapter revisits the task of BDA from first principles, organized around three pillars: data, evaluation, and methods. We first clarify the candidate task for- mulations and argue for a sensor-agnostic, instance-based definition that tolerates missing pre-event imagery. We then review existing datasets along the axes of sensing modality, temporal configuration, and geographic coverage. On the evaluation side, we show that prevailing practices, largely based on a single aggregate score, are theoretically fragile, and we lay the foundations of a more informative, ranking-based, multi-score protocol. We further review methods ranging from task-specific deep networks to recent Earth observation foundation models, noticing that generalization to unseen events and regions remains an open difficulty. Finally, we discuss how these elements can inform the organization of a BDA challenge, including cross-validation, challenge, and private data-sharing setups designed to build trust between developers and users.

</details>


### [118] [LapMerge: Differentiable Exact-Budget Token Merging in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13858)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Aleksandra Nowak,Tomasz Steifer,Łukasz Struski,Szymon Płotka,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LapMerge：一种可微分的序列 token 合并算子，通过拉普拉斯核密度学得质量分布，将序列划分为恰好 N 个等质量段，前向与反向均为 O(M+N) 线性复杂度，兼顾精确输出预算、端到端可微与线性开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉与语言模型处理的 token 序列越来越长，压缩是降低下游成本的主要手段，但现有 token 压缩方法存在根本矛盾：选择式压缩（selecting）直接丢弃信息；合并式压缩（merging）虽保留信息，却只能依赖成对 token 匹配或 MN 级别的 token-to-output 交互，导致代价高、难以端到端优化，且无法精确控制输出长度。作者希望同时获得精确预算、可微性与线性成本这三者。

Method: 提出 LapMerge 可微分合并算子：用可学习的质量分布（learned mass distribution）配合拉普拉斯核密度，将有序序列划分为恰好 N 个等质量段；再通过前缀/后缀前缀和后缀扫描（prefix/suffix scans）对每个段进行聚合。由于划分是精确等质量的，输出预算被严格固定为 N；整个算子在分段与聚合上均为线性，因此前向和反向传播复杂度均为 O(M+N)。算子参数与预算解耦，改变 N 无需重训参数；学到的质量分布本身可作为 token 级别的重要性/预算分配图谱。

Result: 在视觉与视觉-语言任务上，LapMerge 与强 token 压缩器持平或更优，在长序列下速度最多快两个数量级（up to two orders of magnitude）。当 token 顺序反映空间或时间局部性时效果尤为突出；既可作即插即用的压缩模块，也可作为可训练架构组件使用。

Conclusion: LapMerge 用等质量分段的可微合并替代了选择式丢弃与成对合并的折中，统一了精确输出预算、端到端可微和线性复杂度，为长序列视觉-语言模型提供了高效且可解释的 token 压缩方案。

Abstract: Modern vision and language models process ever longer token sequences, and reducing them is the main lever on downstream cost. Token compression, however, faces a fundamental trade-off: selecting tokens discards information, while existing merging methods preserve it only through pairwise token-to-token matching or $MN$ token-to-output interactions. We introduce LapMerge, a differentiable merging operator that resolves this trade-off for ordered sequences: a learned mass distribution partitions the sequence into exactly $N$ equal-mass segments under a Laplace-kernel density, and prefix/suffix scans aggregate each segment, so both the forward and the backward pass run in $\mathcal{O}(M+N)$ time. This combination - an exact output budget, end-to-end differentiability, and linear cost - is precisely what previous reducers trade off against one another. The budget can be changed without changing operator parameters, and the learned mass doubles as a token-level map of where the budget is allocated. Across vision and vision-language tasks, LapMerge matches or improves on strong token reducers while being up to two orders of magnitude faster at large sequence lengths; it is particularly effective when token order reflects spatial or temporal locality, and works both as a drop-in reduction module and as a trainable architectural component.

</details>


### [119] [Make It Flow: Animating Water in 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13860)
*Kjell Keune,Florian Hahlbohm,Jannis Möller,Susana Castillo,Martin Eisemann,Marcus Magnor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将静态3DGS场景"动起来"的方法：从参考图或渲染图估计2D光流并提升为一致的3D欧拉运动场，以cinemagraph式算法变形高斯基元，实现无缝循环的水流动画，保持实时渲染与自由视角导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS等显式表示可实现高质量实时新视角合成，但假设场景静态；真实环境中的高频动态效果（如流水）在随意拍摄条件下无法通过密集多视角高速采集来重建，也无法用相干运动模型捕获，故需在静态3DGS上无需动态采集与物理仿真即可动画化水体。

Method: 1) 从参考图像或模型渲染结果估计2D光流；2) 将2D光流提升（lift）为一致的3D欧拉运动场；3) 提出cinemagraph启发的算法，用该运动场变形（deform）高斯基元；4) 生成时间上无缝的循环动画（temporally seamless looping）。

Result: 在新采集的野外瀑布场景数据集上评估，在视觉质量和计算效率上均明显优于先前的动态高斯泼溅（dynamic Gaussian splatting）与3D cinemagraph生成方法；保留实时渲染性能与场景内自由视角导航，显著提升感知真实感。

Conclusion: 通过光流到3D欧拉运动场的提升与基元变形，可在纯静态3DGS捕获上合成逼真且实时的动态水体，为动态场景编辑提供了一种免采集、免物理仿真的实用路径，但运动需依赖观测流估计，泛化到复杂多物体动态仍受限。

Abstract: Explicit 3D scene representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently enabled high-quality, real-time novel view synthesis. However, these methods assume static scenes, limiting realism in the presence of dynamic phenomena ubiquitous in real-world environments. While coherent motion (e.g., humans or vehicles) can be captured and reconstructed, high-frequency effects such as flowing water remain intractable under casual capture conditions, where dense multi-view, high-speed acquisition is not feasible. We present a method to animate water in static 3DGS scenes, enabling visually plausible motion without requiring dynamic capture or physical simulation. Our approach estimates 2D optical flow from reference images or model renderings and lifts it into a consistent 3D Eulerian motion field. We propose a cinemagraph-inspired algorithm that applies this motion field to deform Gaussian primitives, producing temporally seamless looping animations. The resulting representation retains real-time rendering performance, significantly enhances perceptual realism, and preserves free-viewpoint navigation within the scene. We evaluate our method on a newly captured dataset of in-the-wild waterfall scenes and demonstrate clear improvements over prior work in dynamic Gaussian splatting and 3D cinemagraph generation in visual quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Tracking the Invisible: Self-Supervised Wheel Track Detection with Thermal-RGB Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13872)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出低成本自监督方法，用同步RGB-I（可见光+长波红外）图像检测雪覆盖道路上的车轮轨迹（安全关键可行驶区域），通过将GNSS记录的未来车轮轨迹投影自动生成训练标签，并发布ThermalTrack数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行驶区域/可通过性估计方法在雪覆盖道路上失效；雪地中沿既有车辙行驶能提升抓地力、使车辆动力学更可预测、路径稳定性经验证更可靠，因此车辙检测具有安全关键意义。

Method: 低成本自监督框架：利用同步RGB-I（RGB+长波红外）四通道图像输入神经网络；标签自动生成——将GNSS记录的车辆未来车轮轨迹投影到图像上形成伪标签；针对白化、视觉单调无纹理路面训练车轮轨迹分割/检测网络。

Result: 实验表明在视觉单调、缺乏特征的白化恶劣环境中方法有效，能稳健检测车辙；方法成本低，为冬季自动驾驶提供可行替代方案；公开发布ThermalTrack数据集。

Conclusion: RGB-I多模态自监督车辙检测为雪地冬季自动驾驶提供低成本、鲁棒的可行驶区域方案，并贡献了ThermalTrack数据集。

Abstract: Accurate detection of drivable area is essential for autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. While state-of-the-art methods focus on general traversability estimation, they prove inadequate for snow-covered roads where following existing wheel tracks offers crucial advantages: improved traction, predictable vehicle dynamics, and verified path stability. To address this, we propose a low-cost, self-supervised approach detecting safety-critical wheel tracks using synchronized RGB-I (RGB + Long-Wave Infrared) imagery. Our method automatically generates training labels by projecting future wheel trajectories from recorded GNSS data. A neural network is designed to identify wheel tracks with the 4-channel RGB-I images from challenging, white-out road surfaces. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in visually monotonous and featureless adverse environment, offering a robust and cost-effective alternative for winter autonomy. The newly introduced ThermalTrack dataset is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [Beyond the Snowflakes: Disentangling Weather Effects from Confounding Meta-Factors in LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13891)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过WADS-3D与CADC数据集的细粒度评测发现：降雪强度增加时LiDAR检测精度反而提升，原因是物体点云数量显著增加（雪堆积增大车辆表面积、镜面反射转为漫反射）带来的几何增强效应超过落雪噪声的负面影响，呼吁评测需解耦大气噪声与表面累积效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍将雪视为退化LiDAR性能的大气噪声，但这一假设未被在物体级别细粒度验证；粗粒度的天气严重程度分箱评测可能掩盖物体级相关因素，导致对检测器鲁棒性的结论具有误导性。

Method: 在WADS-3D与CADC数据集上进行细粒度评测，观察检测精度随降雪强度的变化趋势；通过消融实验与元信息分析（目标点云数量统计）分离落雪噪声与目标点数增加的贡献；提出物理解释假说：雪堆积增大车辆表面积、反射特性由镜面反射转向漫反射，从而提升目标点云密度。

Result: 检测精度随降雪强度增加而一致提升；落雪噪声虽存在负面影响，但被目标点云数量显著增加所主导；忽略物体级相关变量、仅按天气严重程度分箱评测会得出关于检测器鲁棒性的错误结论。

Conclusion: 准确评测需将大气噪声效应与表面累积效应解耦；未来工作应通过物理模型验证雪堆积与反射特性变化的假说；WADS-3D数据集已公开。

Abstract: Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) is critical for autonomous perception, yet its performance is known to degrade in adverse weather. While existing literature models snow as atmospheric noise that degrades performance, our granular evaluation on the WADS-3D and CADC datasets reveals a counter-intuitive phenomenon: detection accuracy consistently improves as snow intensity increases. Through ablation study and meta-information analysis, we reconcile this by confirming that while falling snow acts as noise, its negative impact is outweighed by a dominant, concurrent factor: significantly increased object point count. We hypothesize that this object point count boost acts as a geometric enhancement, driven by snow accumulation increasing vehicle surface area and a shift from specular to diffuse reflectance. Our findings demonstrate that evaluating perception systems using broad weather-severity bins without accounting for correlated object-level data can lead to misleading conclusions about detector robustness. We conclude that accurate evaluation requires decoupling atmospheric noise from surface accumulation effects and outline future research to validate these physical models. The WADS-3D dataset is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [122] [Where to Look Matters: Rethinking Sub-Volume Sampling in 3D Medical Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13915)
*Junkai Liu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将3D医学自监督预训练中的随机子体采样重新表述为"where-to-look"问题，提出解剖感知的离线粗到细探针库(plug-and-play)替代随机裁剪，lite版本用几何与图像统计线索即可稳定提升下游迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机子体积采样隐含假设所有空间区域价值相等，忽视了医学图像中的解剖先验与空间冗余。作者通过受控的crop-quality实验证明更优子体积带来更好下游迁移，从而揭示"观测策略"是3D医学SSL被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 提出plug-and-play框架，用离线粗到细探针库(probe bank)替换随机裁剪，其采样准则由解剖感知(anatomy-awareness)、信息量(informativeness)与多样性(diversity)三要素引导。实例化两个变体：轻量lite(几何+图像统计线索)与诊断性feat(冻结视觉特征)，用于对比低成本体积内在先验是否足够。

Result: 在多类3D医学SSL骨干网络与下游任务上，lite版本一致优于随机采样；机制分析将增益归因于更具解剖合理性且信息量更高的观测、空间冗余降低、更高效的学习动态和更结构化的表征。

Conclusion: 子体积采样策略是3D医学SSL的关键设计变量，简单的体积内在先验(lite)已足以带来稳健迁移增益，无需依赖昂贵语义特征；该框架可即插即用，为后续观测策略研究提供基线。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning on 3D medical images commonly relies on random sub-volume sampling during pretraining. However, random cropping implicitly treats all spatial regions as equally worth observing, despite anatomical priors and spatial redundancy that make regions inherently unequal in their value for representation learning. To this end, we first show through controlled crop-quality manipulations that better sub-volumes lead to better downstream transfer, revealing the observation policy as an overlooked bottleneck in 3D medical SSL. Motivated by this finding, we revisit sub-volume sampling as a \emph{where-to-look} problem. We propose \textbf{\method}, a plug-and-play framework that replaces random cropping with an offline coarse-to-fine probe bank guided by \textit{anatomy-awareness}, \textit{informativeness}, and \textit{diversity}. We instantiate \method as a lightweight \lite variant using geometric and image-statistical cues, and a diagnostic \feat variant using frozen visual features, to examine whether lightweight volume-intrinsic priors suffice or richer semantic cues reliably provide additional value. Extensive experiments across multiple 3D medical SSL backbones and downstream tasks demonstrate that \method-\lite consistently improves transfer performance over random sampling. Mechanistic analyses further attribute these gains to more anatomically plausible and informative observations, reduced spatial redundancy, more efficient learning dynamics, and more structured representations.

</details>


### [123] [Can machines reason non-verbally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13925)
*Shaheer U. Saeed,Zion Tse,Gregory Slabaugh,Daniel C. Alexander,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenVerOpt 提出一个将"生成—验证—优化"统一为推理闭环的通用框架，用于视觉非言语推理，在抽象IQ题、前列腺癌分割、肝血管重建、肿瘤边界对齐等任务上超越人类或专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器在语言/逻辑/方程求解上表现优异，但在视觉非言语推理上仍受限（如完成抽象图形、划分模糊兴趣区）。语言推理的中间步骤可用逻辑或自动测试验证，而视觉推理的中间步骤是隐式的、难以验证，因此难以建模，构成当前机器智能的根本瓶颈。

Method: 提出 GenVerOpt 通用框架，把生成、验证与优化整合为一条推理流水线：为每个样本生成候选解、验证其正确性、并迭代精化，实现逐样本（per-sample）级优化，而非仅依赖训练中学到的数据集级统计趋势；该过程模拟人类"设想多种可能—评估—精化"的解题方式。

Result: 在多个领域验证了超出既有系统的能力：在抽象IQ题上超越人类，在前列腺癌分割上达到临床医生水平，在肝血管重建上超过专家，在肿瘤边界对齐上优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: 机器推理可以超越语言，视觉非言语推理可作为更灵活、更可泛化智能的基础。

Abstract: Modern machines can solve equations, follow logic and reason through language, yet they still struggle with problems that require visual non-verbal reasoning. This is evident even in advanced systems such as DeepSeek, ChatGPT and Gemini, which remain limited on visual tasks such as completing abstract patterns or delineating ambiguous regions-of-interest. Unlike explicit intermediate steps in verbal reasoning which can be verified through logic or automated tests, visual reasoning involves implicit intermediate steps with no clear methods for verification. Non-verbal reasoning is therefore challenging to model and, as a result, remains a fundamental limitation of current machine intelligence. Here we introduce GenVerOpt, a general framework that unifies generation, verification and optimisation into a reasoning pipeline for machines. By generating candidate solutions, verifying their correctness, and iteratively refining them, GenVerOpt enables per-sample optimisation rather than relying solely on dataset-level trends captured during model training. This process mirrors human problem-solving where multiple possibilities are imagined, evaluated, and refined until a correct solution emerges. Across diverse domains, GenVerOpt demonstrates capabilities beyond existing systems: it surpasses humans on abstract IQ puzzles, matches clinicians in prostate cancer segmentation, exceeds experts in liver vessel reconstruction and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in tumour boundary alignment. These results show that machine reasoning can extend beyond language, establishing visual non-verbal reasoning as a foundation for more flexible and generalisable intelligence.

</details>


### [124] [Hide and Seek: HIDE Concepts to SEEK explanations for black box models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13935)
*Bhanu Tokas,Rahul Nair,Rockwell Jackson,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免模型权重、免概念标注数据的黑盒视觉模型概念级归因方法 Hide and Seek (HnS)，在 CelebA、COCO-Stuff 上优于现有方法，并扩展到图像描述与卫星图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的可解释性方法只能处理分类/回归任务，且需要模型权重或大规模概念标注数据集，大多无法给出图像级归因；对黑盒视觉模型的概念归因仍缺乏通用、可靠的手段。

Method: HnS 采用黑盒探测流程：给定概念与图像集，先用视觉-语言模型检索概念对齐图像，再在每张图中定位该概念对应区域并掩蔽，最后以原始图像与掩蔽图像之间模型输出的变化量作为该概念的归因分数，从而无需访问权重或标注数据即可获得图像级局部归因。

Result: 在 CelebA 与 COCO-Stuff 上，HnS 与真值归因的一致性优于现有概念归因方法；方法可适用于图像描述、卫星图像分割等此前无法使用概念归因的任务。

Conclusion: HnS 以行为测量而非内部权重访问实现忠实、局部化的概念归因，具备跨任务与跨模型的通用性，拓展了概念归因的适用范围。

Abstract: Interpreting machine learning model predictions is a central challenge, particularly for black-box vision models. Concept-based attribution methods interpret models using human-understandable concepts. Existing concept-based methods can only interpret classification and regression models. They require access to model weights or large concept-annotated datasets, and most methods cannot provide image-level attribution. We propose Hide and Seek (HnS), a new method for concept-level attribution that does not require access to model weights or labeled concept datasets. HnS can interpret a wide range of vision models. Given a concept and image set, HnS retrieves concept-aligned images using a vision-language model, localizes the concept within each image, masks the localized region, and measures attribution from the change in the black-box model's output between the original and masked images. This enables faithful attribution by directly measuring the model's behavior and supporting local attributions. We validated HnS on CelebA and COCO-Stuff. Compared to existing methods, HnS showed better agreement with ground-truth attributions. We demonstrated the applicability of HnS on diverse tasks, including image captioning and satellite image segmentation, where no existing concept attribution method can be used. We provided our anonymous code in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [125] [Images Talk Back: Guiding MMDiTs by Suppressing Image-to-Text Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13941)
*Yucheng Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Shusheng Yang,Jiakang Chen,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态扩散Transformer中图像到文本(I2T)反馈会固化(包括错误的)语义表示,据此提出Semantic Feedback Guidance(SFG)/iSFG,通过抑制I2T反馈、增强T2I条件来提升文生图对齐度与偏好分数,并迁移到视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMDiT中演进图像会通过联合注意力更新内部文本表示,再反向引导图像更新,这种I2T反馈究竟是纠正还是固化对提示词的偏离尚不清楚。作者通过干预实验检验该反馈机制的作用。

Method: 通过移植/抑制I2T消息的因果干预实验验证反馈作用;提出SFG:引导远离强化I2T反馈、削弱T2I条件的辅助分支;提出原地变体iSFG:直接抑制I2T反馈并增强T2I条件,无需额外模型评估;依据时间干预将SFG分支限制在采样前20%步数,约1.2倍基础NFE(无CFG)。

Result: 在三种图像生成骨干上,对齐度与偏好模型分数均提升;FLUX上SFG将GenEval从0.6661提至0.7479;SD3.5M上iSFG在零额外NFE下比CFG提升+1.33 HPSv2.1;控制方法可迁移到文生视频与图生视频,提升VBench分数。

Conclusion: I2T反馈倾向于保留演进图像中已存在的语义(含错误),抑制其影响可改善生成质量;SFG/iSFG是有效、低开销的无需训练的引导策略,具备跨骨干与跨模态泛化性。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs) allow the evolving image to update internal text representations through joint attention. These representations then guide subsequent image updates, raising a question: does this feedback help correct deviations from the prompt, or preserve them? We find that image-to-text (I2T) feedback can preserve the objects and attributes already represented in the evolving image, including errors. Transplanting I2T messages can rescue failures or disrupt successes. At matched intervention strengths, suppressing the influence of I2T feedback improves generation, whereas opposite controls degrade it. These findings motivate Semantic Feedback Guidance (SFG), which suppresses the influence of I2T feedback by guiding away from an auxiliary branch that strengthens this feedback and weakens text-to-image conditioning. The in-place variant, iSFG, directly suppresses I2T feedback and strengthens text-to-image conditioning, requiring no extra model evaluations. Temporal interventions motivate running the SFG branch during the first 20% of image sampling steps, requiring about $1.2\times$ the base NFE without classifier-free guidance (CFG). Experiments across three image-generation backbones show gains in alignment and preference-model scores. On FLUX, adding SFG to CFG raises GenEval from $0.6661$ to $0.7479$; on SD3.5M, iSFG adds $+1.33$ HPSv2.1 over CFG at zero extra NFE. The control also transfers to text-to-video and image-to-video generation, improving VBench scores. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [InstantFusion: A Shared Latent Interface for Heterogeneous Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13944)
*Xuanlang Dai,Jiazi Bu,Yujie Zhou,Yuhong Liu,Beichen Zhang,Penghui Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantFusion 提出一个轻量级共享潜在接口，通过模型专属 LAE 将异构图像生成器的去噪中间态投影到共享隐空间并解码到目标模型潜空间，实现跨模型轨迹切换与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配生成器架构与表示日益异构，但跨模型的隐表示对齐与去噪动态一致性研究不足。作者观察到独立训练的生成器存在相似的潜在空间组织，且未对齐模型间直接传递中间状态仍能保留粗粒度视觉内容，据此希望构建通用的跨模型状态翻译与轨迹衔接机制。

Method: 提出 InstantFusion：为每个生成器配备 Latent AutoEncoder（LAE），将各自中间去噪状态编码进共享隐空间，再解码到目标生成器的潜空间，实现状态翻译；训练时用两个互补目标约束共享空间：(1) 对齐同源图像在匹配噪声水平下的中间状态，(2) 同步对应的局部去噪速度场。训练完成后即可在冻结的生成器之间无缝切换去噪，无需为每个模型对单独训练映射。

Result: 在加速采样、多偏好组合、跨架构 on-policy 蒸馏以及跨模型组合四类任务上验证，均可获得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 跨模型潜在空间存在可被显式利用的结构性兼容，共享潜在接口可作为通用、免成对映射的跨异构生成器桥接方案，拓展扩散/流模型在加速、编辑与蒸馏等场景的组合能力。

Abstract: Diffusion/flow models have emerged as the de facto paradigms for image generation, giving rise to a diverse family of modern generators with increasingly heterogeneous architectures and representations. Despite their remarkable generation capabilities, the cross-model alignment of latent representations and the consistency of their underlying denoising dynamics remain underexplored. In this work, we uncover an intriguing cross-model compatibility: independently trained generators exhibit similar latent spatial organization, while directly handing off intermediate states between unaligned models can still preserve coarse visual content. Motivated by these observations, we propose InstantFusion, a lightweight shared latent interface that connects the denoising trajectories of heterogeneous image generators. Specifically, to enable flexible cross-model state translation, model-specific Latent AutoEncoders (LAEs) are introduced to project intermediate states from different generators into a shared latent space and decode them into the latent space of a target generator. This shared space is constrained by two complementary objectives: aligning intermediate states derived from the same image at matched noise levels and synchronizing their corresponding local denoising velocities. Once trained, InstantFusion allows denoising to seamlessly transition across frozen generators without requiring a dedicated mapping for every model pair. The versatility of InstantFusion is demonstrated across acceleration, multi-preference composition, cross-architecture on-policy distillation, and cross-model composition, achieving competitive performance across these diverse settings.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Physically Grounded Articulated 3D Assets: A Multi-Agent Pipeline and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13947)
*Xudong Cai,Ruijie Zhu,Haoliang Shang,Ruining Li,Marc Pollefeys,Shangzhe Wu,Andrea Vedaldi,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Artisan多智能体铰接式3D资产生成系统，通过物理仿真反馈实现无碰撞运动、重力稳定性与质量/材质属性，并构建四维度统一基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有铰接式3D生成方法（如Articraft）主要复现物体的外观与几何，仅保证物理上"看似合理"的关节结构，缺乏碰撞、重力稳定性、质量与材质等物理属性，难以生成真正可用、可运动、可功能化的资产。

Method: 在Articraft基础上扩展为多智能体流水线（multi-agent pipeline），引入物理仿真反馈闭环，使生成过程能优化碰撞-free运动、重力下稳定性以及准确的质量与材质参数；同时构建覆盖空间保真度、物理合理性、下游任务性能与总体质量的统一基准，含ArtVIP与LightWheel的100个参考资产、50条稳定性提示、50个带参考质量的ABO物体及多指标评测协议。

Result: 在超过10个代表性模型的广泛评测中，Artisan在空间保真度上具竞争力，并在所评测方法中取得最低碰撞率与最高稳定率。

Conclusion: 该系统与基准为3D生成向物理合理、功能可用的资产方向迈进提供了洞见与评测支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we study physically grounded articulated 3D asset generation---moving the goal from reproducing how objects look to capturing how they move and function. Inspired by the literature on procedural and agentic generation, we present Artisan, a system that extends the latest method Articraft by introducing a multi-agent pipeline with physical simulation feedback, enabling comprehensive attributes beyond geometry, appearance, and physically plausible articulation, such as collision-free motion, stability under gravity, and accurate mass and material properties. Furthermore, we construct a unified benchmark spanning four key aspects: spatial fidelity, physical plausibility, downstream task performance, and overall quality. The benchmark encompasses 100 reference assets from ArtVIP and LightWheel, 50 stability prompts, and 50 ABO objects with reference masses, with protocols evaluating various metrics. Through extensive evaluation of more than 10 representative models, Artisan achieves competitive spatial fidelity while attaining the lowest collision rates and highest stability rates among the evaluated methods. Together, our system and benchmark establish insights for advancing 3D generation towards physically plausible, functionally usable assets.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Fly-by-Code: Embodied Coding Agents for Aerial Manipulation with Active Visual and Physical Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12549)
*Jaewoo Lee,Jeongyeon Seo,Sihyun Cho,Gyeongrak Choe,Yutong Wang,Bavin Saravanan,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Sebastian Scherer,Guanya Shi,H. Jin Kim,Seungjae Lee,Dongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Active Feedback（AF）框架，让具身编码智能体在仿真中合成诊断程序，通过主动"查看"（View）未观测结果或"探测"（Probe）失败原因来弥补执行反馈的缺失，从而提升空中操作任务的成功判定与程序精化；仿真中假完成从25降至5、成功率从31.7%升至75%，并无需修改任务程序源码即可迁移至真实空中机械臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身编码智能体依赖执行反馈来判定任务是否成功并指导程序修正，但该假设在空中操作中不成立：载荷限制导致机载相机视野受限，关键结果落在视野之外（部分可观测）；强动态耦合与控制余量有限使物理交互脆弱（交互鲁棒性受限），失败原因无法区分。重复审视同一反馈无法恢复从未采集的观测或区分证据不足的失败原因。

Method: 提出Active Feedback（AF）主动"View and Probe"框架：当执行反馈留下未解决问题时，智能体合成独立的诊断程序并在仿真中运行，一方面从信息量更高的视点检查未观测结果（View），另一方面通过有针对性的物理交互检验失败原因假设（Probe）。

Result: 在六个仿真空中操作任务、每条件60次试验下，与相同编码智能体配置和相同任务执行上限的终端反馈相比，AF将假完成声明从25降至5，最终任务成功率从31.7%提升至75%；借助共享机器人API，在仿真中用AF精化的任务程序无需修改源码即可在真实空中机械臂上运行，并完成多阶段操作任务。

Conclusion: 可执行诊断能够为具身编码智能体填补执行反馈中的信息缺口，同时改善空中操作场景下的完成度评估与程序精化，并实现向真实平台的零源码修改迁移。

Abstract: Embodied coding agents construct robot policies from natural-language instructions by generating executable programs and refining them from execution feedback. This refinement assumes that execution feedback reveals whether the task succeeded and, after a failure, provides sufficient evidence to guide program revision. Aerial manipulation violates this assumption in two ways. Payload constraints restrict onboard camera coverage and leave task-critical outcomes outside the robot's view (partial observability), while strong dynamic coupling and limited control margins make physical interactions fragile (limited interaction robustness) and leave failure causes unresolved. Re-examining the same feedback cannot recover observations that were never collected or separate failure causes that the available evidence does not distinguish. To acquire this missing evidence, we introduce Active Feedback (AF), an active "View and Probe" framework. When execution feedback leaves unresolved questions, the agent synthesizes a separate diagnostic program that runs in simulation to inspect unseen outcomes from informative viewpoints (View) or test hypotheses about failure causes through targeted physical interactions (Probe). Across six simulated aerial manipulation tasks with 60 trials per condition, AF reduces false completion declarations from 25 to 5 and raises final task success from 31.7% to 75% compared with terminal feedback under the same coding-agent configuration and task-execution limit. Using shared robot APIs, task programs refined with AF in simulation run on a physical aerial manipulator without task-program source-code modification and complete multi-stage manipulation tasks in the real world. These results show that executable diagnosis fills gaps in execution feedback for embodied coding agents, improving both completion assessment and program refinement in aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [129] [PanTact: Teaching Whole-Body Dexterity with a Wearable Contact Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12558)
*Xiaomeng Xu,Han Zhang,Zhian Ruan,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Ruihan Yang,Carmelo Sferrazza,Shuran Song,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PanTact 通过机器人无关的人体演示学习人形机器人全身灵巧操作：用可穿戴模块化接触贴片采集全身接触位置与状态，训练视觉运动策略预测全身接触目标，再用接触感知全身控制实现身体支撑、推、扫、分担负载等技能，在 Unitree G1 上无需额外接触传感器完成部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人 loco-manipulation 研究多把操作局限在手部，避免身体其他部位与环境接触，浪费了人形机器人多自由度与多接触面的硬件潜力。人体演示天然包含全身接触操作，但存在人-机具身差异与自我中心视角观测鸿沟，如何将这种能力迁移到机器人是核心挑战。

Method: 以"接触"为跨具身可迁移的操作中心表示，接触位置与时机指定任务交互需求，机器人自行决定身体协调方式。三个组件：1) 机器人无关的可穿戴数据采集界面，在自我中心视觉与双手 UMI 夹爪基础上增加全身模块化接触贴片，记录身体接触位置与状态；2) 视觉运动策略，缓解自我中心观测鸿沟并预测全身接触目标；3) 接触感知的全身控制公式，用合适的机器人表面实现主动接触目标。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上部署，无需额外身体接触传感器，展示了包括身体支撑、推动、扫掠、负载分担在内的全身灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: 以接触为中间表示，可将无机器人的人体全身操作演示迁移到人形机器人，实现超越手部的全身灵巧操作，为 loco-manipulation 提供可扩展的数据与策略范式。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have many degrees of freedom and contact surfaces, yet most loco-manipulation research restricts manipulation to the hands and avoids other body contacts with the environment. To extend manipulation beyond the hands, we present PanTact, a system that learns humanoid whole-body dexterity, the capability to manipulate with any suitable body surface, directly from robot-free human demonstrations. Our key idea is to use contact as manipulation-centric information that transfers across embodiments. Contact locations and timing specify the interactions needed to accomplish a task, while the robot chooses how to coordinate its body to realize them within its own physical constraints. Specifically, PanTact captures, learns, and executes whole-body contact via: 1) a wearable, robot-free data collection interface that augments egocentric vision and bimanual UMI grippers with whole-body modular contact patches to record body contact locations and states; 2) a visuomotor policy that mitigates the egocentric observation gap and predicts whole-body contact goals; and 3) contact-aware whole-body control formulations that realize active contact goals using suitable robot surfaces. We deploy PanTact on a Unitree G1 humanoid without additional body contact sensors, demonstrating whole-body dexterity involving body support, pushing, sweeping, and load-sharing. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [130] [What Should Embodied Agents Remember? Benchmarking and Evaluating Memory Modality for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12561)
*Junxiang Wang,Yuchen Liu,Yi Wu,Guofei Chen,Nir Pechuk,Tao Chen,Zackory Erickson,Yuzhe Qin,Tianyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MeMoREval 基准，通过"先体验、后作答"的受控协议研究具身智能体的记忆模态选择及其推理成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体可用的记忆模态多样（文本记录、帧、场景图等），不同模态在信息量与构建/使用时的推理开销上存在权衡，但缺乏系统化评测手段。

Method: 构建含 53 个任务、4 个仿真室内环境的基准；智能体先无任务先验地经历事件序列，随后仅凭记忆回答信息与动作请求；同时度量任务成功率与记忆构建/使用的推理成本，并扩展到经验累积、选择性帧保留、视觉输入写入记忆等设置。

Result: 参考评测显示：存储帧在主模型下以更低写入成本优于生成式描述；存储帧加场景图在所有测试模型上取得最高综合任务成功率。另有两项物理机器人演示验证协议可迁移至真实世界。

Conclusion: MeMoREval 为记忆设计（模态选择、成本权衡、经验累积策略）提供了可控可扩展的评测框架，并初步证明其不限于仿真。

Abstract: Memory allows large language model (LLM) agents to use past experience, often stored as textual records, when responding to requests. For embodied agents that control robots, visual and spatial observations introduce a broader choice of memory modalities, affecting both the information available for subsequent tasks and the model inference costs of constructing and using memory. We introduce MeMoREval, a benchmark for studying this problem through a controlled experience-request protocol. Agents first experience a sequence of events without knowing the subsequent tasks, and subsequently they use memory to respond to information and action requests without receiving new observations. The benchmark comprises 53 tasks across four simulated indoor environments and measures task success alongside the inference costs of memory construction and use. Our reference evaluations reveal that storing frames outperforms storing generated descriptions at lower write-time inference cost for the primary model, while storing frames with scene graphs achieves the highest aggregate task success for every tested model. Extensions to accumulated experience, selective frame retention, and visual input to memory writers demonstrate how the benchmark accommodates further memory-design investigations. Two physical-robot demonstrations establish the feasibility of applying its protocol beyond simulation.

</details>


### [131] [CASTER: Formalizing Zero-Shot Manipulation from Synthetic Videos as Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12567)
*Yinlong Dai,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将合成视频生成到真实机器人轨迹的过程形式化为约束优化问题：用视频中的物体轨迹作为初始解，并在数字孪生中迭代精化以满足物理可行性与语义一致性；实验证明去除现有方法的简化假设后，零样本操作任务成功率提升34.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用视频生成模型生成任务合成视频可避免真实人类演示，且视频处于机器人当前环境；但生成视频缺乏物理一致性与机器人接地（robot grounding），无法直接rollout，因此需要一套从合成视频到真实可执行轨迹的严格方法论。

Method: 将问题建模为约束优化：从合成视频中提取物体轨迹作为初始猜测，在数字孪生等环境中以物理可行性和语义一致性为约束进行局部精化。理论上给出成功条件——视频生成必须"概率性地"把搜索初始化在语义吸引域内，使轨迹局部修改不丢失任务对齐；并指出已有SOTA方法可视为该统一框架的带简化版本。

Result: 通过去除这些简化（更贴近底层优化问题），零样本轨迹更鲁棒，平均任务成功率相比最优基线提升34.1%。

Conclusion: 合成视频到真实机器人轨迹可统一为受约束优化，视频生成的核心要求是在语义吸引域内提供初始解；更忠实的优化实现带来显著的零样本操作增益。

Abstract: Prior research on learning from humans often requires real human demonstrations. But advances in video generation models offer a new paradigm: we can now prompt these generators to make synthetic videos of the task. On the plus side, these synthetic videos take place in the robot's current environment, and do not require actual human teaching. However, while the generated videos appear to show plausible motions, they lack both physical consistency and robot grounding, and thus the robot cannot directly roll out the trajectory shown by the video. In this paper we seek to formalize the process of going from synthetic video generation to real-world robot trajectories for zero-shot manipulation tasks. We recognize that this problem is an instance of constrained optimization. The object trajectories extracted from the synthetic video provide an initial guess at the correct motion, and the robot must refine these trajectories (for instance, within a digital twin) while enforcing physical feasibility and semantic consistency. Our optimization-based formalism provides insights into the video generation properties necessary for success. Specifically, we show that the video generation must probabilistically seed the search within a semantic basin of attraction where the trajectory can be locally modified without losing its task alignment. Our analysis further reveals that state-of-the-art approaches can be viewed as instances of our unified formalism with key simplifications. In our experiments we remove these simplifications to more closely match the underlying optimization, leading to more robust zero-shot trajectories from synthetic videos and improving average task success by 34.1% compared to the best-performing baseline. See videos at our project website: this https URL

</details>


### [132] [Graph-Based Heuristics for Minimum Constraint Removal in Cluttered Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12609)
*Antony Thomas,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Marco Baglietto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于图的启发式流水线求解最小约束移除（MCR）问题，结合双向可行集生成与缩减MCR图上的定长搜索，并以扩展可行集生成器补全最优解集缺失情形，在效率与解质量间取得折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱人类环境中作业的人形与服务机器人，若将所有周边物体视为固定障碍，运动规划可能无解。MCR问题通过移除最少约束（如可移动物体）来获得无碰撞路径，但最优MCR求解是NP-hard，需要实用的近似方法。

Method: 提出图启发式流水线：将MCR建模为图结构，通过双向可行集生成缩减问题规模，再在缩减后的MCR图上执行固定长度搜索；同时推导扩展可行集生成器，用于首个生成器产生的候选集合不含完整最优移除集的情形。

Result: 在六个基准上，流水线在三个案例中与精确解一致；其余案例最多比最优多选31.25%的障碍物，速度比精确搜索快3.4至33.7倍。某杂乱环境操作任务中，选择2个而非7个遮挡物体，使MCR、逆运动学与运动规划总时间从84.72 s降至30.59 s。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算效率与解质量之间提供了实用折中，适用于可移动障碍物环境下的运动规划，但结论基于基准与单一操作任务，尚未体现实机部署或泛化验证。

Abstract: Humanoid and service robots operating in clut- tered human environments may encounter motion planning problems that are infeasible when all surrounding objects are treated as fixed obstacles. The Minimum Constraint Removal problem, which we refer to as MCR, addresses this situation by identifying the minimum number of constraints, such as movable objects, whose removal enables a collision-free path. However, computing an optimal MCR solution is NP-hard. In this work, we propose a graph-based heuristic pipeline that combines bidirectional feasible-set generation with fixed- length searches over reduced MCR graphs. We further derive an extended feasible-set generator for instances in which the candidates produced by the first generator do not contain a complete optimal removal set. Across six benchmarks, the evaluated pipeline matches the exact solution in three cases and, in the remaining cases, selects at most 31.25% more obstacles than the optimum, while being between 3.4 and 33.7 times faster than exact search. In one of the benchmarks, involving a manipulation task amidst clutter, selecting two rather than seven obstructing objects reduces the combined MCR, inverse- kinematics, and motion planning time from 84.72 s to 30.59 s. These results indicate that the proposed approach provides a practical compromise between computational efficiency and solution quality for motion planning among movable obstacles.

</details>


### [133] [Image-Based Path-Planning for Navigation of Soft-Growing Robots in the Spinal Sub-Arachnoid Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12631)
*George Dimas,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Panagiotis Vartholomeos,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图像的3D路径规划算法（A*扩展），用于软体生长机器人在脊髓蛛网膜下腔（SSS）内导航，兼顾安全路径与锚点设置以支持机器人转向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人手术需在人体特定部位进行微创操作或给药，需专用路径规划算法在保护脆弱组织的同时满足软体机器人导航的运动学与转向需求。

Method: 将A*算法扩展为图像驱动的3D路径规划方法，在高度精细的SSS解剖模型上估计安全路径，并显式引入锚点（anchor points）以辅助软体生长机器人的转向控制。

Result: 在精细SSS模型上评估，验证了算法在满足机器人运动要求与路径效率方面的能力（摘要未给出量化指标）。

Conclusion: 该A*扩展方法可为软体生长机器人在蛛网膜下腔导航提供安全且满足转向需求的路径，属于医疗软体机器人专用规划工具。

Abstract: Recently, soft robotic procedures are being developed aiming at achieving targeted minimally invasive operations or drug administration in specific locations in the human body. Accordingly, specialized path-planning algorithms need to be investigated for the estimation of efficient paths that would protect the delicate tissue structures and simultaneously satisfy a set of requirements for the efficient navigation of the robot. In this context, we propose an image-based 3D pathplanning algorithm, which is an extension of the A* algorithm, for the navigation of a soft-growing robot inside the spinal subarachnoid space (SSS). The proposed algorithm is capable of estimating a safe pathway towards the goal location, while ensuring the establishment of anchor points that facilitate the efficient steering of the soft robot. The algorithm is evaluated using a highly detailed model of the SSS with respect to its capacity to satisfy the robot movement requirements and path efficiency.

</details>


### [134] [Selectively Actuated Fiberbots for High-precision Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12636)
*Jinshi Zhao,Libaihe Tian,Alex Ranne,Qindong Zheng,Yue Li,Xiaotong Guo,Ziqi Yang,Ali Anil Demircali,Mohamed E. M. K. Abdelaziz,Bruno Gil Rosa,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena,Burak Temelkuran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种电热驱动的光纤机器人，将焦耳热集中于远端主动段，实现微创手术中1.2米长、1.9毫米直径器械的亚100微米级远端精确操控，并可穿过软式内镜工作通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术依赖细长器械，但当驱动分布于整个器械长度时，远端精确操控变得困难，亟需将驱动集中在远端以提升精度与可控性。

Method: 采用热拉伸工艺制备光纤，内嵌金属导线作为电热驱动单元，使焦耳热集中于远端主动段；光纤直径1.9 mm、长1.2 m，可操控手术激光光纤；器件紧凑可穿过软式内镜工作通道，且与内镜机械解耦以实现独立运动；并在体模实验中与da Vinci器械联合演示。

Result: 机器人光纤对手术激光光纤的定位精度优于100 μm；可穿过软式内镜工作通道并保持与内镜机械解耦；体模演示验证了与现有内窥镜系统的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该光纤机器人实现了精确的远端运动，有望通过提升微创手术精度改善临床结果，并具备与现有内镜系统集成的潜力。

Abstract: Long, slender instruments are essential for minimally invasive surgery (MIS), but achieving precise distal manipulation becomes increasingly difficult when actuation extends along the entire instrument. Here, we present an electrothermally actuated fiber-based robot designed for MIS procedures, in which Joule heating is concentrated in the distal active segment. The fiber is fabricated using a thermal drawing process and uses embedded metallic wires for thermal actuation. This robotic fiber, 1.9 mm in diameter and 1.2 m in length, can precisely manipulate surgical laser fibers with positional precision below 100 $\mu$m. The compact size of the fiber robot allows it to pass through the working channel of a flexible endoscope, while keeping both devices mechanically uncoupled to allow independent motion. A phantom demonstration combining the robotic fiber with a da Vinci instrument conceptually demonstrated its compatibility with existing endoscopic systems for clinical applications. Our robotic fiber enables precise distal motion and holds the potential to improve clinical outcomes by enhancing precision in MIS procedures.

</details>


### [135] [Distributed Harmonic-Covariance Feedback for Hole Closure in Planar Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12637)
*Jitian Liu,Subhrajit Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对感知圆盘构成时变Čech复形的平面多智能体系统，提出基于调和协方差的反馈律：用调和边信号定位孔洞并将其转化为智能体力，借助正则化Hodge Laplacian的固定次多项式经局部算子作用实现分布式计算，并给出洞面积累积量的离线有理下界及收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体覆盖/编队中"孔洞"（未被感知覆盖的区域）需要被检测并消除，但孔洞随时间演化且只能通过局部感知获知。作者希望在仅依赖局部信息、无全局轨迹查询的前提下，既实现分布式孔洞闭合控制，又能对累积孔洞面积给出可计算、可验证的界与收敛保证。

Method: 将感知圆盘形成的时变Čech复形作为拓扑载体，构造基于调和边信号的协方差反馈控制律：从Hodge Laplacian核空间（调和信号）中提取孔洞位置信息，映射为智能体受力。分布式实现采用正则化Hodge Laplacian的固定次数多项式，通过局部算子迭代施加，避免全局通信。对模型数据与初值给出任意精度的逼近程序及显式误差界。在孔面积可测有界、孔洞连续演化不分裂不合并、仅有有限次临时新孔的假设下，离线有限计算给出时间域上累积孔面积的有理下界。

Result: 证明闭环系统从任意安全初始状态出发存在唯一全局轨迹；离线有限计算所得有理下界无需查询轨迹数据，且随计算收敛到真实累积孔面积（有限或无限）。若累积面积有限，则孔洞在时间平均意义下闭合：任意固定长度时间窗内的平均归一化孔面积随t→∞趋于零。15秒数值演示中所有孔洞在9.9s时闭合并此后保持闭合。

Conclusion: 该工作把代数拓扑（Hodge/调和信号）与分布式多智能体控制结合，为时变拓扑下的孔洞检测与闭合提供了带理论保证的局部反馈方案与可验证的面积界；验证限于数值仿真，未涉及物理机器人平台。

Abstract: We consider hole closure in a planar multi-agent system whose sensing disks form a time-varying Čech complex.
