We propose a harmonic-covariance feedback that uses harmonic edge signals to locate holes and converts those signals into agent forces. For distributed evaluation, we use a fixed-degree polynomial of the regularized Hodge Laplacian. This polynomial is evaluated through local operator applications. The closed loop has a unique global trajectory from every safe initial state. The model data and initial state are supplied with procedures for approximation to any requested accuracy with explicit error bounds. We assume that hole area is measurable and bounded, holes evolve continuously without splitting or merging, and only finitely many temporary new holes appear. Under these conditions, offline finite calculations produce rational lower bounds for the cumulative hole area on the time horizon without querying trajectory data. These bounds converge to that cumulative area, whether finite or infinite. If it is finite, holes close in the time-averaged sense: the average normalized hole area over every fixed-length time window tends to zero as time tends to infinity. In a 15-second numerical demo, all holes close by 9.9s and remain closed through the end.

</details>


### [136] [PriMoRetarget: Humanoid Motion Transfer Through Prioritized Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12642)
*Nicholas Ioannidis,Dun Yang,Max Smith,Tue Vu,Ziyu Zhang,Shayegan Omidshafiei,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PriMoRetarget，用显式优先级层级替代加权多目标优化来做运动重定向；低优先级更新受约束以保留高优先级目标（如足部落地、交互接触）。在四个数据集（单角色、人-物操作、人-人交互）上验证，任务相关交互保持更好，参考轨迹更易被跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类运动数据迁移到人形机器人时，多角色/多物体交互引入额外接触与空间关系需要保持。现有方法多对单角色有效，且普遍采用加权多目标优化，目标冲突需调权重，仍可能损害关键运动特征（如足部接触、交互接触）。

Method: PriMoRetarget：优先级运动重定向框架。用显式优先级层级替代相对目标加权；低优先级（如姿态目标）更新被约束在保留高优先级目标（足部放置、交互接触）的可行域内，形成分层/受限优化，避免权重调参。

Result: 在覆盖单角色运动、角色-物体操作、角色-角色交互的四类数据集上评估。相比广泛使用的重定向方法，生成动作更好地保留了任务相关交互，且产生的参考轨迹可被更可靠地跟踪。

Conclusion: 用优先级层级替代加权求和能更稳健地处理重定向中的多目标冲突，尤其利于接触与交互的关键特征保持，为人形机器人提供更可跟踪的参考轨迹。

Abstract: Motion retargeting is a critical preprocessing step for transferring human motion data to humanoid robots. While existing methods are effective for single-character motions, interactions involving multiple characters and objects introduce additional contacts and spatial relationships that must be preserved. Retargeting is commonly formulated as a weighted multi-objective optimization problem, where conflicting objectives can require careful tuning of relative weights and can still compromise important motion characteristics. We introduce PriMoRetarget, a prioritized motion-retargeting framework that replaces relative objective weighting with an explicit priority hierarchy. Lower-priority updates are constrained to preserve higher-priority objectives, allowing critical characteristics such as foot placement and interaction contacts to take precedence over lower-priority pose objectives. The method is evaluated on four datasets spanning single-character motion, character-object manipulation, and character-character interactions. The resulting motions better preserve task-relevant interactions and provide reference trajectories that can be tracked more reliably than those produced by widely used retargeting methods.

</details>


### [137] [Robust and Scalable UAV-UGV Planning for Aerial Monitoring under Energy and Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12670)
*Cahit Ikbal Er,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多UAV-UGV协同空中监测任务，提出多项式时间规划算法，联合优化车辆路径与时序（允许减速以满足访问顺序约束），在UAV续航与有界行程时间扰动下最小化任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV受续航限制，需UGV作为移动充电站接驳；监测点存在访问顺序约束，且执行中行程时间存在有界扰动。既有工作多固定时序或分别处理路径与调度，未联合决策路径与时序，导致顺序约束下解空间受限、鲁棒性不足。

Method: 建模为含顺序约束的NP-hard规划问题；以"续航=满电最大飞行时间"刻画UAV能量约束，UGV承担充电与转运；联合求解多UAV-UGV车队的路径与时序，允许通过减速（延长行程时间）满足访问顺序；设计多项式时间算法生成高效解，并给出可行性保证与复杂度分析，最后数值对比评估。

Result: 给出多项式时间算法，建立了可行性保证并刻画了计算复杂度；在NP-hard问题下仍能高效生成解；数值实验与对比验证了算法性能（在有界行程时间扰动下兼顾顺序约束与续航限制）。

Conclusion: 该工作将路径规划与时序调度统一到UAV-UGV协同监测框架中，借助减速自由度实现顺序可行性并应对有界扰动，提供了具有可行性保证的多项式近似/构造性方法。

Abstract: We consider planning for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) teams in aerial monitoring missions with ordering requirements on the monitoring points to be visited by the UAV. The system consists of multiple UAV-UGV teams, each comprising one UAV and one UGV. UAV energy limitations are modeled through endurance, defined as the maximum flight time on a full battery. The UGVs serve as mobile charging stations and transport the UAVs between consecutive aerial tours. Given a set of monitoring points subject to ordering constraints and the teams' initial and terminal positions, the objective is to minimize mission completion time while satisfying UAV energy limits and the required visit order under bounded travel-time disturbances that may arise during execution. Unlike prior work, the plan jointly determines vehicle routes and their timing, allowing teams to slow down as needed to satisfy the ordering constraints. We develop a polynomial-time algorithm that generates efficient solutions to the underlying NP-hard planning problem. We establish feasibility guarantees, characterize the algorithm's computational complexity, and evaluate its performance via numerical comparisons.

</details>


### [138] [Non-Prehensile Multi-Object Placement with Mobile Robots via Diffusion-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12684)
*Lance Pharand,Megnath Ramesh,Mark Chiu,Yash Vardhan Pant,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向移动机器人多物体精密推动任务的扩散规划器，通过"机器人去噪步+以当前干净机器人估计为条件的物体去噪步"的因果分解实现多模态采样，并设计推动专用代价用于引导与 best-of-N 推理，在 Push-T、多箱仓库场景与 TurtleBot 3 Burger 真机上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非抓取（non-prehensile）推动研究多聚焦粗糙目标区域放置，精密放置则主要依赖固定基座机械臂且使用全向末端执行器、可从任意方向再接近物体（如 Push-T）。移动机器人无法自由换向，纠正同样的放置误差需绕物体做大幅重定位机动，因此多物体、紧公差推动成为带约束的多目标分配问题，现有方法难以兼顾成功率与效率。

Method: 将移动机器人多物体推动形式化为紧公差下的多目标分配问题；提出因果分解的扩散规划器：每个机器人去噪步后接一个以当前干净机器人估计为条件的物体去噪步，从而在单条机器人计划下采样多个物体结果，捕捉推动的多模态性；设计一组移动机器人推动专用代价，同时用于推理时的引导（guidance）与 best-of-N 采样。

Result: Push-T 上成功率高于所有对比方法且不合理物体运动比例最低；在三个类仓库多箱推动环境中，相比无引导基线将放置成功率提升 21.5 个百分点，重规划速度比联合去噪两条轨迹的规划器快 1.8 倍；在 TurtleBot 3 Burger 真机上对训练集未出现的物体形状放置成功率达 85.0%，而 Diffusion Policy 仅 37.5%。

Conclusion: 因果分解的引导式扩散规划能有效应对移动机器人紧公差多物体推动任务，兼顾多模态建模、规划效率与真机部署的泛化能力，验证了推动专用代价与 best-of-N 推理的有效性。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation with mobile robots has primarily targeted placement in coarse goal regions. Precision placement has been studied with manipulators on benchmarks such as Push-T, where an omnidirectional end effector can readily re-approach an object from any side. For a mobile robot, correcting the same placement errors may require wide repositioning maneuvers around the object. We formalize multi-object pushing with a mobile robot as a multi-target assignment problem at tight tolerances. We propose a diffusion planner with a causal factorization: each robot denoising step is followed by an object step conditioned on the current clean robot estimate. This factorization allows us to sample multiple object outcomes for a single robot plan, capturing the multi-modality of pushing. We also design a set of pushing-specific costs for mobile robots, used for both guidance and best-of-$N$ sampling at inference. On Push-T, our planner attains the highest success rate of the methods we compare, while having the lowest rate of implausible object motion. Across three multi-box pushing environments resembling warehouse scenarios, it raises the fraction of boxes placed by $21.5$ percentage points over an unguided baseline, and replans $1.8\times$ faster than a planner that denoises the two trajectories together. Lastly, we deploy the guided planner on a TurtleBot 3 Burger with object shapes absent from the training data, where it places $85.0\%$ of objects within tolerance against Diffusion Policy's $37.5\%$.

</details>


### [139] [Spa-Bench: A Real-Robot Benchmark for Spatial Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12696)
*Justin Tien-Smith,Matthew Pidden,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出真实机器人空间理解基准 Spa-Bench，覆盖六类桌面空间任务，对比 ID/OOD 空间配置并设置四种诊断条件，发现 VLA 能执行操作但空间接地不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 评测多关注物体、指令或环境变化下的总体成功率，未澄清策略能否可靠地把空间描述接地到物理场景，即"知道怎么做"与"知道对哪里做"的分离问题。

Method: 在 SO-101 机械臂上构建 Spa-Bench 真实机器人基准，含六类桌面任务（Object State、Relative Size、Referential Description、Relational Placement、Ordinal Position、Counting）；核心对比为保留物体/概念/动作支持的 ID 与 OOD 空间配置，另设四种诊断条件探测物理执行、措辞敏感性、物体-任务关联与"无需动作"识别；发布 4,092 条 episode（1,200 条人类遥操作演示 + 2,892 条五个 SOTA VLA 策略的真实机器人 rollout）。

Result: 三个完整评测的策略在 ID 空间配置上成功率 67.5-73.3%，在 OOD 空间配置上降至 40.7-52.0%；匹配的直接指令可将成功率提升 28.3-40.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前 VLA 通常能完成所需操作动作，但缺乏鲁棒的空间接地能力；评测范式应显式分离操作能力与空间语言理解。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) offer a promising route toward general-purpose robotics by transferring semantic knowledge from large-scale vision-language pretraining into language-conditioned control. However, most VLA evaluations emphasize aggregate manipulation success under changes in objects, instructions, or environments, leaving unclear how reliably these policies ground spatial descriptions in the physical scenes where they act. We therefore ask a sharper question: how reliably can current VLAs translate spatial descriptions into appropriate real-robot behavior? To investigate this, we introduce Spa-Bench, a real-robot benchmark that evaluates spatial grounding across six tabletop tasks covering Object State, Relative Size, Referential Description, Relational Placement, Ordinal Position, and Counting. Our primary evaluation compares in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) spatial configurations while retaining training support for the relevant objects, concepts, and actions, reducing object and motor unfamiliarity as alternative explanations for failure. Four diagnostic conditions further probe physical execution, sensitivity to wording, object-task associations, and recognition that no action is required. We release 4,092 episodes collected on the SO-101 robot arm: 1,200 human-teleoperated demonstrations and 2,892 real-robot evaluation rollouts from five state-of-the-art VLA policies. Across the three fully evaluated policies, success falls from 67.5-73.3% on ID spatial configurations to 40.7-52.0% on OOD spatial configurations, while matched direct instructions improve success by 28.3-40.0 percentage points. These results show that the evaluated VLAs can often execute the required manipulations but lack robust spatial grounding. Project materials, including videos, code, and benchmark data, are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [FallGown: A Soft Pneumatic Wearable Device for Fall Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12706)
*Megan C. Coram,Haoran Lei,Steven H. Collins,Steve B. Cousins,Michelle Baldonado,Cynthia Sung,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FallGown是一种内置充气气动软体支撑结构的可穿戴防跌倒装置，可承载使用者全身体重，便携（6.9 kg、0.051 m³）、拖曳阻力低（7.24–20.8 N）、穿脱快（<2.5 min / <0.5 min），12气室、1.4 m底径裙摆配底座框架可阻止除一次机械故障外的所有跌倒，并可通过板载压力传感器感知体重分布以支持监测训练与自适应支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人因担心跌倒及受伤而回避步行、舞蹈、太极等有益健康的运动；现有被动的助行器需手臂支撑，主动的刚性外骨骼与机器人框架笨重、体积大且带污名，使用受限。因此需要一种柔软、便携、不显眼且能真正防止跌倒的可穿戴支撑装置，同时创造可监测运动与自适应支持的机会。

Method: 设计为软体可穿戴支撑结构：织物外壳内嵌加压气动气室（pneumatic pouches），可支撑使用者全身体重；可选滚动底座降低与地面摩擦。通过测量存储体积、质量、拖曳力、穿脱时间评估便携性与负担；通过单用户跌倒预防测试，比较气室数量、裙摆底径等结构尺寸对稳定性与倾覆的影响。

Result: 装置重6.9 kg、收纳体积0.051 m³；拖曳力7.24–20.8 N（取决于地面类型及是否使用可选滚动底座）；平均穿戴<2.5 min、脱下<0.5 min。滚动底座降低摩擦但牺牲便携性与裙摆形变能力（该形变有助于穿门及狭窄空间）。跌倒测试显示装置尺寸与抗倾覆稳定性之间存在权衡：12个气动气室、底径1.4 m的裙摆加底座框架可阻止除一次机械故障外的全部跌倒。板载压力传感器可感知体重分布。

Conclusion: 软体可穿戴支撑结构（FallGown）在便携性、低负担、易穿脱方面具备可行性与初步防跌倒能力（12气室、1.4 m底径加底座框架基本阻止全部跌倒），并通过压力传感为监测训练与自适应支持提供可能；未来需解决尺寸—稳定性权衡及机械可靠性。

Abstract: Concerns about falling and potential resulting injuries prevent many older adults from participating in forms of health-improving exercise such as walking, dance, and tai chi. Existing passive devices like walkers require use of the arms for support, and active devices like exoskeletons and robotic frames are often rigid, bulky, and stigmatized, which limits their use. The FallGown is a soft wearable support structure consisting of pressurized pneumatic pouches within a fabric shell; it can support the full weight of a user's body and aims to prevent falls. It is portable (6.9 kg and 0.051 m3 when stored), minimally burdensome (7.24-20.8 N drag force depending on floor type and whether an optional rolling base frame is used), and convenient (less than 2.5 minutes on average to don and less than 0.5 minutes on average to doff). The optional rolling base lowers friction with the floor, but reduces portability and skirt deformation (which can be useful to fit through doors and other tight spaces). Fall prevention testing with a single user revealed a tradeoff between device size and stability against tipping; a skirt with 12 pneumatic pouches and a floor diameter of 1.4 meters with a base frame prevented all falls except in one case with a mechanical failure. On-board pressure sensors can sense the user's weight distribution, providing an opportunity for monitored exercise and adaptive support.

</details>


### [141] [REMM: Revisable Embodied Memory for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12710)
*Revanth Krishna Senthilkumaran,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Alessandro Oltramari,Jonathan Francis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REMM：为冻结的G0.5 VLA执行器提供可修订的具身记忆层，将只增证据历史与可修订任务状态分离，通过依赖回溯撤回失效结论并在技能边界触发定向恢复，物理试验中将干预恢复进度由57%提升至91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作需要机器人在扰动后保留任务相关历史，同时撤销不再被物理证据支持的结论。仅保留观测无法确定哪些已完成条件应被撤回、哪些义务需要修复，因此需要一个显式的可修订记忆机制。

Method: REMM将只增的证据历史与可修订的任务状态分离，并把派生关系与已完成条件显式链接到其支持事实；当新证据改变某支持事实时，撤回依赖结论，并在经验证的技能边界安排定向恢复，且不更新底层执行器。

Result: 51次物理试验（含操作员辅助运行）中，干预恢复的平均记录任务进度由最强基线的57%提升至91%，序列召回由43%提升至70%；视频诊断显示保留历史更好地落地到下一指令，并与π0.5兼容的高层规划器变体进行了对比。

Conclusion: 可修订记忆可作为持续操作的有效接口，无需策略再训练即可在冻结VLA执行器上实现扰动恢复与序列召回。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires robots to retain task-relevant history while revising conclusions no longer supported by physical evidence. Retaining observations alone does not specify which completed conditions should be withdrawn after a disturbance or which obligations require repair. We introduce REMM, Revisable Embodied Memory for Manipulation, a supervisory memory layer for a frozen G0.5 vision-language-action executor. REMM separates an append-only evidence history from revisable task state and explicitly links derived relations and completed conditions to their supporting facts. When accepted evidence changes a supporting fact, it withdraws dependent conclusions and schedules targeted recovery at verified skill boundaries without updating the executor. Across 51 physical trials, including operator-assisted runs, REMM improves mean recorded task progress over the strongest baseline from 57% to 91% in intervention recovery and from 43% to 70% in sequence recall. Complementary video diagnostics show improved grounding of retained history into the next instruction, including comparisons with $\pi_{0.5}$-compatible high-level planner adaptations. These results support revisable memory as a practical interface for continued manipulation without policy retraining. Project: this https URL .

</details>


### [142] [Visual-Wi-Fi Navigation to Signal-Emitting Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12733)
*Lihao Wang,Tianjian Xu,Tianmin Shu,Renjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出WiFiSpaces仿真器（在Habitat中加入在线光线追踪Wi-Fi感知，可在策略rollout中对连续位姿渲染Wi-Fi观测）以及ViFiNav导航框架（在线源定位算法利用机器人运动融合多视角方位测量，学习策略将置信度感知的源估计与深度观测融合）。实验表明利用运动对定位至关重要，仿真微调提升成功率，策略可零样本迁移至未见数据集，并在真实机器人（商用路由器Wi-Fi传感器）上无真实训练部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内导航到目标在目标小、被遮挡或视觉不显著时仍困难；而智能家电、移动设备和IoT标签在日常通信中发射Wi-Fi信号，提供了普遍但未被充分利用的空间线索。作者研究视觉-Wi-Fi导航：机器人利用机载视觉观测与旁听（不连接设备、不修改基础设施）的Wi-Fi包，在未建图环境中到达信号发射设备。难点在于闭环策略学习需要位姿相关的Wi-Fi观测，以及移动机器人上多径传播导致的方位测量噪声。

Method: 1) WiFiSpaces：扩展Habitat具身仿真器，加入在线光线追踪Wi-Fi感知，为机载天线阵列在策略rollout的连续位姿上渲染Wi-Fi观测。2) ViFiNav：定位感知导航框架，包含在线源定位算法（利用机器人运动对多视角方位测量进行消歧与鲁棒聚合，得到置信度感知的源估计）以及一个学习策略（融合演化的源估计与深度观测，并在估计被精化过程中学习对其采取动作）。

Result: 实验显示利用机器人运动是获得准确源估计的关键；在WiFiSpaces中对光线追踪Wi-Fi观测进行微调可提升ViFiNav成功率；策略零样本迁移到未见场景数据集，并在真实机器人上部署（使用由商用路由器构建的Wi-Fi传感器），无需任何真实世界训练。

Conclusion: 视觉-Wi-Fi导航可通过仿真中光线追踪Wi-Fi感知与定位感知策略实现，运动消歧对定位至关重要；仿真训练的策略可零样本迁移并直接部署到真实机器人，无需真实数据训练，展示了该感知模态在具身导航中的潜力。

Abstract: Indoor navigation to task-relevant targets remains difficult when the target is small, occluded, or visually indistinct. Meanwhile, smart appliances, mobile devices, and IoT tags emit Wi-Fi signals during everyday communication, providing a pervasive but underused spatial cue. We study visual-Wi-Fi navigation, where a robot reaches a signal-emitting device in an unmapped environment using onboard visual observations and Wi-Fi packets overheard from the device, without connecting to the device or modifying infrastructure. Realizing this requires pose-dependent Wi-Fi observations during closed-loop policy learning and reliable spatial cues from bearing measurements corrupted by indoor multipath propagation on a moving robot. We present WiFiSpaces, which extends the Habitat embodied simulator with online ray-traced Wi-Fi sensing, rendering Wi-Fi observations for a robot-mounted antenna array at continuous poses during policy rollouts. On top of it, we build ViFiNav, a localization-aware navigation framework: an online source-localization algorithm exploits robot motion to disambiguate and robustly aggregate bearings from multiple viewpoints into a confidence-aware source estimate, and a learned policy fuses this evolving estimate with depth observations and learns to act on it as it is refined. Experiments show that exploiting robot motion is key to accurate source estimates, and that fine-tuning on ray-traced Wi-Fi observations in WiFiSpaces improves the success rate of ViFiNav. The policy transfers zero-shot to unseen scene datasets, and we deploy it on a real robot with a Wi-Fi sensor built from a commodity router, without any real-world training.

</details>


### [143] [StereoEngine: Scaling Stereo Bimanual Robot Data without Stereo Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12734)
*Jason Chen,I-Chun Arthur Liu,Gaurav Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StereoEngine 提出两种合成机器人操作立体数据的方法：单目视频经深度估计变形+视频扩散模型修补遮挡区生成右视，或仿真中渲染立体对并联合生成逼真双视图，用于 VLA 策略预训练/微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作立体数据稀缺，现有生成式单目转立体方法面向 VR 与自动驾驶，尚无面向操作任务的逼真立体数据合成；立体监督主要来自仿真。

Method: 两条互补路线：(1) 用估计深度将单目左视视频 warp 到右视，并用视频扩散模型 inpaint 遮挡消失区域，无需真实立体对训练；(2) 仿真渲染立体对后联合生成左右两幅逼真视图，无需真实视频输入。

Result: 生成的立体数据质量优于现有基线；将合成数据用于策略 mid-training 或任务特定微调，显著提升双臂操作下游性能。

Conclusion: StereoEngine 填补了机器人操作逼真立体数据合成的空白，可作为 VLA 策略扩展的有效数据来源。

Abstract: Stereo vision is increasingly used for robotic manipulation, with a growing body of work incorporating it into Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, the scarcity of large-scale, real-world stereo datasets remains a fundamental bottleneck for scaling such policies. Recent generative monocular-to-stereo methods target settings such as VR content and autonomous driving. No prior work produces realistic synthetic stereo data for robotic manipulation, where stereo supervision has come predominantly from simulation. We propose StereoEngine, which introduces two complementary methods to synthesize stereo data for robotic manipulation. In the first, we warp a monocular (left) video into the right view using estimated depth and inpaint the disoccluded regions with a video diffusion model, lifting monocular demonstrations into stereo pairs without training on real stereo. In the second, we render a stereo pair in simulation and jointly generate both photorealistic views, producing consistent left-right data without any real video as input. StereoEngine outperforms existing baselines in generated stereo quality, and using its synthetic data for policy mid-training or task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves downstream bimanual manipulation. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [144] [Trajectory Prediction for Autonomous Driving in Road Construction Zones Using Human-in-the-Loop Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12747)
*Shuo Zhang,Aman Sunesh,Semiha Ergan,Kaan Ozbay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WZDrive-Data城市施工区多模态驾驶数据集（33名驾驶员、1183段）与多模态自车轨迹预测器WZDrive，融合驾驶员控制与注视信号，minADE(K=6)=1.09m、minFDE(K=6)=2.12m，较最优基线分别降低7.18%与13.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市道路施工区自车轨迹预测研究稀缺，缺乏专用数据与系统评测。施工区车道布局与连通性临时改变，与周边车辆及施工人员的交互复杂，导致未来轨迹难以预测。

Method: 采用人在回路驾驶模拟器采集多模态数据，含预录施工人员动画与VR控制工人；记录驾驶员控制与注视信号，并由模拟器获取车辆轨迹与场景信息。基于该数据训练多模态自车轨迹预测模型WZDrive。

Result: WZDrive取得minADE(K=6)=1.09 m、minFDE(K=6)=2.12 m；相对本次基准中各指标最优的SOTA基线，minADE降低7.18%，minFDE降低13.18%。

Conclusion: 工作填补了城市施工区自动驾驶研究的空白，将人在回路数据采集与人类驾驶信号的多模态学习相结合，为施工区轨迹预测提供数据与评测基准。

Abstract: Ego-trajectory prediction in urban roadway work zones remains an underexplored topic in autonomous-driving research, with limited dedicated data and systematic evaluation. Roadway work zones involve temporary changes in the lane layouts and connectivity of the roads, complicating interactions with surrounding vehicles and workers. These changes make future vehicle trajectories difficult to predict. To address these challenges, we collect WZDrive-Data, a multimodal dataset comprising 1,183 work-zone driving segments from 33 drivers, using a human-in-the-loop driving simulator with prerecorded worker animations and VR-controlled workers. Driver control and gaze signals are recorded from human participants, while vehicle trajectories and scene information are obtained from the simulator. Our proposed multimodal ego-trajectory predictor, WZDrive, achieves a mean minADE (K=6) of 1.09 m and minFDE (K=6) of 2.12 m. Compared with the best-performing state-of-the-art baseline for each metric in our benchmark, WZDrive reduces minADE (K=6) by 7.18% and minFDE (K=6) by 13.18%. This research addresses a gap in autonomous-driving research on urban work zones by combining human-in-the-loop data collection with multimodal learning from human driving signals and in the context of work zones.

</details>


### [145] [Verifier-Mediated Multi-LLM Coordination for Multi-Robot Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12761)
*Vivek Khatana,Zijian Song,Naira Hovakimyan,Petros Voulgaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM多机器人任务规划的执行感知协调问题，提出"验证器中介"架构：由几何验证器将LLM生成的符号计划落地为轨迹、检测机器人间冲突并生成结构化反馈，驱动集中式单LLM与分散式多LLM两种架构的定向重规划，并在CARLA与Crazyflie实机上评估随任务复杂度变化的协调性能与可执行性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多用于多机器人高层任务规划，但各机器人独立生成的符号计划在几何落地后可能因共享时空约束而变得联合不可行，即语义层可行不等于执行层可行，缺少面向执行的协调机制。

Method: 对比两种LLM规划架构：集中式（单一LLM对机器人团队联合推理）与分散式多LLM（每台机器人独立提出任务计划）。引入几何验证器将候选计划落地为机器人轨迹、检测预测的机器人间冲突，并将冲突信息转化为结构化反馈用于定向重规划，从而在LLM规划器间实现迭代协调，实现语义任务推理与执行级可行性的解耦。

Result: 在CARLA仿真与Crazyflie空中机器人上开展实验，评估随任务复杂度提升的协调性能，刻画集中式推理、分散式验证器中介协调与物理可执行性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 验证器中介的解耦架构能够在不牺牲语义规划能力的前提下提升多机器人计划的联合可执行性，集中式与分散式架构各有优劣，需按任务复杂度取舍。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used for high-level multi-robot task planning, yet independently generated symbolic plans can become jointly infeasible after geometric grounding because of shared spatial and temporal constraints. We study execution-aware coordination of large-language-model (LLM) planners for multi-robot navigation. We compare a centralized architecture, in which one LLM jointly reasons over the robot team, with a decentralized multi-LLM architecture in which each robot independently proposes a task plan. A geometric verifier grounds the candidate plans into robot trajectories, detects predicted inter-robot conflicts, and converts conflict information into structured feedback for targeted replanning. This verifier-mediated architecture separates semantic task reasoning from execution-level feasibility while enabling iterative coordination among LLM planners. Experiments in CARLA and on Crazyflie aerial robots evaluate coordination performance as task complexity increases and characterize the trade-offs between centralized reasoning, decentralized verifier-mediated coordination, and physical executability.

</details>


### [146] [Reflex: Visually Grounded Reactive Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12772)
*Taoyang Jia,Weikai Huang,Linxin Song,Jared Darlington,Jieyu Zhao,Yue Wang,Jiafei Duan,Zhongzheng Ren,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Reflex三阶段框架，让人形机器人从自视角RGB-D实时接住抛来的箱子：先RL学状态策略，再学从延迟不完整观测推断物体动力学，最后用RGB-D编码器恢复该动力学表征。仿真85.7%成功率，真机Unitree G1在2.5/5米达65%/40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能在约1秒内以全身反射式动作应对移动目标（接球、接住掉落的杯子、躲避推车等），这要求全身协调控制、对物体动力学的实时反应、以及以自视角视觉为基础。人形机器人难以同时满足这三点；已有系统只覆盖部分能力，依赖末端执行器、动捕或带标记物体。

Method: 以接抛来的箱子为代表任务，采用三阶段分解学习，阶段间用紧凑的物体动力学表征连接：(1) 用强化学习训练基于状态的全身接取策略；(2) 让策略学会从延迟且不完整的观测推断运动箱子的动力学；(3) 训练自视角RGB-D编码器直接从视觉观测恢复该动力学表征，同时冻结全身控制器。该分解为每项能力提供直接训练信号，使视觉学习更可扩展。

Result: 仿真中自视角RGB-D接取成功率85.7%，接近特权箱体观测的87.9%。时序视觉历史关键：去除后在中置与横向偏移抛掷上分别下降35.6和39.8个百分点。真机Unitree G1在2.5米和5米接人类抛箱成功率达65%和40%，相当于同距离人类接取者成功率的76%和59%。

Conclusion: 三阶段分解与共享动力学表征可有效实现人形机器人的视觉反应式全身接取，并在真机上取得接近人类水平的部分成功，证明分阶段训练对视觉全身控制的可扩展性。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly react to the world; they adeptly return a tennis shot, catch a falling cup, step aside from a passing cart, or pass a box tossed during truck unloading. These everyday actions require anticipating a moving target and reflexively responding with the whole body within about a second. Such visual reactive whole-body control remains challenging for humanoids, which must (1) coordinate whole-body control, (2) react to object dynamics in real time, and (3) ground perception in egocentric vision. We study catching thrown boxes as a representative task that demands all three capabilities; existing systems cover only part of it, relying on end-effectors, motion capture, or marked objects. We present Reflex, a three-stage framework that learns these capabilities stage by stage while connecting them through a compact representation of object dynamics. First, a state-based policy learns whole-body catching with reinforcement learning. Second, the policy learns to infer the dynamics of a moving box from delayed and incomplete observations. Third, an egocentric RGB-D encoder learns to recover this dynamics representation directly from visual observations while keeping the whole-body controller fixed. This decomposition provides each capability with a direct training signal and makes visual learning substantially more scalable. In simulation, Reflex achieves 85.7% catch success from egocentric RGB-D, compared with 87.9% when given privileged box observations. Temporal visual history is critical: removing it reduces success by 35.6 and 39.8 points on centered and laterally offset throws. On a real Unitree G1, Reflex catches human-thrown boxes with 65% and 40% success at 2.5 and 5 m, reaching 76% and 59% of human catchers' success at the same distances.

</details>


### [147] [Multimodal Image-based Robotic Assistance and Guidance Environment (MIRAGE): A Novel Unified Framework for Surgical Navigation and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12780)
*Ehsan Nasiri,Yi Zheng,Michael A. Kokko,Xiaoyao Fan,William R. Warner,Keith D. Paulsen,Ryan J. Halter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRAGE：面向da Vinci手术机器人的多模态图像引导框架，集成立体视觉重建、AR叠加、HUD量化导航与术中超声，在S/Si/Xi/SP平台上完成体模验证，平均3D重投影误差0.6mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人缺乏统一的实时多模态可视化与导航集成平台，现有系统在异构视频/跟踪流同步、标定-注册-重建流程一致性上难以复用，因此需要一个可扩展的临床前技术验证框架。

Method: C++后端负责视频I/O与渲染，Python GUI通过进程内零拷贝绑定协调标定、注册、重建与可视化；融合立体视觉场景重建、AR视频叠加、HUD量化导航、术中超声（TilePro显示）、光学/电磁跟踪注册、立体标定、基于标记的刚体配准（RMSE反馈）与3D点云重建；形式化空间变换、模块状态及异构视频与跟踪流的同步；术前用同步棋盘格图像对做内参/外参/校正优化，术中复用。

Result: 在da Vinci S、Si、Xi、SP四平台的体模实验中，临床相关工作距离下平均3D重投影误差0.6mm，Xi平台100mm处最佳RMSE 0.1mm。

Conclusion: MIRAGE可作为精确、低延迟且可扩展的多模态手术导航的临床前技术验证平台。

Abstract: This paper presents the computational architecture and quantitative preclinical evaluation of MIRAGE, a multimodal image-guided robotic surgery framework for real-time visualization and navigation. It integrates stereovision scene reconstruction, augmented-reality (AR) video overlay, quantified heads-up display (HUD) navigation, and intraoperative ultrasound within the da Vinci console's TilePro display (Intuitive Surgical). Additional modules include optical/electromagnetic tracker registration, stereo calibration, fiducial rigid registration with RMSE feedback, and 3D point-cloud reconstruction. Video I/O and rendering run in a C++ backend, while a Python GUI coordinates calibration, registration, reconstruction, visualization, and user interaction through an in-process zero-copy binding layer. The framework formalizes spatial transforms, module states, and synchronization of heterogeneous video and tracking streams. Preoperative calibration uses synchronized checkerboard image pairs and optimization, with estimated intrinsics, extrinsics, and rectification reused intraoperatively. MIRAGE was validated in phantom experiments across da Vinci S, Si, Xi, and SP platforms. Across clinically relevant working distances, mean 3D reprojection error was 0.6mm, with best 0.1mm RMSE at 100mm on Xi. Results support MIRAGE as a preclinical technical-validation platform for accurate, responsive, and extensible multimodal surgical guidance.

</details>


### [148] [Tactile Sensing-based Secure and Gentle Grasping of Unknown Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12790)
*Feng Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线数据驱动的预测控制框架，用双单通道触觉模块与高斯过程在线学习抓取动力学，将模型不确定性转化为鲁棒抓取约束嵌入MPC，实现在线辨识安全抓取力约束并以100 Hz实时动态操作未知物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知物体的抓取需要同时满足"抓稳（不滑移）"与"轻柔（不损坏）"两难约束，且缺乏物体先验与离线训练数据；现有方法难以在线确定既能防滑又防损的抓取力范围，也难以在动态操作中保证约束满足。

Method: 1）硬件：一对紧凑单通道触觉传感模块，实时表征手-物动态交互，无需先验知识或离线训练；2）有限状态机抓取力探测机制，建立初始抓取力并显式辨识抓取力约束上下界；3）以高斯过程（GP）在线学习手-物交互动力学，用模型不确定性构造鲁棒抓取约束；4）将约束嵌入模型预测控制（MPC），并给出概率意义下的误差收敛性分析；5）控制回路以100 Hz实时运行，实现动态抓取与搬运而非静态保持。

Result: 在多种接触表面拓扑与多类物体上的实验表明，相比基线策略具有更优的性能与效率；可操作从生豆腐、棉花糖等易损软物到石块等粗糙刚性物体的广泛对象。

Conclusion: 触觉在线学习+鲁棒MPC的组合可在无先验、无离线训练条件下实现未知物体的安全动态抓取，并具备概率收敛保证与实时性，验证了secure-and-gentle约束的可行性。

Abstract: Reliable robotic manipulation requires maintaining a firm grasp while strictly avoiding both contact slip and structural damage to the object. Satisfying these inherent \textit{secure-and-gentle} grasping constraints is challenging for unknown objects. We present a novel online data-driven predictive control framework for safe dynamic robotic grasping of unknown objects. The proposed system features a pair of compact, single-channel tactile-sensing modules that characterize the gripper-object dynamic interaction in real time, entirely bypassing the need for prior knowledge or offline training. A finite-state-machine-based grasping force probing mechanism is developed to establish the initial grasping force and explicitly identify grasping force constraints. Through online dynamics learning, a Gaussian Process (GP) model captures gripper-object interaction dynamics. The model uncertainties are further used to form robust grasping constraints, which are embedded into a model predictive control scheme. We demonstrate error convergence in a probabilistic sense. The proposed control framework runs at 100~Hz in real time to dynamically manipulate and handle objects rather than static holding. Extensive experimental evaluations across diverse contact surface topologies and various types of objects validate superior performance and efficiency compared to baseline strategies. The proposed method is able to manipulate objects ranging from delicate items, such as raw tofu and marshmallows, to rough objects, such as rocks.

</details>


### [149] [Gamma: Grounded Multi-Agent Memory with Mechanical Audit for Memory-Intensive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12793)
*Yudong Liu,Yueqian Lin,Zijia Tang,Yuxi Zheng,Yuan Li,Shuai Zhang,Yi Li,Taotao Jing,Shuangjun Liu,Dashan Gao,Ning Bi,Jingwei Sun,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAMMA 将 VLA 记忆任务的"记忆→接地子目标"瓶颈重构为双 Agent 协作记忆系统（SAM-3 检测器 + VLM 写入器 + 追加式文本记忆库 + VLM 推理器），并用"提议-验证"机制以机械视觉证据审计每条断言，在 16 项记忆-操作任务上大幅逼近 oracle 上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 VLA 策略训练数据高度马尔可夫，难以把情节记忆直接映射到动作块，因此在依赖久远事件的记忆任务上失败。作者发现同一策略若微调为跟随特权 oracle 的接地子目标文本，几乎能解决这些任务——说明全部难点在于"记忆→接地符号子目标"这一阶段。现有做法交给单个 VLM 做关键帧选择与原始帧推理，存在身份与动态被噪声像素掩埋、上下文跨越数百帧无界、幻觉污染记忆三重缺陷，远不及 oracle。

Method: 提出 GAMMA：把该阶段构建为协作式双 Agent 记忆系统——①检测工具 SAM-3 负责空间接地；②VLM writer 将每个重规划窗口蒸馏为一行接地文本；③追加式文本记忆库；④VLM reasoner 读取记忆库并按 oracle 的节拍输出下一接地子目标。全流程包裹"提议-验证"（propose-verify）框架，对每个 agent 断言用机械视觉证据做准入/延后/纠正/拒绝，且该框架在训练语料构建与部署阶段一致使用。

Result: 在 16 项记忆-操作任务套件上，保持策略固定不变，GAMMA 恢复了 oracle 上限的很大一部分，在若干任务上达到上限；并以不变的接口契约迁移到第二个 benchmark。

Conclusion: 将记忆到符号子目标的映射解耦为可验证的多 Agent 文本记忆流水线，可在不重训策略的前提下显著提升 VLA 的长时记忆能力，且模块化契约具备跨 benchmark 可用性。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies fail on memory tasks, behaviour that depends on events observed long before the current frame, because their training data is overwhelmingly Markov and mapping episodic memory directly to action chunks is hard. Yet the same policy, finetuned once to follow a privileged oracle's grounded-subgoal text, nearly solves these tasks: the difficulty lies entirely in mapping memory to a grounded symbolic subgoal. Existing work entrusts this stage to a single VLM that selects keyframes and reasons over raw frames, and falls far short of the oracle: key identities and dynamics are buried in noisy pixels, the reasoning spans hundreds of frames in an unbounded context, and hallucination corrupts the memory itself. We instead present GAMMA (Grounded multi-Agent Memory with Mechanical Audit), which builds the stage as a collaborating two-agent memory system: a detector tool (SAM-3) for spatial grounding, a VLM writer that distils each replan window into one grounded text line, an append-only text memory bank, and a VLM reasoner that reads the bank and emits the next grounded subgoal at the oracle's cadence, wrapped in a propose-verify harness that admits, defers, corrects, or rejects every agent claim against mechanical visual evidence, at training-corpus construction and at deployment alike. On a sixteen-task memory-manipulation suite GAMMA recovers a large fraction of the oracle ceiling with the policy held fixed, reaching the ceiling on several tasks, and transfers with unchanged contracts to a second benchmark.

</details>


### [150] [LeWAM: End-to-End World and Action Modeling with JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12818)
*Minghao Fu,Tavis Siebert,Eryk Halicki,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeWAM 提出端到端 JEPA 控制框架，联合训练下一隐状态预测与动作流匹配，并施加各向同性高斯隐状态约束，使同一模型兼具直接策略执行与基于隐动力学的采样规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 流水线通常分阶段训练：先学视觉表征与动作条件动力学，再单独加控制策略或求解器，导致表征与控制器割裂、规划效率低、在接触密集与长时序任务上成功率差。

Method: 两点核心设计：(1) 联合训练目标，同时优化下一隐状态预测与动作流匹配（action flow matching）；(2) 对隐状态施加各向同性高斯约束，使隐空间具备良好采样结构，从而单模型即可支持直接策略执行，也可通过评估采样动作+学习到的隐动力学进行高效规划。

Result: 视觉规划上成功率从 28.6% 提升至 89.7%（接触密集任务），长时序任务从 10.9% 提升至 46.2%，规划速度快 32.3 倍；仿真与真实机器人行为克隆任务中与成熟方法相当；可解释性分析显示其学到与控制相关的表征及有效引导测试时规划的动作分布。

Conclusion: 端到端联合训练 JEPA（隐预测+流匹配）配合高斯隐约束，可在不重建像素的前提下统一策略执行与规划，显著优于分阶段 JEPA 基线，并在真实机器人 BC 任务上具竞争力。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models without pixel reconstruction. Existing JEPA pipelines typically train visual representations and action-conditioned dynamics before adding a control policy or solver. We introduce LeWAM, an end-to-end JEPA for control with: (1) a joint training objective over next latent state prediction and action flow matching, and (2) an isotropic Gaussian constraint on the latent state, enabling a single model to support direct policy execution and efficient planning by evaluating sampled actions with its learned latent dynamics. Compared with prior JEPAs in visual planning, LeWAM improves success rate from 28.6% to 89.7% on contact-rich tasks and from 10.9% to 46.2% on long-horizon tasks, and plans 32.3 times faster. On behavior cloning tasks in simulation and physical robots, LeWAM is also competitive with established methods. Interpretability analyses show that LeWAM learns control-relevant representations and action distributions effectively guiding test-time planning and control.

</details>


### [151] [Cubic Spline Interpolation for Smooth Trajectory Generation in Autonomous Motion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12826)
*Aarush Mandayam,Sriram Aditya Gundla,Elliott Tammaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化求解三次样条插值问题的程序，将位置-时间路径点序列的插值与连续性条件组装为单一线性系统并直接求解样条系数，从而生成位置、速度、加速度连续的光滑轨迹，避免分段线性路径跟随中的无穷加速度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分段线性路径点跟随存在加速度无穷大的固有问题，且每更换一组路径点都需人工推导各段方程，成本高、易出错。作者希望用自动化求解替代手工推导，为机器人/无人机平滑轨迹生成提供通用底层工具。

Method: 把三次样条插值条件与各阶连续性条件统一组装成一个线性方程组，直接求解样条系数，从而免去逐段手动推导；在自由曲线绘制与签名数字化两个任务上进行演示，二者与机器人、无人机平滑轨迹生成共享同一套机制。

Result: 程序能够对给定路径点序列自动产出位置、速度、加速度连续（C²）的轨迹，消除无穷加速度；并在自由曲线绘制和签名数字化两个任务上验证了该方法的可用性。文中的未来工作（多轴轨迹与显式速度/加速度约束、三对角高效求解、定量对比评估）表明尚无定量实验与机器人平台验证。

Conclusion: 该工作本质是一个经典三次样条插值的自动化实现与教学式演示，属于轨迹生成的工具性/背景性贡献，对物理AI与机器人轨迹平滑有间接价值，但缺乏真实机器人实验、定量基准与显式约束处理，属于相关背景或工具类工作。

Abstract: We presented a program that automates the construction and solution of the cubic spline interpolation problem for a sequence of position-time waypoints, producing trajectories that are continuous in position, velocity, and acceleration and therefore avoid the infinite-acceleration problem inherent to piecewise-linear waypoint following. By assembling the interpolation and continuity conditions into a single linear system and solving directly for the spline coefficients, the program removes the need to manually derive per-segment equations for each new waypoint set. We illustrated the approach on free-form curve drawing and signature digitization, two tasks that use the same underlying machinery required for smooth robot and drone trajectory generation. Future work includes extending the implementation to multi-axis trajectories with explicit velocity and acceleration bounds, adopting the more computationally efficient tridiagonal solution method for larger waypoint sets, and evaluating the approach quantitatively against alternative trajectory generation methods.

</details>


### [152] [VLMs are Native Action Models: Unleashing the Manipulation Capabilities of VLMs with Explicit Action-Space Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12832)
*Jingkun Yi,Shujie Zhang,Zirui Zhou,Haoxiao Wang,Haiyang Sun,Yuhang Dong,Xudong Xu,Xing Shen,Tao Zhang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EASG 将 TCP 动作帧投影到腕部相机观测中，并用 VLM 原生文本生成接口表达连续动作，使预训练 VLM 无需动作预训练即可零样本操作机器人，并支持同一接口微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 VLM 已具备视觉、空间与推理能力，但如何通过合适的动作接口把这些能力转化为通用操作策略仍是难点：VLA 需从机器人数据学习动作 token/头/专家的语义，模块化基础模型方法依赖不可端到端的外部规划控制框架。

Method: 提出 Explicit Action-Space Grounding (EASG)：把以工具中心点 (TCP) 为中心的动作坐标系投影到腕部相机观测中，并将动作以 VLM 原生文本生成形式表达，使连续机器人动作空间语义在 VLM 感知上下文中直接可观测；同一动作接口也支持用机器人轨迹直接微调。

Result: 可实现零样本操作，在物体位置、类别和机器人本体上的泛化，同时支持在同一连续动作空间内有效微调，持续提升操作能力。

Conclusion: 在合适的动作接口下，预训练 VLM 本身即可作为机器人操作的原生动作模型，无需机器人动作预训练或额外动作模块。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) already exhibit strong visual, spatial and reasoning capabilities relevant to robotic manipulation. The key challenge in turning pretrained VLMs into generalist manipulation policies is to expose these capabilities through an appropriate action interface. Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified trainable policy, but typically introduce learned action tokens, action heads, or action experts whose semantics must be learned from robot data pretraining. In contrast, modular foundation-model-based manipulation methods can exploit pretrained VLM capabilities for zero-shot manipulation, but rely on external planning and control scaffolds that are not end-to-end trainable as a unified continuous-action policy. In this work, we introduce EASG, an Explicit Action-Space Grounding framework that bridges these two paradigms. By projecting a tool center point (TCP)-centered action frame into the wrist camera observation and expressing actions in the form of VLM's native textual generation interface, EASG makes the semantics of a continuous robot action space directly observable in the VLM's perceptual context. This allows pretrained VLMs to directly generate executable robot actions using their existing visual-spatial reasoning capabilities, without robot-action pretraining or additional action modules. Besides, the same action interface supports direct fine-tuning with robot trajectories, allowing the models to continuously improve beyond their manipulation capabilities unleashed by EASG. Overall, EASG enables zero-shot manipulation and generalization across object positions, categories, and robot embodiments, while supporting effective fine-tuning in the same continuous action space. These results demonstrate that, given an appropriate action interface, pretrained VLMs can themselves serve as native action models for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [153] [Evidence-Guided Counterfactual Learning for Long-Tailed Robotic Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12861)
*Weiwei Chen,Chenghao Xu,Meiling Yu,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出证据引导反事实学习（EGCL）训练框架，用指令与动作相关信号定位决策关键视觉证据并构造反事实观测，强化指令-证据-动作关联，按任务样本量自适应调节监督，缓解长尾VLA策略中的头部动作偏见，无需额外数据与标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集高度不平衡，长尾任务部署困难。作者识别出"头部任务动作偏见"：尾部任务视觉证据不足时，策略退回到数据丰富头部任务强化的动作模式，导致欠代表任务性能下降。

Method: 用指令相关与动作相关信号识别决策关键视觉证据；通过移除所选证据构造反事实观测；用反事实预测强化任务指令、视觉证据与动作之间的关联；监督权重按各任务演示数量自适应调整。不依赖额外训练数据、区域级标注，部署阶段无额外计算开销。

Result: 在多个仿真基准、多种VLA架构以及真实机器人平台上实验，一致且稳健地提升长尾策略学习效果，尾部任务改善尤为明显，同时保持头部任务可靠性能。

Conclusion: 面向长尾VLA策略的免标注、免额外数据的训练框架，通过反事实视觉证据引导纠正头部动作偏见，在仿真与真机中均验证有效，可作为长尾机器人示教数据学习的通用训练组件。

Abstract: In the practical deployment of vision--language--action policies, handling long-tailed task distributions is a common challenge due to the highly imbalanced composition of robot demonstration datasets. To investigate this issue, we study the head-task action bias in shared robot policies, particularly focusing on its relationship with task-specific visual evidence. Our experimental analysis shows that, when the visual evidence required by a tail task is insufficient, the policy increasingly falls back on action patterns reinforced by data-rich head tasks, resulting in degraded performance on under-represented tasks. Motivated by this observation, we propose Evidence-Guided Counterfactual Learning, a training framework for long-tailed robot policies. Our method identifies decision-relevant visual evidence using instruction-related and action-related signals, and constructs counterfactual observations by removing the selected evidence. The resulting counterfactual predictions are used to strengthen the association among task instructions, visual evidence, and actions, with the supervision adaptively adjusted according to the number of demonstrations available for each task. The proposed framework requires neither additional training data nor region-level annotations and incurs no additional computation during deployment. Extensive experiments across simulated benchmarks, different VLA architectures, and real-world robot platforms demonstrate that our method robustly and consistently improves long-tailed robot policy learning, particularly for tail tasks, while maintaining reliable performance on head tasks.

</details>


### [154] [TAME-Nav: Temporal Asymmetric Mixture-of-Experts Distillation for Agile and Versatile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12877)
*Lingjie Zhang,Jun Xue,Shanze Wang,Haoyu Xi,Xinming Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAME-Nav：基于时序软混合专家（Soft-MoE）的非对称师生导航框架，教师用特权动态障碍轨迹跟踪构建表征，学生从受损观测历史中经时序编码、软专家路由与隐空间蒸馏学习互补特征，实现复杂动态场景下的敏捷可靠导航与实机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人在复杂空间约束与动态交互中需要敏捷可靠的导航，但单一策略难以同时满足多类场景各异的决策需求，因此需要能按场景自适应组合多种决策模式的导航策略。

Method: 采用非对称 Teacher-Student 架构：教师以干净观测加因果维护的动态障碍物轨迹构成特权表征；学生仅用受损观测历史，通过时序编码、时序 Soft-MoE 软专家路由与隐空间蒸馏学习互补导航特征。训练侧采用程序化场景生成与并行任务级仿真扩大覆盖，并以面向部署的非对称域随机化支持 sim-to-real。

Result: 在分布内及未见组合场景中，成功率、导航得分高于代表性基线，碰撞率更低；真实四足机器人上，在局部陷阱逃脱与突发动态障碍的狭窄通道导航中完成全部 20 次试验，仅出现一次轻微接触。

Conclusion: 非对称师生蒸馏结合时序软混合专家与部署导向域随机化，可在多场景动态导航中获得优于基线的性能并稳定迁移到真实四足平台，为动态约束下的多模式导航策略提供可行方案。

Abstract: Agile and reliable navigation is a core capability for embodied robots navigating complex spatial constraints and dynamic interactions. However, a single policy often struggles to accommodate the distinct decision-making demands of diverse scenarios. We present TAME-Nav, an asymmetric Teacher-Student navigation framework built around a temporal soft mixture of experts (Soft-MoE). The Teacher combines clean observations with causally maintained dynamic-obstacle tracks to construct privileged representations, while the Student learns complementary navigation features from corrupted observation histories through temporal encoding, soft expert routing, and latent distillation. Procedural scenario generation and parallel task-level simulation broaden training coverage, while deployment-informed asymmetric domain randomization supports sim-to-real transfer. Across in-distribution and unseen compositional scenarios, TAME-Nav achieves higher success rates, lower collision rates, and higher navigation scores than representative baselines. On a real quadruped robot, it completes all twenty trials across local-trap escape and narrow-passage navigation with sudden dynamic obstacles, with only one minor contact.

</details>


### [155] [LeapBot-JAC: Joint Action Chains for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12886)
*Chenhao Zhu,Baibing Liu,Pei Liu,Rui Yang,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeapBot-JAC 在固定动作 token 预算下，用共享动作专家同时建模"稀疏未来控制参考链 + 密集执行目标链"，在不增加 token 与参数的前提下显著提升 VLA/WAM 策略的成功率与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 action-chunking 策略虽预测未来控制序列，但只执行前缀后即重新规划，导致固定动作 token 预算的组织方式未被充分研究：全用于近端执行会缺乏远期引导，全用于远期预测又与执行脱节。作者希望在不增加 token/参数、不改变宿主世界建模流程的条件下，明确"哪个动作 token 承担什么角色"，以兼顾可执行性与前瞻性。

Method: 提出 JAC（Joint Action Chaining），在单一共享动作专家内划分两类 token：(1) 稀疏的未来控制参考链，(2) 密集的执行目标链。两条链均由演示控制监督，并在对齐的物理时间位置上联合去噪；通过 directed attention 让执行 token 在宿主可见性约束下读取参考 token。方法不新增动作 token 或参数，保留宿主 world-modeling 过程；推理时两链均参与，但仅执行链驱动机器人。在 FastWAM 与 LeapBot-WA 两个宿主上实现，并于 LIBERO、零样本 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 及两个真实任务上评测，附单训练种子对比与消融。

Result: LIBERO 上 FastWAM 与 LeapBot-WA 成功率分别由 97.0%、97.3% 提升至 98.3%；零样本 LIBERO-Plus 上分别由 53.5%→70.4%、73.1%→77.3%。RoboTwin 与两个真实世界任务提供额外部署验证，单种子对比与 FastWAM 消融支持"动作角色组织"作为固定 token 预算下的实用设计选择。

Conclusion: 在固定动作 token 预算下，通过显式的角色分工（稀疏参考 + 密集执行）与定向注意力耦合，可在零额外 token/参数成本下提升 chunking 策略的执行性能，尤其显著改善零样本分布外泛化，说明动作链的组织方式是 VLA/WAM 策略的有效设计维度。

Abstract: Action-chunking policies predict future controls but execute only a prefix before replanning. How should a fixed action-token budget be organized to support execution? We propose LeapBot-JAC, which allocates sparse future-control references and dense execution targets within one shared action expert. Both chains are supervised by demonstrated controls and jointly denoised at aligned physical-time positions. Directed attention allows execution tokens to read reference tokens subject to the host's visibility constraints. JAC adds neither action tokens nor parameters and retains the host's world-modeling process; both chains participate in inference, while only the execution chain drives the robot. On LIBERO, JAC reaches 98.3% success with FastWAM and LeapBot-WA, up from 97.0% and 97.3%. On zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, their success rates improve from 53.5% to 70.4% and from 73.1% to 77.3%, respectively. RoboTwin and two real-world tasks provide additional deployment evaluations. Single-training-seed comparisons and FastWAM ablations support action-role organization as a practical design choice under a fixed action-token budget.

</details>


### [156] [Sampling-based Kinodynamic Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12910)
*Yitian Gao,Hanlan Yang,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将采样型运动规划（RRT、KPIECE）引入世界模型潜空间，用学习模型作为树搜索传播器，在长时程规划上以更低时间匹配或超越CEM/MPPI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于世界模型的规划多依赖CEM/MPPI等优化器，这类方法在需要"无奖励中间运动"的长时程任务（如绕过障碍再推动）上容易停滞，因为目标函数无法奖励必要的前置动作。

Method: 把世界模型当作潜空间中树搜索的传播器，复用配置空间高维搜索的采样策略：最近邻引导的RRT与基于投影的KPIECE两种探索策略，并做GPU原生批量扩展以提升设备利用率；使用现成DINO-WM作为世界模型。

Result: 在4个仿真域和真实机器人推块任务上评估，仿真中成功率与CEM/MPPI相当或更高，规划时间最多降低13.8倍；真机成功率提升20%。

Conclusion: 采样型运动规划可直接迁移到世界模型潜空间，通过快速穿越短视优化器不会采样的中间状态，改善长时程规划的效率与成功率，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Visual world models predict the consequences of actions from images, but planning with them is typically done by optimizers such as CEM and MPPI. However, these optimizers stall on long-horizon tasks where progress requires motion the objective does not reward, such as moving around a block before pushing it. This paper revisits kinodynamic sampling-based motion planning for world models, using the learned model as the propagator of a tree search in latent space. Our key insight is that the sampling strategies devised for high-dimensional search in configuration space apply directly to the latent space of a world model, where they rapidly expand through intermediate states that myopic optimizers never sample. We present two exploration strategies for the high-dimensional latent space, nearest-neighbor-guided RRT and projection-based KPIECE, with GPU-native batched expansion for efficient device utilization. We evaluate with an off-the-shelf DINO-WM on four simulated domains and a real-world block-pushing task. Our approaches match or exceed the success rate of CEM and MPPI at up to 13.8x lower planning time in simulation, and improve the success rate by 20% on the real robot.

</details>


### [157] [Evo-World: A Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model with Future Motion Prediction via Optical-Flow Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12936)
*Zile Zhong,Yuxin Du,Tao Lin,Haoxiao Wang,Wei Dai,Binhua Zuo,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Evo-World：轻量VLA模型，用光流监督的“未来运动预测”增强策略学习，同时通过训练策略保持预测表征，在Meta-World MT50达91.1%成功率、真机AgileX单/双臂任务优于基线，仅0.87B参数且更省显存、推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA仅靠动作监督无法显式鼓励对未来场景演化的预判；而直接预测未来图像会把任务相关的运动与大量静态外观纠缠在一起，带来噪声与冗余计算。作者希望用更聚焦任务的预测信号（运动而非外观）提升策略的预测能力，同时保持轻量高效。

Method: 在VLA策略学习中引入光流监督的未来运动预测作为辅助任务，以运动表征替代未来图像预测；并设计训练流程，在策略学习阶段保留这些预测表征——通过调节表征多样性、将表征学习与动作优化解耦，避免预测能力被动作损失侵蚀。整体为轻量架构（约0.87B参数）。

Result: 仿真基准Meta-World MT50上分层平均成功率91.1%，相对无预测基线有13.0%的相对提升；真实AgileX机器人上，在单臂与双臂操作任务中优于所评估基线，同时参数量仅0.87B，GPU显存占用更低、推理效率更高。

Conclusion: 以光流监督的未来运动预测是一种有效且轻量的方式，可提升VLA模型的预测与操控能力；未来运动预测相比未来图像预测更具任务相关性，且不牺牲效率，适合真机部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for visual understanding, language grounding, and robot control, yet anticipating how the scene will evolve beyond the current observation can provide additional information for control. Action supervision alone does not explicitly encourage such anticipation, while future-image prediction entangles task-relevant motion with largely static appearance. To address these limitations, we introduce Evo-World, a lightweight VLA model that augments policy learning with future motion prediction through optical-flow supervision, thereby providing a task-relevant predictive signal focused on motion rather than appearance. We further design the training procedure to preserve these predictive representations during policy learning, regulating representation diversity while decoupling representation learning from action optimization. Across simulation benchmarks, Evo-World demonstrates strong performance, achieving a tier-averaged success rate of 91.1% on Meta-World MT50 , with a 13.0% relative improvement over the non-predictive baseline. On real-world AgileX robots, Evo-World outperforms the evaluated baselines across single-arm and bimanual manipulation tasks while using only 0.87B parameters and delivering lower GPU memory usage and higher inference efficiency. These results demonstrate that future motion prediction through optical-flow supervision provides an effective and lightweight approach to enhancing the predictive capabilities of VLA models.

</details>


### [158] [JoyInside-VLM-Agent: Product-Conditioned Multimodal Policy Learning for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12957)
*Gangyi Li,LuYao Wang,Jingfu Sun,Jingyi Yuan,Hang Liu,Hao Zong,Yujia Hu,Tingting Gao,Jianyong Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JoyInside-VLM-Agent：面向机器人与智能硬件的 ReAct 式多模态智能体，通过闭环沙箱构建产品条件化轨迹、分阶段策略学习（SFT→多教师 on-policy 蒸馏→DPER 强化学习）实现执行动作生成，BFCL v4 得分 77.44，在自建设备/机器人基准上全面领先，并有四足机器人真实案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需将多模态观测与用户请求转化为可执行动作，同时应对产品约束、目标变更与执行反馈；现有训练缺少产品条件化的轨迹数据与稳定的策略优化手段，难以在真实硬件上兼顾目标修正与失败恢复。

Method: 1) 闭环沙箱生成覆盖目标修改与失败恢复的多模态交互轨迹，并以执行结果与语义准则校验；2) 分阶段策略学习：监督微调 + 多教师 on-policy 蒸馏；3) 强化学习采用 DPER（双源特权 on-policy 自蒸馏 + token 级证据路由），在冻结特权教师与共享 rollout 参数的特权学生之间路由 token 级证据，调制源于奖励的 token 优势但保持其符号不变；4) 训练奖励融合生成式过程评估、多裁判排序与任务队列一致性。

Result: BFCL v4 总分 77.44，优于所评估基线；其他公开基准结果不一。在 JoyInside-Devices 与 JoyInside-Robotics（含领域技能精调）上所有报告指标均领先基线。两个真实四足机器人案例展示视觉 grounding 搜索、执行失败后重规划与显式失败上报。

Conclusion: 产品条件化轨迹构建与分阶段策略学习（DPER）可有效提升具身多模态智能体的工具调用与任务执行能力，真实硬件场景下的改进比公开通用基准更显著，但跨基准泛化仍不均衡。

Abstract: Embodied agents must translate multimodal observations and user requests into executable actions while adapting to product constraints, changing goals, and execution feedback. We present JoyInside-VLM-Agent, a ReAct-style multimodal agent developed through product-conditioned trajectory construction and staged policy learning for robotic platforms and smart hardware. A closed-loop sandbox generates multimodal interaction trajectories covering goal revisions and failure recovery, which are checked against execution outcomes and semantic criteria. Supervised fine-tuning and multi-teacher on-policy distillation precede reinforcement learning with DPER (Dual-Source Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Token-Level Evidence Routing). DPER routes token-level evidence between a frozen privileged teacher and a privileged student sharing the rollout policy's parameters, modulating reward-derived token advantages while preserving their signs. Training rewards combine generative process evaluation, multi-judge rankings, and task-queue consistency. JoyInside-VLM-Agent achieves a BFCL v4 overall score of 77.44, exceeding the evaluated baselines; results on the other public benchmarks are mixed. On JoyInside-Devices and JoyInside-Robotics, the evaluated system, which includes domain-specific skill refinement, leads the baselines on all reported metrics. Two real-world quadruped case studies illustrate visually grounded search, replanning after execution failure, and explicit failure reporting.

</details>


### [159] [Adaptive Whole-Body MPC with Optimal Posture Planning for Humanoid Robots under Unknown Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12972)
*Manh Cuong Nguyen,Duc Cuong Vu,Viet Anh Vu,Duc Tuan Pham,Toan Nguyen,Minh Hiep Phung,Ngo Anh Vien,Minh Nhat Vu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应全身控制架构，融合质心MPC、自适应模糊平衡控制器与基于三重积分模型的位姿规划器，在载荷不确定下实现人形机器人稳定起身、抓取、举升与搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人举升与物料搬运时载荷质量、位置未知且变化，导致全身动力学模型失配，传统控制器易失稳或跟踪性能下降。

Method: 三层架构：(i) 质心模型预测控制生成动力学可行参考运动；(ii) 自适应模糊平衡控制器估计并补偿载荷引起的模型失配，输出校正力矩分配至支撑腿关节；(iii) 基于三重积分器模型的最优位姿规划器，生成满足CoM稳定约束、关节限位与全身可行性的起坐/坐起平滑轨迹。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，相比非自适应基线，框架在姿态转换与举升任务中提升了平衡鲁棒性，减少了跟踪性能退化，支持下降、抓取、举升、搬运、放置等行为。

Conclusion: 自适应模糊补偿与质心MPC及轨迹规划的紧耦合是应对载荷不确定性的有效途径，具备真机验证价值，但方法组件较经典，创新性偏增量。

Abstract: Humanoid robots performing lifting and material-handling tasks must maintain balance despite unknown and varying payloads. However, payload uncertainty significantly affects whole-body dynamics and may destabilize conventional controllers. We propose an adaptive whole-body control architecture that integrates three components: (i) a centroidal model predictive controller (MPC) that generates dynamically feasible reference motions, (ii) an adaptive fuzzy balance controller that compensates for payload-induced model mismatch by generating corrective torques distributed across the supporting-leg joints, and (iii) a waypoint-based optimal posture planner built on a triple-integrator model that produces smooth sit-to-stand and stand-to-sit trajectories satisfying center-of-mass (CoM) stability constraints, joint limits, and whole-body feasibility requirements. Together, these components enable stable lowering, grasping, lifting, carrying, and placing behaviors under significant payload uncertainty. Extensive simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework improves balance robustness and reduces tracking degradation during posture transitions and lifting tasks compared to non-adaptive baselines.

</details>


### [160] [EgoReveal: Learning Active Vision and Object Manipulation under Partial Occlusions from Egocentric Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12999)
*Hiromu Kurihara,Kazutoshi Tanaka,Masashi Hatano,Tadashi Kozuno,Hideo Saito,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoReveal 提出一个从第一人称人类演示中同时学习视角重定位与操作动作的框架，用两个模仿策略在目标被部分遮挡时切换，先移动相机获得可见性再完成任务，优于固定相机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称演示数据易于采集、可规模化，无需遥操作或专用示教设备；但日常环境中被操作物体常被部分遮挡，而现有这类方法未显式处理遮挡问题，导致目标可见性不足、任务失败。

Method: EgoReveal 从第一人称人类演示中联合学习视点重定位与操作动作，实例化为两个模仿策略：策略一由当前视图与相机-机械臂位姿预测未来的相机-机械臂位姿序列（用于视角调整）；策略二由物体关键点与控制指令历史预测未来控制指令（用于操作）。二者依据目标可见性进行切换，形成"先重定位视角获得可见性、再执行操作"的闭环。

Result: 在物体初始即被部分遮挡的实验设置下，EgoReveal 能主动重定位相机以获得目标可见性，并比固定相机基线更可靠地完成任务。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了免机器人、免专用示教设备的第一人称演示学习在遮挡场景下的空白，表明将视角重定位纳入模仿学习可有效提升部分遮挡下的操作鲁棒性，为可扩展的演示数据利用提供了一条路径。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstrations (LfD) enables robots to learn object manipulation directly from human demonstrations. In contrast to resource-intensive and labor-intensive teleoperation, egocentric demonstrations are easy to collect, enabling scalable data collection. Manipulated objects are often partially occluded in everyday environments, yet prior egocentric demonstration-based methods that require no robots or specialized teaching devices do not explicitly address occlusions. To fill this gap, we propose EgoReveal, a framework that learns viewpoint-repositioning together with manipulation actions from egocentric human demonstrations to reposition the viewpoint and obtain target visibility under partial occlusions. EgoReveal instantiates this as two imitation policies: one predicts the future camera-arm pose sequence from the current view and camera-arm pose, and the other predicts the future control commands from object keypoints and a history of control commands. EgoReveal switches between the two based on target visibility. In experiments where the manipulated object is partially occluded initially, EgoReveal repositions the camera to obtain visibility and completes the task more reliably than fixed-camera baselines.

</details>


### [161] [CeDR-Nav: Cross-Embodiment Mapless Navigation via Offline Demonstration Recasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13007)
*Xianghui Wang,Xinming Zhang,Yiwei Qian,Siwei Cheng,Shanze Wang,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CeDR-Nav 提出跨具身示范重铸框架，用源机器人示范数据离线训练目标具身的专用导航策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的无地图导航性能强，但策略与训练具身强绑定；现有跨具身方法需耗时在线训练且常劣于目标专用策略。源具身示范中蕴含可复用的导航经验，却因具身不匹配无法直接监督目标策略。

Method: 几何一致缩放配合逐示范有效性评估，将源示范转换为目标训练数据；再用目标专用高斯混合策略生成目标具身可行的避障机动；无需在线数据采集。

Result: 多机器人平台的定量仿真与真实部署验证有效，跨具身仿真中相对 DRL-DCLP 平均成功率提升 13.8 个百分点，目标专用策略训练在数秒内完成。

Conclusion: 示范重铸可在离线条件下高效产出目标具身专用导航策略，显著优于基线并支持快速适配。

Abstract: Learning-based methods have demonstrated strong performance in mapless robot navigation. However, a learned policy remains restricted to the embodiment on which it was trained. Many cross-embodiment navigation methods require time-consuming online training and can still underperform target-specific policies. In fact, demonstrations collected on a source embodiment already contain navigation experience that is potentially reusable for deriving a policy for each target embodiment, but the embodiment mismatch prevents these demonstrations from directly serving as valid supervision for the target. In this article, we present CeDR-Nav, a cross-embodiment demonstration recasting framework that trains a dedicated navigation policy for each target embodiment from existing source demonstrations without online data collection. Geometry-consistent scaling with per-demonstration validity assessment converts the source demonstrations into target training data, and a target-specific Gaussian mixture policy generates feasible avoidance maneuvers for each target embodiment. Extensive quantitative simulations and real-world deployments across multiple robot platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of CeDR-Nav, with an average success-rate gain of 13.8 percentage points over DRL-DCLP in cross-embodiment simulations and target-specific policy training completed within seconds.

</details>


### [162] [Failure-Resilient Multi-Robot Coordination with $k$-Connectivity Constrained Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13049)
*Yoonjae Hwang,Hojune Kim,Ellen Novoseller,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"弹性k连通子模最大化"(Resilient $k$-Connected Submodular Maximization),同时优化任务收益、故障韧性与通信连通性;用Menger定理做连通性归约+连通性感知贪心+曲率分析,给出$[\max(1-\nu_f(\mathcal{I}),h(N,\beta))]^2\rho\frac{1}{\kappa_f}(1-e^{-\kappa_f})$的最坏情况近似保证,并在多机器人目标跟踪实验上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同(覆盖、目标跟踪、信息收集)需要联合选动作以最大化团队收益,子模最大化及其弹性变体可建模机器人失效或对抗性移除。但现有方法忽略机器人损失对通信网络的影响:存活机器人可能因网络断连而无法继续协同。因此需要在任务性能、弹性与通信连通性之间联合权衡。

Method: 1) 形式化弹性k连通子模最大化问题,要求解的可行族在任意故障后仍保持k连通(不会使幸存队伍断连);2) 指出该可行族非拟阵,经典曲率贪心保证无法直接套用;3) 基于Menger定理的连通性归约(Menger-based connectivity reduction);4) 设计连通性感知贪心算法,并加入保持可行性的回退(feasibility-preserving fallback);5) 提出新的基于曲率的分析,用单一因子ρ刻画强制连通性的代价。

Result: 理论上,算法在故障后最坏情况目标值可达最优连通-弹性分配的 $[\max(1-\nu_f(\mathcal{I}),h(N,\beta))]^2 \rho \frac{1}{\kappa_f}(1-e^{-\kappa_f})$ 倍近似,并在适当极限下退化为经典无约束子模最大化保证。实验上在多机器人目标跟踪任务中验证方法有效性,并刻画连通性约束的实际影响。

Conclusion: 将通信连通性显式纳入弹性子模最大化是必要且可行的;通过Menger归约与曲率分析可突破非拟阵可行族带来的理论障碍,在保证连通性的同时得到可控的近似比,并在多机器人目标跟踪中体现连通性约束的实践代价。

Abstract: Multi-robot coordination is essential in applications such as coverage, target tracking, and information gathering, where robots must jointly select actions to maximize team performance. Submodular maximization provides an effective framework for such coordination, and resilient variants further account for robot failures or adversarial removals. However, existing methods typically overlook the impact of robot loss on the communication network, which may disconnect the surviving team and prevent continued coordination. To address this issue, we introduce Resilient $k$-Connected Submodular Maximization, which jointly considers task performance, resilience, and communication connectivity. The resulting feasible family is not a matroid, preventing the direct application of classical curvature-greedy guarantees. We overcome this challenge through a Menger-based connectivity reduction, a connectivity-aware greedy algorithm with a feasibility-preserving fallback, and a new curvature-based analysis that captures the cost of enforcing connectivity through a single factor $\rho$. Our method achieves a worst-case post-failure objective within a factor of $[\max(1-\nu_f(\mathcal{I}),h(N,\beta))]^2 \rho \frac{1}{\kappa_f}(1-e^{-\kappa_f})$ of the optimal connectivity-respecting resilient assignment, while recovering the classical unconstrained guarantee in the appropriate limits. Experiments on multi-robot target tracking validate the proposed method and characterize the practical impact of connectivity constraints.

</details>


### [163] [Towards Safer Cities: A Design Process for Perception Datasets in Urban Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13077)
*Alexander Kroth,Cristian Martinez,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于用户生成地图数据（OSM类）分析现有自动驾驶数据集的方法，用于引导式数据集创建，并识别卡车等重型车辆的典型用例以支持采集规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境对自动驾驶感知极具挑战，但现有数据集多为乘用车、城市场景覆盖少，且忽略卡车、公交、有轨电车等运输模式，这些平台在地理与运行约束上差异显著。

Method: 利用用户生成的地图数据对现有数据集进行覆盖度与地理分布分析，据此提出引导式的数据集创建方法；并归纳代表性卡车专用用例作为示例。

Result: 展示了所提方法在识别数据集覆盖缺口与规划针对性采集方面的适用性，给出了卡车相关用例的示例说明。

Conclusion: 该工作为面向城市重型车辆感知的数据集采集提供了分析框架与规划依据，但尚未涉及实际采集完成或感知模型验证。

Abstract: Urban environments present significant challenges for the perception systems of automated driving due to their high complexity, characterized by dense traffic, diverse obstacles, or a wide range of possible scenarios. While numerous automotive datasets exist, few focus on urban settings, and the majority are acquired exclusively on passenger cars and overlook other transport modalities such as heavy-duty trucks, busses and trams. In contrast to passenger vehicles, these platforms operate under different geographical and operational constraints; including traversing pedestrian zones or platform-specific tasks such as movement of trailer or winter service. In this work a method is proposed for a guided dataset creation based on an analysis of existing datasets using user-generated map data. Furthermore, representative truck-specific use cases are identified and exemplified to demonstrate the applicability of the proposed methodology and to support targeted acquisition planning.

</details>


### [164] [VICAR: Video Imitation with Contact-Aware Retargeting for Humanoid Interactive Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13079)
*Dvij Kalaria,Bike Zhang,S. Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICAR提出从第三人称人类视频学习人形机器人接触丰富交互技能：先从单目视频恢复人体运动并重建任务障碍物，再以全局接触位姿、速度、碰撞间隙为高优先级拉格朗日惩罚项做全身重定向，训练接触条件运动生成器（输入含可移动接触点）以支持运行时接触位置自适应，在10项技能（乒乓发球、抓取、爬梯）上验证并部署于真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务特定奖励工程、遥操作和手工脚本演示成本高，第三人称人类视频是更可扩展的替代方案。现有video-to-humanoid系统虽能提取带噪人体运动/物体轨迹/接触并精炼为可部署技能，但接触丰富模仿仍存在关键缺口：任务由世界坐标系接触定义，其位姿、时序与速度必须同时跨本体差异与运行时场景变化保持，而现有重定向方法仅消除穿透或保留局部交互几何，无法满足该要求。

Method: 三步流水线：（1）从单目第三人称视频恢复人体运动并重建任务相关障碍物；（2）接触感知重定向——将用户指定的接触位姿与碰撞间隙建模为高优先级拉格朗日惩罚项，对整段运动做全局优化，使运动在接触丰富过渡中保持平滑，同时保持全局接触位姿、运动速度与碰撞间隙；（3）用重定向后运动训练接触条件运动生成器，其输入包含可移动接触点，从而在运行时对新的接触位置自适应。

Result: 在10项多样化第三人称视频技能上验证框架（含6个乒乓球发球、3个物体抓取任务与爬梯），并将学到的技能部署到真实人形硬件上运行。

Conclusion: 接触保持式重定向是人形机器人通过观看人类视频学习有用交互行为的一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Third-person human videos offer a scalable alternative to task-specific reward engineering, teleoperation, and manually scripted demonstrations for humanoid loco-manipulation. Recent video-to-humanoid systems have shown that noisy human motion, object trajectories, and contacts can be extracted from unstructured videos and refined into deployable skills. However, a key gap remains for contact-rich imitation: the task is often defined by world-frame contacts whose poses, timing, and speed must survive embodiment mismatch and runtime scene changes. Towards this, we present VICAR (Video Imitation with Contact-Aware Retargeting), a pipeline for learning humanoid interactive skills from third-person human videos. The system first recovers human motion, reconstructs task-relevant obstacles from monocular video, and then retargets the motion to a humanoid while preserving global contact poses, motion speed, and collision clearance. Unlike retargeting methods that only remove penetration or preserve local interaction geometry, our formulation represents user-specified contact poses and collision clearance as high-priority Lagrangian penalty terms and optimizes the full motion to remain smooth through contact-rich transitions. The resulting motions are used to train a contact-conditioned motion generator whose inputs include movable contact points enabling runtime adaptation to new contact positions. We demonstrate the framework on ten diverse third-person video skills, including six table-tennis serves, three object-pickup tasks, and ladder climbing, and deploy the learned skills on hardware. These results suggest that contact-preserving retargeting is a practical step toward humanoids that learn useful interactive behavior by watching humans.

</details>


### [165] [Belief-Overlap Partial Inversion: Object-Centric Flow Replanning for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13087)
*Chen Yu,Sudarshan S Harithas,Juan Borbon,Dingqi Zhang,Jiuguang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BOPI：面向质量与脚轮刚度未知的推车任务，将在线估计、流匹配规划与全身控制闭环，并用"信念重叠部分反转"按信念分歧程度缩放重规划，仿真18目标误差降33%/47%，实机完成非完整推车平移与转向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过小幅探索性推动来学习陌生物体的运动响应，并据此决定下一步动作。本文希望将这一"推一下—看响应—再推"的行为机制赋予人形机器人，使其在推车质量与脚轮刚度未知时能从交互中在线辨识物体动力学。核心矛盾在于：在线信念更新与已执行中的规划之间存在张力——信念一变就换计划会打断当前机动（动作不连续、抖动、全身稳定受损），而坚持旧计划又会让机器人沿已被证据否定的路径继续推进。需要一种既响应新证据又不粗暴打断执行的折中机制。

Method: 提出对象中心（object-centric）闭环框架，将在线参数估计、流匹配（flow-based）规划与全身控制串联成回路。核心方法为 Belief-Overlap Partial Inversion（BOPI）：在信念发生偏移时，不整体重规划，而是在旧信念下将生成式流轨迹"部分反转"回到新旧信念一致（overlap）的区域，再在新信念下从该点向前重构成新轨迹；反转深度随新旧信念的吻合程度自适应缩放——分歧越大反转越深，分歧小则仅做局部修正，从而把修正幅度与证据变化量对齐。对象层面估计质量与脚轮刚度，规划层面输出推车流形上的运动目标，控制层面由人形机器人全身控制器实现接触与接触力。

Result: 在18个仿真目标上，与固定深度重规划基线相比，合并的最终位置误差与偏航（yaw）误差分别降低约33%和47%。硬件实验中人形机器人成功对非完整约束（non-holonomic）推车完成平移与转向操作，验证了在真实系统中在线估计与自适应重规划闭环的有效性。

Conclusion: BOPI表明，把重规划深度与信念一致度绑定，可缓解在线估计与执行之间的冲突，在保持轨迹平滑与全身稳定性的同时及时修正被证据否定的规划；该方法适用于未知载荷/被动关节特性的对象交互操作，为人形机器人非完整对象操作提供了一条可落地的闭环方案。局限在于评估目标数（18个）与场景类型有限，参数辨识收敛性与罕见信念跳变情形下的鲁棒性仍待更大规模验证。

Abstract: People often learn how an unfamiliar object moves through a small exploratory push, using its response to guide their next motion. Inspired by this behavior, we study humanoids pushing carts whose mass and caster stiffness are initially unknown. Our object-centric framework closes online estimation, flow-based planning, and whole-body control into a loop, creating a tension: replacing the plan whenever the belief shifts disrupts the maneuver under way, while keeping it strands the robot on a path its evidence has ruled out. Belief-Overlap Partial Inversion (BOPI) scales revision to agreement, inverting the flow under the old belief only as far as the beliefs disagree before reconstructing under the new one. Across 18 simulated goals it lowers pooled final position and yaw error by roughly 33% and 47% against fixed-depth replanning, and on hardware it translates and turns a non-holonomic cart.

</details>


### [166] [ReAT: Residual Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13093)
*Dijia Zhan,Zhuo Li,Xuemiao Xu,Sun Zheng,Jinyi Li,Wenjun Wang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReAT（残差动作分词）让每个离散动作 token 显式承担修复当前动作重建残差的角色，用解码回传作为反馈构建下一 token，形成编解码闭环；在19个操作任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归机器人策略依赖动作分词器把连续动作块压缩为离散预测目标。现有分词器虽能重建动作块，但每个 token 对重建动作的贡献是隐式的，token 之间缺乏显式分工与纠错机制，限制了策略的渐进式自我修正能力。

Method: 提出 Residual Action Tokenization (ReAT)：以已解码重建作为反馈，计算剩余动作误差，并据此构造下一个 token，使每个新 token 对实际解码前缀留下的误差作出响应，从而把编码与解码闭合成环，赋予每个 token 明确的残差细化角色。

Result: 在覆盖三个仿真基准与真实场景的19个操作任务上，8-token ReAT 策略的任务成功率优于所评估基线；在 LIBERO 上，4-token ReAT 以一半 token 预算超过所评估的 8-token 动作分词基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明该路径可支撑自纠正式策略学习，帮助自回归策略学会逐步细化自身的动作规划。

Abstract: Autoregressive robot policies rely on action tokenizers to convert continuous action chunks into compact sequences of discrete prediction targets. Existing tokenizers can reconstruct action chunks from discrete codes, but often leave implicit what each individual token contributes to the reconstructed action. We introduce Residual Action Tokenization (ReAT), which gives each token an explicit role in refining the current action reconstruction. It uses the decoded reconstruction as feedback to construct the next token from the remaining action error. This closes the loop between encoding and decoding, making each new token responsive to the errors left by the actual decoded prefix. Across 19 manipulation tasks spanning three simulation benchmarks and real-world settings, 8-token ReAT policies outperform the evaluated baselines in task success; on LIBERO, even 4-token ReAT surpasses the evaluated 8-token action-tokenization baselines at half the token budget. These results suggest a path toward action tokenization that supports self-corrective policy learning, helping autoregressive policies learn to progressively refine their own action plans.

</details>


### [167] [Forward Dynamics Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13117)
*Zipei Ma,Junzhe Jiang,Jiahui Zhang,Juntong Zhang,Chun Gu,Bozhou Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出前向动力学模型（FDM），以“动作到未来”的方式让动作特征条件化地预测未来世界状态，从而在训练中获得未来状态的显式监督，同时避免推理时生成未来、也避免预测误差反向污染动作。骨架无关，兼容VLM与VGM，仿真与真机均验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA缺少未来世界状态的显式监督；WAM以预测未来为条件生成动作，既带来额外推理开销，又会让视觉预测误差传播到动作上。作者希望兼得“未来监督”的收益与“动作纯净”的鲁棒性，并去掉推理时的未来生成成本。

Method: FDM将动作与未来世界状态预测耦合在一个动作到未来的范式里：未来预测以动作特征为条件，绝不反向；端到端训练使真值未来状态通过这些特征监督策略，而预测误差无法流入动作分支。推理阶段完全跳过未来生成。该范式与骨干无关，作者分别用VLM骨干与VGM骨干实例化。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上跨两类骨干均验证有效；LIBERO上达到99.4%的平均成功率（SOTA），真机任务上相对VLA与WAM方法取得最高13%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: FDM表明“以动作为条件的未来预测”可作为纯粹的训练期辅助监督，兼顾VLA的简洁与WAM的世界状态监督优势，同时消除推理开销与误差传播，且不受骨干限制，具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models and world action models (WAMs) have advanced robotic policy learning by building on pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) and video generative models (VGMs), respectively. Yet VLAs learn without explicit supervision from future world states, while WAMs that condition actions on predicted futures incur extra inference cost and let visual prediction errors propagate into actions. We present Forward Dynamics Models (FDM), a family of robotic action models that couples action and future world-state prediction through an action-to-future paradigm. Future prediction is conditioned on action features, never the reverse: end-to-end training lets ground-truth future states supervise the policy through these features, while prediction errors cannot propagate into actions. At inference, FDM skips future generation entirely, avoiding its computational cost. The paradigm is backbone-agnostic, and we instantiate it with both VLM and VGM backbones. Experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate the effectiveness of FDM across both backbone families, achieving a state-of-the-art 99.4% average success rate on LIBERO and absolute gains of up to 13% over VLA and WAM methods on real-world tasks.

</details>


### [168] [The value of information and the emergence of communication in evolved e-puck robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13118)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在PyBullet中复现两台e-puck机器人觅食任务，用带残差路径的GRU单独控制或嵌入EASA架构，研究通信行为何时涌现及先天行动选择架构的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自主智能体间通信涌现的条件，以及为单体任务设计的先天行动选择脚手架在社交任务中是否成为阻碍。

Method: 两台e-puck机器人（从Webots模型重建到PyBullet并验证）在有界环境中寻找仅当经过其上时才能探测到的觅食区；机器人可发送1比特信号，方向由接收方感知；控制器为带残差通路的GRU网络，独立运行或作为EASA内的一个通道；适应度仅为停留于食物区的时间；对比有无罗盘、小/大场地、演化环境等条件。

Result: 有罗盘指向食物时信号使用完全消失；无罗盘小场地三次独立运行以指称信号、常亮信标或不使用信号三种策略达到相近性能；场地扩大时指称信号策略泛化最佳；在大场地直接演化不提升通信频率但分离策略，指称信号明显优于独搜；EASA内先天反射约束网络、降低性能，演化随优先级变化持续削弱这些反射。

Conclusion: 通信的涌现程度与所传递信息的价值成正比；为孤立智能体设计的先天脚手架可能成为社交任务中的阻碍。

Abstract: We study when communication emerges between autonomous agents and what role an innate action selection architecture plays. Two e-puck robots, reconstructed in PyBullet from their Webots model and validated against it, must find a feeding zone that can only be detected when a robot passes over it. Each robot can emit a one-bit signal whose direction is detected by the receiver. Control is provided by a GRU recurrent network with a residual path, either operating alone or as a channel within an Embedded Action Selection Architecture (EASA). Fitness measures only the time the robots spend over the food. When the robots receive a compass pointing to the food, use of the signal disappears completely. Without a compass, in a small arena, three independent runs achieved similar performance using different strategies: a referential signal, a permanent beacon, or no use of the signal. As the arena expands, the strategy based on a referential signal generalizes best. In contrast, evolving directly in the large arena does not increase the frequency of communication, but it does separate the strategies, and the referential signal clearly outperforms solitary searching. Within EASA, innate reflexes constrain the network and reduce performance. As its priorities evolve, evolution consistently weakens those reflexes. The results suggest that communication emerges in proportion to the value of the information it transmits, and that an innate scaffolding designed for isolated agents can be an impediment in a social task.

</details>


### [169] [Human2Dex: Dexterous Robot Learning from Wrist-Centric Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13119)
*Jiachen Zhang,Yuanyuan Li,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Human2Dex，一种以手腕为中心的人类示教接口，将共享的人类观测与运动语义和具体手部本体执行解耦，使一套人类示教语料可复用于 Linker O6 与 Wuji 两种灵巧手，并引入 GPIR 表示以表达以手为中心的功能抓握凹槽坐标系下的物体位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手示教数据采集需针对每个机器人手重复进行任务特定的数据获取，成本高、难以扩展。作者希望验证：一套共享的人类自然示教语料能否作为可复用的数据接口，服务于不同灵巧手，而无需在每个目标机器人上重新采集任务示教。

Method: 以手腕为中心的示教接口：通过局部手部外观自适应和跨人机一致的 21 点手部表征对齐观测，保持结构语义一致性；融合人类手部运动并重定向至各目标手以构建可执行监督；提出 Grasp-Pocket Interaction Representation (GPIR)，将被操作物体表达在以手为中心的功能抓握凹槽坐标系中。语料按手分别转换为 Linker O6 与 Wuji 的训练集，动作标签、归一化、训练与扩散策略检查点均保持手部特定。

Result: 在六个真实世界任务上，Linker O6 成功 109/120（90.8%），Wuji 成功 97/120（80.8%）。跨两手平均：仅用原始人类数据（Human-Raw）为 63/240（26.3%），完整 Human2Dex 配置为 206/240（85.8%）。

Conclusion: 结果支持将以手腕为中心的人类示教作为跨已标定灵巧手的可复用数据接口，同时保留本体特定的策略学习。

Abstract: Collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations for each robot hand requires repeated task-specific data acquisition. We ask whether one shared corpus of natural human demonstrations can instead serve as a reusable data interface for different dexterous hands, without collecting task demonstrations on each target robot. We present Human2Dex, a wrist-centric human demonstration interface that separates shared human observation and motion semantics from embodiment-specific execution. It aligns observations through local hand-appearance adaptation and a shared 21-point hand representation with consistent structural semantics across human and robot observations. It constructs executable supervision by retargeting fused human hand motion to each target hand, and introduces the Grasp-Pocket Interaction Representation (GPIR) to express tracked objects relative to a hand-centered Functional Grasp Pocket. The corpus is converted into separate training sets for Linker O6 and Wuji, while action labels, normalization, training, and diffusion-policy checkpoints remain hand-specific. Across six real-world tasks, Human2Dex achieves 109/120 (90.8%) successes on Linker O6 and 97/120 (80.8%) on Wuji. Across both hands, Human-Raw achieves 63/240 (26.3%) successes, whereas the complete Human2Dex configuration achieves 206/240 (85.8%). These results support the use of wrist-centric human demonstrations as a reusable interface across calibrated dexterous hands while retaining embodiment-specific policy learning. The project website is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [170] [Low-Latency Mapless ESDF Queries for MAV Reactive Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13158)
*Leonard Freißmuth,Cedric Le Gentil,Helen Oleynikova,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无地图、GPU加速的ESDF查询方法，直接作用于带位姿的深度图，预处理开销可忽略，分辨率由图像池化决定；嵌入MPC后在仿真森林和真实林区实现高速低碰撞飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式避障需要在毫秒级内感知并修正运动，但现有方法依赖地图进行高频碰撞查询，其加速结构需预处理，只在长时域规划的大量查询中才能摊销；反应式规划是近场短时域问题，每次测量查询次数可能仅数百次，建图成本不划算。

Method: 直接在带位姿的深度图上进行GPU加速的欧氏符号距离场（ESDF）查询，避免显式地图与体素建图预处理；查询分辨率由图像池化而非体素尺寸决定，并以可忽略的预处理在嵌入式GPU上运行。

Result: 在嵌入式GPU上，该方法比测试的所有体素网格配置（2-20 cm）同时更准确且总延迟更低，在同等分辨率下内存低1-2个数量级；与MPC集成后，在激光扫描森林仿真中以最高9 m/s飞行，密集树枝中碰撞率0.4%，优于最佳体素网格的5.9%和最佳无地图基线的5.5%；并已在两个林区机载部署，以最高4 m/s指令速度飞行。

Conclusion: 无地图、低预处理的GPU ESDF查询在反应式避障中优于体素地图方案，兼具精度、延迟与内存优势，并在真实森林环境中完成机载部署验证。

Abstract: Reactive collision avoidance in unknown, cluttered environments must perceive obstacles and correct motion within milliseconds.
