Existing methods rely on maps for high-rate collision queries, but their acceleration structures require pre- processing. That cost is amortized in long-horizon planning, where a map serves millions of queries; reactive planning, however, is a near-field, short-horizon problem with potentially only hundreds of queries per measurement, where building a map may not pay off. We propose a mapless, Graphics Processing Unit (GPU)-accelerated Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF) query with negligible pre-processing that operates directly on posed depth images, with resolution set by pooling the image rather than by a voxel size. On an embedded GPU it is simultaneously more accurate and lower in total latency than every voxel-grid configuration we measured between 2 cm and 20 cm, at one to two orders of magnitude less memory at matched resolution. With our query integrated into a Model Predictive Controller (MPC), simulated runs in laser-scanned forests at up to 9 m/s collide in 0.4 % through dense branches, against 5.9 % with the best voxel grid and 5.5 % with the best mapless baseline at our resolution, whose failures we attribute to voxel resolution and total latency respectively. We deploy the system onboard at two forest sites and fly at commanded speeds of up to 4 m/s.

</details>


### [171] [Grounded-Dreamer: Robot Learning from World Model Synthetic Data with Physical Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13164)
*Feng Jiang,Shu Chen,Yuanfei Wang,Yuan Li,Zhenhao Shen,Jiaqi Liang,Yang Chen,Lei Wei,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Grounded-Dreamer 结合生成式视频世界模型与仿真物理引擎，从少量真实初始帧自动重建物体与相机并采集物理有效轨迹，用于后训练视频世界模型与逆动力学模型，仅用合成的“有物理根基”轨迹训练真实机器人策略，无需人工遥操作演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略扩展受限于带动作标注数据的获取成本：人工遥操作昂贵难扩展，纯仿真视觉真实性差，人类视频缺乏本体相关的动作标注。作者希望在不依赖人工演示的前提下，合成既视觉真实又物理可行的机器人操作数据。

Method: 1) 从少量真实任务初始状态图像出发，自动重建任务相关物体与相机几何，并在仿真中对齐；2) 在仿真中采集物理有效轨迹；3) 用这些轨迹后训练视频世界模型和逆动力学模型；4) 由真实图像生成视觉真实的操作视频与对应机器人动作；5) 将生成的轨迹回放至重建仿真中做物理可行性过滤，仅保留成功完成任务的数据；6) 完全基于该合成轨迹训练真实机器人策略。

Result: 真实世界任务上的实验表明，Grounded-Dreamer 能合成物理有根基的数据，并在无需任何人工遥操作的情况下显著促进策略学习。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将视频世界模型与仿真物理结合的机器人数据合成框架，通过仿真回放过滤保证物理可行性，验证了无人工演示条件下合成数据的策略学习价值，为策略规模化提供新路径。

Abstract: Scaling robot manipulation policies requires large amounts of diverse, action-labeled data, yet collecting such data in the real world remains expensive and difficult to scale. Existing methods either depend on human teleoperation, rely on simulation with limited visual realism, or use human videos that lack embodiment-specific annotations. In this paper, we introduce Grounded-Dreamer, a framework for synthesizing physically grounded robot manipulation data with video world models while avoiding human teleoperation demonstrations. Grounded-Dreamer combines the visual realism of generative video models with simulator physics engines. Starting from a small set of real images depicting robot task initial states, our method automatically reconstructs task-relevant objects and camera geometry, aligns them in simulation, and collects physically valid trajectories. These trajectories are used to post-train both a video world model and an inverse dynamics model, enabling the generation of visually realistic robot manipulation videos and corresponding robot actions from real images. To filter physically implausible rollouts, Grounded-Dreamer replays generated trajectories in the reconstructed simulation and retains only those that successfully complete the tasks. The real-world robot policies are trained solely on the grounded synthetic trajectories without any human demonstrations. Empirical results in real-world tasks demonstrate that Grounded-Dreamer can synthesize physically grounded data and substantially facilitate policy learning without requiring human teleoperation effort.

</details>


### [172] [Prediction-Informed Landing Window Planning for UAV Landing on USV in High Sea States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13190)
*Kaizhen Li,Xi Zhou,Dan Zhang,Xiaowei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测感知的分层控制框架，显式预测USV姿态以辅助高海况下的无人机自主着舰窗口与触地时机选择，并构建real-sim验证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海况下UAV在USV上自主降落困难；现有分层着舰方法多从短观测历史隐式推断USV未来运动，难以适应剧烈且快速变化的海况，导致着舰窗口与触地时机选择不可靠。

Method: 预测驱动的分层控制框架：高层预训练模型显式预测USV姿态，用于识别可着舰窗口并选择触地时机；低层采用常规飞行控制器保持UAV稳定并跟踪运动指令。构建real-sim验证，将真实海况采集的USV运动数据迁移到MuJoCo中USV模型上进行评测。

Result: 实验表明，显式姿态预测可提升着舰窗口选择质量，并支持在快速变化海况下进行时机感知的UAV着舰规划，验证了框架可行性。

Conclusion: 显式姿态预测相比隐式历史推断更利于高海况UAV-USV着舰决策，但验证停留在real-sim仿真，尚缺真实海试部署证据。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) on unmanned surface vehicle (USV) is difficult in high sea states. Existing hierarchical landing methods often infer future USV motion only implicitly from short observation histories, which limits their ability to adapt to severe and rapidly changing sea conditions. This paper presents a prediction-informed hierarchical control framework. A pretrained model is developed to explicitly predict the USV attitude during high-level landing decisions. The prediction helps identify suitable landing windows and choose the right touchdown time, while a conventional low-level flight controller maintains UAV stability and tracks motion commands. To evaluate the framework under realistic disturbances, a real-sim validation setup is constructed in which USV-motion data collected in real sea conditions are transferred to a MuJoCo USV. The experiments demonstrate the feasibility of using explicit attitude prediction to improve landing-window selection and support timing-aware UAV landing planning in rapidly varying marine environments.

</details>


### [173] [PATACON: GPU-Parallel Tangent-Bundle RRT-Connect for Constrained Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13195)
*Gahyun Oh,Junho An,Suhyun Jeon,Suhan Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PATACON提出一种GPU并行的双向采样式规划器，直接在人形机器人高维约束流形的切空间中探索，避免了对偏离流形候选点的反复投影，在高约束、高维任务上较前作提速至多11.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身运动规划需在动态环境中持续重规划，同时满足避障与运动学约束。但约束规划的计算瓶颈不仅来自碰撞检测与最近邻搜索，更来自对采样候选点的反复约束投影；现有的GPU大规模并行规划器仍在环境配置空间中扩展，导致高维、高余维约束场景下投影开销依旧显著。

Method: 提出PATACON：一种GPU并行的双向运动规划器，利用已知约束函数诱导的局部流形几何，在切空间中执行扩展/探索，使采样候选天然近似满足约束，从而减少投影次数；算法在GPU上进行大规模并行化，并与约束投影、碰撞检测、最近邻搜索等模块协同。

Result: 在双臂闭链与人形全身约束规划任务上，随着配置空间维度与约束余维的增加，PATACON优于先前方法，最高取得11.25倍加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明，GPU并行切空间探索可将约束下的全身运动规划推向近实时性能，为动态环境中的连续重规划提供了可行的效率路径。

Abstract: Fast whole-body motion planning enables continuous replanning for humanoid robots in dynamic environments while satisfying collision avoidance and kinematic constraints. However, constrained motion planning incurs substantial computational cost due to repeated constraint projection in addition to collision checking and nearest-neighbor search. Recently, GPU-based massively parallel planners have been proposed to reduce computational cost. Nevertheless, these planners still steer in the ambient configuration space, so projecting candidates that deviate from the manifold remains a significant source of overhead, particularly in highly constrained, high-dimensional problems. In this work, we introduce PATACON, a GPU-parallel bidirectional planner that explores tangent space to exploit the local manifold geometry induced by known constraint functions. Experiments on dual-arm closed-chain and humanoid whole-body constrained planning tasks show that PATACON outperforms the prior work as configuration-space dimension and constraint codimension grow, achieving up to an 11.25 times speedup. These results suggest that GPU-parallel tangent-space exploration brings constrained whole-body motion planning closer to real-time performance.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Actions: Learning Future Operation Targets for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13212)
*Min Lin,Bokai Zhou,Jianqi Lin,Kaiyuan Yang,Yicheng Chang,Junjie Yu,Xiaofan Liu,Jianhua Han,Rui Li,Shen Zhao,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AFCE，将“未来物理效果（Effect）”作为 VLA 策略的主预测目标，替代传统的动作块预测，在 DexJoCo 双臂灵巧操作上取得 50.97% 总体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作只规定机器人执行什么，却不描述这些命令会产生什么结果。现有 VLA 策略主要依赖从图像、语言、状态预测动作块，缺少对动作所引发的物理变化的显式监督。

Method: 用离线 tokenizer 从示范动作、真实机器人响应与稀疏视觉转移中构建时序有序的 Effect token，并以重建目标保留可执行细节与观测到的物理变化；以 flow-matching VLA 从当前图像、指令和机器人状态预测 Effect，再由状态条件解码器将其转换为动作块；通过具身专用接口让单臂与双臂任务共享同一 Effect 模型。

Result: 在 DexJoCo 上总体成功率 50.97%，分别比 π₀.₅ 和 UVT 提升 11.82 与 15.21 个百分点，并在全部五个双臂任务上领先。

Conclusion: 以未来物理效果作为监督信号对协同灵巧操作具有显著价值，Effect 预测可作为动作块预测的有效替代或补充范式。

Abstract: Actions specify what a robot should execute, but do not describe what those commands will achieve. Vision-language-action (VLA) policies nevertheless learn primarily by predicting action chunks from images, language, and state. We introduce Action--Future Coupled Effect Learning (AFCE), which makes a future-grounded physical Effect the policy's primary prediction target. An offline tokenizer constructs temporally ordered Effect tokens from demonstrated actions, realized robot responses, and sparse visual transitions. Reconstruction objectives encourage the representation to retain executable detail and observed physical change. A flow-matching VLA predicts Effects from current images, instruction, and robot state, while a state-conditioned decoder converts them into action chunks. Embodiment-specific interfaces allow single- and dual-arm tasks to share the Effect model. On DexJoCo, AFCE achieves $50.97\%$ overall success, improving over $\pi_{0.5}$ and UVT by 11.82 and 15.21 percentage points, respectively. It leads on all five bimanual tasks, highlighting the value of Effect supervision for coordinated dexterous manipulation. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [175] [Dual-Stream Memory for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13262)
*Yang Wang,Ping Liu,Ruiyu Wang,Xuhua Chen,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双流记忆架构用于VLA策略：Gated DeltaNet压缩历史为时序token，任务条件检索器保留高相关帧，以线性多尺度融合编码空间token，冻结时序压缩器后联合优化检索与策略；RoboMME十六任务均值49.22%（对比44.51%），真机四任务62.5%（对比37.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖型操作任务要求策略同时具备两项能力：跟踪执行状态（progress/阶段），以及在视觉证据不再可观测时将其恢复。单一表征难以兼顾，故需将二者分配给互补的表示。

Method: 双流记忆：(1) 任务无关的Gated DeltaNet将历史压缩为单个时序token，用进度预测与动作条件特征重建进行监督；(2) 任务条件检索器保留相关性高于固定阈值的帧，线性多尺度融合将每帧编码为16个空间token。随后冻结时序压缩器，联合优化检索与策略学习。

Result: RoboMME 16个任务平均成功率49.22% vs FrameSamp+Modul 44.51%，14个任务及4个套件均值全部提升；真机4任务平均62.5% vs 37.5%，在需要执行阶段区分的重复动作上增益最大；杯子交换场景下失败，暴露持续身份跟踪仍未解决。

Conclusion: 执行上下文（时序进度）与选择性视觉访问的组合有效，二者互补；但物体身份跟踪在遮挡/交换下仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Memory-dependent manipulation requires a policy to track execution state and recover visual evidence that is no longer observable. We propose dual-stream memory for vision-language-action policies, assigning these requirements to complementary representations. A task-agnostic Gated DeltaNet compresses history into one temporal token, supervised by progress prediction and action-conditioned feature reconstruction. A task-conditioned retriever retains frames above a fixed relevance threshold; linear multi-scale fusion encodes each frame into sixteen spatial tokens. We then freeze the temporal compressor and jointly optimize retrieval and policy learning. Across sixteen RoboMME tasks, the complete model achieves 49.22% mean success versus 44.51% for the published FrameSamp+Modul reference, improving fourteen tasks and all four suite means. On four real-robot tasks, mean success reaches 62.5% versus 37.5%, with the largest gains on repeated actions requiring execution-stage discrimination. These results support combining execution context with selective visual access, while failures under cup swaps expose persistent identity tracking as an unresolved requirement.

</details>


### [176] [Evo-RL: Iterative Robot Reinforcement Learning from Selective Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13345)
*Kai Ye,Yifan Jin,Qianhang Luo,Hongru Zhang,Jiedong Zhang,Shuyuan Hu,Song-Yuan Wu,Jihai Xu,Jie Zhao,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Evo-RL 迭代式真实世界强化学习方法，将人类干预与优势引导的经验选择结合，并发布 1000+ 小时、覆盖 4 种机器人本体的 RW-RL-Dataset；在高精度真实操作任务上成功率较 RECAP 提升 30.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界 RL 常将人类修正与自主 rollout 经验混合使用，但经验对策略改进的价值参差不齐，成功轨迹中也可能包含低效或冗余行为，因此需要更有选择性地复用经验。

Method: Evo-RL 为迭代式 RL 框架：利用人类修正，以及来自成功轨迹且估计优势为正的经验来迭代精炼策略，实现优势引导的经验筛选；为支撑多来源经验学习，构建 RW-RL-Dataset，覆盖 4 种机器人本体、累计超过 1000 小时。

Result: 在一个真实世界高精度操作任务上，Evo-RL 的尝试成功率较 RECAP 提升 30.46%。代码、模型检查点与数据集均已公开。

Conclusion: 选择性复用高价值经验（人类修正 + 正优势成功轨迹）能显著提升真实机器人策略学习效率；配套数据集与开源资源为该方向提供可复现基础。

Abstract: Existing real-world reinforcement learning methods improve robot policies by combining human corrections with autonomous rollouts. However, accumulated experience varies in its value for policy improvement, and even successful trajectories can contain inefficient or redundant behavior, motivating more selective experience reuse. To this end, we propose Evo-RL, an iterative reinforcement learning method that combines human interventions with advantage-guided experience selection. Evo-RL uses human corrections and experience with positive estimated advantages from successful trajectories to iteratively refine the policy. To support learning from these different experience sources, we introduce RW-RL-Dataset, which spans over 1,000 hours across four robot embodiments. On a real-world high-precision manipulation task, Evo-RL improves attempt success rate over RECAP by 30.46\%. Our code, model checkpoints, and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [177] [PartiGen: Part-Aware Representations for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13373)
*Chuxuan Huang,Zhanxiang Cao,Tianyue Zhan,Haoyang Li,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Chenlin Ming,Keqi Zhu,Yizhi Chen,Shengcheng Fu,Zihao Zhou,Xinyi Peng,Kaixuan Zhang,Boyang Xing,Jiawei Bao,Yang Yang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PartiGen：一种部件感知的潜在扩散框架，将解剖结构融入共享全身运动表示，以文本与运动历史为条件自回归生成运动基元，并配合前向运动学监督与终止预测器，在 Unitree G1 上实现更优生成质量与时长控制及真机执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的人形运动生成需同时解决三件事：身体各部位的协调、运动时长的确定、以及可被机器人执行的参考轨迹。既有方法多将全身当作整体建模，缺乏解剖结构先验，且多以固定长度截断，导致时长误差大、难以直接用于真实人形机器人。

Method: 提出 PartiGen，部件感知潜在扩散框架：在共享全身运动表示中注入解剖结构，用部件专属注意力处理按解剖掩码划分的帧特征，再融合局部肢体信息与全身上下文；以文本和运动历史为条件自回归生成运动基元；引入前向运动学监督保证运动学一致性；训练 end-of-sequence 预测器在生成后进行帧级端点选择。

Result: 在重定向到 Unitree G1 的 HumanML3D 上，段级 FID 为 3.165，较 TextOp 降低 16.8%，R@1 提升 1.4 个百分点。16 帧基元的时长研究中，端点预测把平均绝对时长误差从固定长度截断的 12.471 帧降至 3.080 帧。用冻结的 GRIT 跟踪器评估执行：500 条参考中 409 条（81.8%）通过 MuJoCo 姿态稳定性筛查；经仿真筛查与人工挑选后，真机 G1 上 27 个尝试动作中 26 个成功。

Conclusion: 将解剖结构显式建模进共享全身表示，并以自回归基元＋终止预测替代固定长度截断，可同时提升生成质量、时长控制精度与下游机器人可执行性，在仿真和真机人形平台上均得到验证。

Abstract: Translating language into humanoid motion requires coordinating body parts, determining motion duration, and producing references suitable for robot execution. We introduce PartiGen, a part-aware latent diffusion framework that incorporates anatomical structure into a shared whole-body motion representation. Part-specific attention processes anatomically masked frame features, and its outputs are fused to combine local limb information with whole-body context. Conditioned on text and motion history, the model generates motion primitives autoregressively. Forward-kinematics supervision encourages kinematic consistency, while a learned end-of-sequence predictor selects a frame-level endpoint after generation. On HumanML3D retargeted to the Unitree G1, PartiGen achieves a segment-level FID of 3.165, a 16.8% reduction over TextOp, and improves R@1 by 1.4 percentage points. In the duration study with 16-frame primitives, endpoint prediction reduces mean absolute duration error from 12.471 to 3.080 frames compared with a fixed-length cutoff. We further evaluate robot execution using a pretrained, frozen GRIT tracker: 409 of 500 generated references (81.8%) pass the posture-based stability screen in MuJoCo. Following simulation screening and manual selection, 26 of 27 attempted motions are judged successful on the real G1.

</details>


### [178] [EndoRSI: Physical-Evidence-Driven Recursive Improvement of Endoscopic Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13376)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Tianchun Wu,Siu Hing Lui,Hanlin Zhao,Huxin Gao,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EndoRSI（内窥镜递归自我改进）：人监督下由 EndoForge 编码智能体依据物理实验证据迭代开发与修正内窥镜机器人策略，插入成功率从第一代 2/21 提升至第四代 17/21。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理机器人策略研究仍依赖人类解读实验失败并决定控制器修改方向；作者希望验证物理实验能否作为智能体自主驱动策略迭代的"实验证据"，实现人可控的机器人研究自改进闭环。

Method: 嵌入的编码智能体（EndoForge）在安全约束、人工授权的运行时内迭代开发与精炼内窥镜机器人策略；物理执行被隔离。证据链为三段：验证证据使智能体否决初始动作表示；后续物理实验证伪了一个离线数据支持的插入策略（离线数据中不存在的危险闭环行为）；失败物理 rollout 被回灌进策略修订循环。最终策略被冻结后跨视觉与硬件变化测试，并在六个离体猪标本上评估。

Result: 插入成功率由第一代 2/21 提升至第四代 17/21；冻结后的最终策略在视觉与硬件偏移下保持能力，并在六个独立离体猪标本上取得最佳自主策略表现。

Conclusion: 物理机器人交互可作为智能体驱动机器人策略研究的实验证据，形成人治理、自我改进的机器人研究范式；离线数据的成功不能替代闭环物理验证。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is beginning to automate scientific discovery, yet physical robot-policy research still relies on scientists and engineers to interpret experimental failures and decide how controllers should be revised. We present EndoRSI (Endoscopic Recursive Self-Improvement), a human-governed, self-improving robotic research system in which the embedded EndoForge coding agent iteratively develops and refines endoscopic robot policies using evidence from physical experiments. Physical execution remains isolated within a safety-constrained runtime under human authorisation. In motorised endoscopy, validation evidence first caused the agent to reject an initial action representation, while subsequent physical experiments falsified an offline-supported insertion strategy by revealing hazardous closed-loop behaviour absent from the source data. Failed physical rollouts were then incorporated into policy revision; insertion success subsequently increased from 2/21 in the first generation to 17/21 in the fourth. The frozen final policy retained competence across visual and hardware shifts and was the best performing autonomous policy across six independent ex-vivo porcine specimens. These results show that physical robot interaction can serve as experimental evidence for agent-driven robot policy research.

</details>


### [179] [Robot-WATCHDOG : failure detection through object-centric graph representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13403)
*Quentin Rolland,Fabrice Mayran de Chamisso,Jean-Baptiste Mouret*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robot-WATCHDOG 提出自监督机器人策略失败检测框架，将操作场景建模为物体中心的时空图，并比较 TPC（几何偏差与关系一致性非参数分数）与 GnnT（GAT+Transformer 预测未来物体状态）两种失败分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界中机器人策略的失败难以显式定义，因此需要仅依赖正常（标称）演示进行失败检测；现有方法难以在容忍良性变化与捕捉语义、交互级错误之间取得平衡。

Method: 以物体中心时空图表示操作场景，在此基础上定义两类互补失败分数：TPC 为非参数化度量，刻画几何偏差与关系一致性；GnnT 为 Graph Attention Network 结合 Transformer，用于预测未来物体状态。

Result: 在公开基准与新建的语义复杂操作任务数据集上，覆盖 6 个任务、10 个随机种子，两种分数均优于基于图像的失败检测器，在语义与交互级失败上尤为明显；TPC 在两个数据集的平均 AUPR 与 MCC 上最强，且跨种子方差低。

Conclusion: 物体中心时空图表示可有效支撑仅用正常演示训练的失败检测，非参数几何/关系分数（TPC）在鲁棒性与准确率间表现最佳，为开放世界机器人策略部署提供可靠实时监控手段。

Abstract: Reliable real-time failure detection is critical for deploying learned robotic policies, yet defining failures explicitly is infeasible in open-world settings. This motivates failure detection from nominal demonstrations alone. Existing methods, however, struggle to balance robustness to benign variations with the ability to capture semantic and interaction-level errors. We introduce Robot-WATCHDOG, a self-supervised framework that models manipulation scenes as object-centric spatio-temporal graphs. Building on this representation, we investigate two complementary failure scores: Trajectory Projection and Correlation (TPC), a non-parametric measure of geometric deviation and relational consistency, and GnnT, a Graph Attention Network with a Transformer that predicts future object states. We evaluate both on a public benchmark and on a new dataset of semantically complex manipulation tasks, across six tasks and ten random seeds. Both scores outperform image-based failure detectors, particularly on semantic and interaction-level failures. TPC provides the strongest performance in AUPR and MCC on both dataset averages, with low variance across seeds. Qualitative results are made available at this https URL .

</details>


### [180] [ManeuverFlow: Maneuver Hypothesis Generation with Future Evidence for Trajectory Evaluation and Refinement in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13409)
*Yuhao Wei,Yong Chen,Chen Yang,Yingchen Wang,Delin Ouyang,Lingfeng Qi,Qi Lan,Zhanhua Zhang,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManeuverFlow 用单步流匹配（MeanFlow）按驾驶意图生成轨迹，并以当前+未来证据（世界模型预测）做 PDM 打分与 Frenet 残差精修，NAVSIM v1/v2 达 90.6/90.1 PDMS，3DGS 闭环基准取得最高 PDMS* 75.19/74.58。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端规划需覆盖多样驾驶意图并依据场景上下文与未来风险选轨迹，但多查询回归易模式坍塌；基于锚点或生成式规划器存在候选集规模瓶颈：小候选集限制机动覆盖与高质量轨迹发现，大候选集则推高生成与打分成本。

Method: 1) 选取相关机动模式，从高斯混合先验中采样对应噪声；2) 经机动路由的 MoE MeanFlow 场单步映射为意图特定轨迹；3) 世界模型预测未来特征，与当前特征沿候选路径聚合为轨迹级证据，用于 PDM 因子预测；4) 精修器基于同一证据预测选中候选的局部 Frenet 残差，精修与保留候选联合重打分后选择。骨干为轻量 TransFuser 风格 ResNet-34。

Result: NAVSIM v1/v2 navtest 分别达 90.6 PDMS 与 90.1 EPDMS；WorldEngine 3DGS 闭环基准在日志流量与 IDM 控制流量下取得列出的最高 PDMS* 75.19 与 74.58；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 用"意图→单步流生成 + 当前/未来证据打分精修"替代大规模候选采样，在较低算力（ResNet-34 骨干）下同时提升机动覆盖与轨迹质量，验证了世界模型未来证据与流匹配意图解耦在多模态规划中的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end planning in complex scenes must model diverse driving intentions and select the most appropriate trajectory using scene context and future risks. However, multi-query regression may collapse to one mode, while anchor-based and generative planners share a scaling bottleneck: small candidate sets limit maneuver coverage or discovery of high-quality trajectories, whereas large sets increase generation and scoring costs. We propose ManeuverFlow to generate trajectories for distinct intentions in one step and evaluate and refine them using current and future evidence. The model selects relevant maneuver modes, draws corresponding noise from the Gaussian mixture prior, and maps it to intention-specific trajectories through a maneuver-routed mixture-of-experts MeanFlow field. Current features and future features predicted by the world model are aggregated along each candidate path into trajectory-specific evidence for PDM-factor prediction. Using the same evidence, the refiner predicts local Frenet residuals for selected candidates; refined and retained candidates are then jointly re-scored for selection. With a lightweight TransFuser-style ResNet-34 backbone, ManeuverFlow achieves 90.6 PDMS and 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v1/v2 navtest, respectively. On WorldEngine's recent 3DGS closed-loop benchmark, it attains the highest listed PDMS$^\ast$ scores of 75.19 and 74.58 under logged and IDM-controlled traffic, respectively. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [181] [Reflect-VLA: Internalizing Reflective Guidance via Dual-Path On-Policy Self-Distillation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13453)
*Yuechen Luo,He Chong,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Hexin Zhang,Ruiqi Zhang,Ziying Song,Lei Yang,Zhi-Xin Yang,Fuxi Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将反思式指导作为训练期特权信息，通过双路径在线策略自蒸馏内化到单次前向推理的自动驾驶VLA规划模型中，无需RL即可获得密集纠正信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶VLA模型的RL微调依赖标量轨迹奖励，只能反映整体优劣，无法定位具体规划错误（场景理解、行为选择、轨迹执行），在长尾场景中错误被压缩成单一分数，缺乏可操作的纠正信号。

Method: 提出Reflect-VLA：将Reflective Guidance（把轨迹评估转为显式诊断与纠正指令）视为训练期特权信息，用Dual-Path On-Policy Self-Distillation（Dual-OPSD）内化其收益。驾驶员分数仅用于路由学生自生成轨迹：对高风险轨迹蒸馏教师token级密集纠正分布，对可靠轨迹施加自模仿。教师通过三阶段渐进构建：Driving-Knowledge Pretraining、Planning-and-Refinement SFT、Self-Refinement Rejection Sampling。推理时单次前向生成轨迹，无显式指导或迭代精修。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1/v2与Bench2Drive上取得有竞争力的规划性能，训练时间较RL方法减少90%以上。

Conclusion: 显式诊断式指导可作为可蒸馏的特权信息，替代标量奖励RL，在保持规划精度的同时大幅降低训练成本，并支持无引导的单次推理部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) substantially improves planning performance in autonomous-driving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, existing RL methods rely on scalar trajectory rewards that measure overall quality but fail to identify specific planning errors or provide actionable correction signals. In long-tail scenarios, errors in scene understanding, behavior selection, and trajectory execution are compressed into a single score. We observe that Reflective Guidance turns trajectory evaluation into explicit diagnoses and corrective instructions, substantially improving planning accuracy. Yet such guidance requires an initial prediction and its evaluation results, making it unavailable for direct single-pass inference. We propose Reflect-VLA, which treats Reflective Guidance as training-time privileged information and internalizes its planning benefits through Dual-Path On-Policy Self-Distillation (Dual-OPSD). Unlike scalar-reward optimization, Dual-OPSD uses the driving score only to route student-generated trajectories. It distills dense token-level corrective distributions from the teacher for risky trajectories while applying self-imitation to preserve reliable ones. To provide reliable corrective distributions on student rollouts, the teacher is progressively constructed through Driving-Knowledge Pretraining, Planning-and-Refinement SFT, and Self-Refinement Rejection Sampling. At inference time, Reflect-VLA generates trajectories in a single pass without explicit guidance or iterative refinement. Experiments on NAVSIM v1/v2 and Bench2Drive demonstrate competitive planning performance while reducing training time by over 90% compared with RL.

</details>


### [182] [RESCUE: Is This Alarm Worth Acting On? Counterfactual Evaluation of VLA Runtime Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13516)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Abdelwahed Khamis,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RESCUE反事实评估框架，用模拟器与策略状态回滚比较替代动作，衡量机器人策略运行时失败监控器告警是否带来有效干预收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时监控器多以"能否区分失败/成功执行"的检测精度来评价，但精确告警可能来得太晚无法恢复，而不必要的干预又会破坏本可成功的执行；即监控器的价值应取决于其是否促成了有用的干预，而非单纯的检测分数。

Method: RESCUE为反事实评估框架：在执行检查点处恢复模拟器与策略状态，比较替代动作，找出满足指定恢复判据的最晚检查点，并评估基于告警采取行动是否降低期望任务完成成本；在OpenVLA-OFT与π0.5上，于LIBERO-Spatial与LIBERO-Plus中进行128,340条反事实分支的评估。

Result: 可恢复性取决于"状态—干预"配对而非仅机器人状态：τ=0.5时，66个OpenVLA-OFT/LIBERO-Plus失败中有14个在单纯继续下存在可恢复检查点，而在继续或脚本化恢复下为34个；其他条件下恢复反而降低可恢复性。在强策略主条件下，良好的检测分数并不意味着更低干预成本；在评估的stop-and-reset模型下，即使理想检测器也仅能承受2.0–3.3%的误报率，否则不必要重置的代价会超过提前止损的收益。

Conclusion: 监控器价值共同取决于可用的干预方式与其部署的任务负载；评估应超越检测准确率指标，转向反事实的干预效用度量。

Abstract: Runtime failure monitors for robot policies are typically evaluated by how accurately they distinguish failed from successful executions. Yet an accurate alarm may arrive too late for recovery, while an unnecessary intervention may disrupt an execution that would otherwise succeed. We introduce RESCUE, a counterfactual framework for evaluating whether a monitor's alarms enable useful interventions. By restoring simulator and policy states at execution checkpoints, RESCUE compares alternative actions, identifies the latest checkpoint satisfying a specified recovery criterion, and evaluates whether acting on alarms reduces expected task-completion cost. We evaluate RESCUE with OpenVLA-OFT and $\pi_{0.5}$ over LIBERO-Spatial and LIBERO-Plus using 128,340 counterfactual branches. Recoverability depends on the state--intervention pair rather than on robot state alone: at $\tau=0.5$, 14 of 66 OpenVLA-OFT/LIBERO-Plus failures contain a recoverable checkpoint under continuation, compared with 34 of 66 under continuation or scripted recovery, while recovery reduces recoverability in other conditions. On the primary strong-policy conditions, favourable detection scores do not establish lower intervention cost. Under the evaluated stop-and-reset model, even an idealised detector can afford only a 2.0--3.3% false-alarm rate before unnecessary resets outweigh the savings from stopping failures early. These results show that monitor value depends jointly on the intervention available and the workload in which it is deployed.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Successful Robot Demonstrations: Learning from Human Interactions and Modeling Failure Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13522)
*Peiyan Tu,Cong Wang,Hanxin Zhu,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HF-WM：结合人类交互视频学习与失败感知后训练的动作条件世界模型，通过双分支（视频生成+稳定点流）弥合人-机具身差异，并用GRPO在扰动动作上学习失败动力学，实现SOTA动作条件视频生成与策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据集交互动力学覆盖不足；大规模第一人称人类视频虽可补充交互经验，但人手与机械臂存在具身差异；且现有数据以成功演示为主，失败动力学严重缺失，导致世界模型难以预测失败执行后果。

Method: 提出HF-WM：1）双分支框架，将视频生成与辅助pointflow预测耦合，用稳定化pointflow作为跨人类/机器人交互的统一运动表示，并抑制第一人称相机自运动以弥合具身差距；2）失败感知后训练，在由成功演示导出的扰动动作上执行GRPO，以动作对齐与物理合理性为奖励，无需配对真值视频即可学习合理的失败动力学。

Result: 大量实验表明，HF-WM在动作条件视频生成上达到SOTA，并在下游机器人任务的策略评估中展现出较强实用性。

Conclusion: 跨具身人类交互学习加上失败感知的后训练，可有效提升动作条件世界模型的泛化性与失败预测能力，为通用机器人智能提供更有价值的世界模型基础。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models offer a promising foundation for generalist robotic intelligence by predicting the consequences of actions. However, existing robotic datasets provide limited coverage of the diverse interaction dynamics required for generalizable world modeling. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer complementary interaction experience, yet the embodiment gap between human hands and robotic manipulators hinders effective cross-embodiment learning. Meanwhile, the predominance of successful demonstrations leaves failure dynamics substantially underrepresented, limiting the ability of world models to anticipate unsuccessful executions. To address these limitations, we introduce HF-WM, an action-conditioned world model that combines Human interaction learning with Failure-aware post-training. To bridge the embodiment gap, we develop a dual-branch framework that couples video generation with auxiliary pointflow prediction, using stabilized pointflow as a unified motion representation across human and robot interactions while suppressing egocentric camera motion. To improve failure modeling, we further perform Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training on perturbed actions derived from successful demonstrations, guided by action alignment and physical plausibility rewards, enabling the model to learn plausible failure dynamics without paired ground-truth videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HF-WM achieves state-of-the-art performance in action-conditioned video generation and exhibits strong utility for policy evaluation in downstream robotic tasks.

</details>


### [184] [ATHLETE: Learning Reactive Humanoid Tennis via Trajectory-Guided Motion Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13572)
*Zhihao Tian,Zifan Wang,Enzheng Zhu,Xun Yang,Jialong Qin,Mengbo Wang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATHLETE 框架利用通用运动数据与普通网球视频，通过轨迹引导运动匹配和物理教师-学生 DAgger 蒸馏，学习可实时反应的人形机器人网球技能，并在真机上实现正手90%、反手86.67%的击球成功率与多回合对打。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人网球需要快速变化球况下的全身反应协调，但采集覆盖多接近状态、击球位置与时机的任务专用动捕演示成本高昂。作者受人类通过复用运动经验加少量任务演示获得新技能的启发，寻求用通用运动数据与易得视频替代昂贵的动捕。

Method: ATHLETE 将 LAFAN1 运动数据与从视频提取的击球动作结合，使用轨迹引导运动匹配（TGMM）构建多样的任务条件化全身参考运动；再通过物理仿真中的教师-学生学习、在线 DAgger 蒸馏得到反应式策略，并用 PPO 针对网球任务微调。部署时策略直接由机器人与球的观测驱动，无需参考运动输入。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上，正手与反手击球成功率分别为90.00%和86.67%，并能与人类选手进行多回合连续对打。

Conclusion: 结果表明，ATHLETE 能够从可复用的运动数据与视频演示中习得动态人形网球技能，验证了跨数据源技能组合与无需参考输入的反应式全身控制的可行性。代码与视频已公开。

Abstract: Humanoid tennis requires reactive whole-body coordination under rapidly changing ball dynamics, yet collecting task-specific motion-capture demonstrations that cover diverse approach states, contact locations, and timing conditions is costly. Inspired by how humans acquire new skills by combining reusable motor experience with task-specific demonstrations, we present ATHLETE, a framework for learning reactive humanoid tennis from generic locomotion data and ordinary tennis videos. ATHLETE combines LAFAN1 locomotion with video-derived striking motions and uses Trajectory-Guided Motion Matching (TGMM) to construct diverse task-conditioned whole-body references. These references are transformed into a reactive policy through physics-based teacher--student learning with online DAgger distillation, followed by PPO fine-tuning for the tennis task. The resulting policy responds directly to robot and ball observations without requiring reference-motion inputs at deployment. On a real humanoid robot, ATHLETE achieves forehand and backhand hit rates of $90.00\%$ and $86.67\%$, respectively, and enables multi-shot rallies with human players. These results demonstrate that ATHLETE can learn dynamic humanoid tennis skills from reusable locomotion data and video demonstrations. Result videos and code can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [185] [RARA: Robust Policy Fine-Tuning through Retrieval-Anchored Representation Alignment for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13578)
*Xuhui Kang,Sung-Wook Lee,Yan Zhuang,Tianle Zhong,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段适应方法RARA，通过预训练特征检索对齐与微调过程中的表征漂移约束，缓解VLA/扩散策略少样本微调时的泛化退化，在13个任务上分布内提升9.5、分布外提升13.6个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略（如VLA）具备通用操作基础，但少样本适应新任务困难：直接微调虽分布内表现好，却会破坏预训练表征，导致分布外泛化下降。核心问题是微调阶段的表征漂移。

Method: RARA为两阶段适应：1）从预训练表征中检索任务意图与动作相似的演示，将目标演示的策略表征与检索演示的预训练表征对齐，缩小动作学习前的表征错配；2）在目标演示上微调时，约束检索演示的表征变化幅度，以保留预训练知识，抑制不必要的表征漂移。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboCasa及真实机器人共13个任务上，结合Diffusion Policy与GR00T，微调平均成功率分布内提升9.5点、分布外提升13.6点，并优于所有对比基线。

Conclusion: 检索锚定的表征对齐可作为预训练策略少样本适应的通用正则化手段，兼顾分布内性能与分布外泛化，适用于多种策略骨干与仿真/真机平台。

Abstract: Pre-trained robot policies, such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, provide a strong foundation for generalist robotic manipulation, but adapting them to new tasks with only a few demonstrations remains challenging. Directly fine-tuning a pre-trained policy often achieves strong in-distribution performance, yet it can distort the representations learned during pre-training and weaken the model's generalization ability under out-of-distribution shifts. We propose Retrieval-Anchored Representation Alignment (RARA), a two-stage adaptation method, to address this issue. First, RARA retrieves representation features from pretraining with similar task intent and motion, and aligns the policy's representations of the target demonstrations with the pre-trained representations of the retrieved demonstrations. Second, RARA fine-tunes the policy on the target demonstrations while limiting how much its representations of the retrieved demonstrations change during fine-tuning. By reducing the representation mismatch before action learning and preserving pre-trained knowledge during adaptation, RARA limits unnecessary representation drift during fine-tuning. Experiments with Diffusion Policy and GR00T on 13 tasks in RoboTwin, RoboCasa, and on a real robot show that RARA improves the average success of fine-tuning by 9.5 points in-distribution and 13.6 points out-of-distribution, and outperforms all compared baselines. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [186] [JitterVLA: A Replay Benchmark for Motion Quality in VLA-Driven Human-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13628)
*Faith Ogunwoye,Didar Seyidov,Konstantin Gubernatorov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JitterVLA评测框架，通过重放相同命令与到达时序隔离控制器本身影响，系统评估插值与jerk限制生成对VLA策略稀疏延迟动作的执行平滑性改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略输出的稀疏目标到达机器人控制器时存在延迟与不规则性，导致即使预测合理也会在人群附近产生突变运动；现有缓解手段在不同数据流、延迟与指标下评测口径不一，无法判断改善究竟来自策略还是控制器。

Method: JitterVLA将相同命令与到达轨迹重放到不同控制器上，从而隔离控制器效应。评测对象包括合成reach、20人手部轨迹、三个开源VLA流程（838回合）与一台实体UR3。对比手段为插值与jerk受限生成，指标为建模p99 jerk与跟踪误差，并检验熵-不连续关联与熵门控。

Result: 插值在所有来源上降低p99 jerk，200 ms延迟下人体运动从615降至106 m/s^3（-83%），跟踪代价2.0 cm，并在598个新回合复现；jerk受限生成进一步降低jerk但代价更大。熵-不连续关联在两个流程中呈相反且可复现的符号，熵门控仅对其中之一有效。UR3上插值在六组探索性配对中使加速度计jerk下降3.0–14.0%，位置派生指标未能分辨该对比。

Conclusion: 动作平滑改善主要由控制器侧的重放/插值决定，可用作以人为中心研究前的低成本筛选工具；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies issue sparse targets whose arrival at the robot controller is delayed and irregular, so a reasonable predicted action can still produce abrupt motion near people. Remedies are evaluated on different streams, delays and metrics. JitterVLA replays identical commands and arrival traces through alternative controllers, isolating the controller's effect. We apply it to synthetic reaches, hand trajectories from 20 people, three open VLA pipelines (838 episodes) and a physical UR3. Interpolation lowered modeled p99 jerk for every source, by 83% (615 to 106 m/s^3) on human motion with 200 ms delay, at a 2.0 cm tracking cost, and the effect replicated on 598 new episodes; jerk-limited generation lowered jerk further at a larger cost. Entropy-discontinuity associations had opposite, replicated signs in two pipelines, and entropy gating helped in one but not the other. On the UR3, interpolation lowered accelerometer-derived jerk by 3.0-14.0% in all six exploratory pairs, while a position-derived metric did not resolve the contrast. JitterVLA supports screening before human-centred studies; code and data will be released.

</details>


### [187] [AutoBio+: Benchmarking Dexterous Manipulation with Dynamic Experimental Phenomena in Realistic Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13643)
*Xuanye Wu,Yue Shi,Yongcan Yang,Yiqun Wang,Zhaoqi Wan,Dayu Xia,Jiezhang Cao,Xiangfei Li,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoBio+：面向真实视觉条件下生物实验室灵巧操作的仿真基准与并行框架，含14任务/4机器人本体、动态实验现象（火焰/蒸汽/气泡）、高斯泼溅真实背景混合渲染、PFSM并行数据生成与闭环评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化生物实验室要求机器人在真实视觉条件与动态实验现象下完成灵巧工具交互，现有基准缺乏此类真实感与动态性，难以系统评测策略能力。

Method: 构建14个任务、4种机器人本体的仿真基准；引入可控动态实验现象（火焰、蒸汽、气泡）；混合渲染管线将交互式仿真前景与6个真实实验室场景的高斯泼溅背景融合；基于mjlab搭建并行仿真框架，提出PFSM并行有限状态机，结合批量化渲染与解耦轨迹回放，支持演示生成与模型无关的闭环策略评测。

Result: 评测显示不同策略在各任务上性能差异显著，暴露现有操作策略的具体优势与短板；真实世界实验表明sim-to-real gap有所缩小，验证了该基准用于真实感操作评测的有效性。

Conclusion: AutoBio+提供了具备动态现象与真实视觉条件的可扩展评测平台，可作为生物实验室灵巧操作策略的系统评测与数据生成工具，并具备向真实场景迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Automating biological laboratories requires robots to perform dexterous tool interactions under realistic visual conditions while handling dynamic experimental phenomena, presenting significant challenges for robotic manipulation. To enable systematic evaluation under these conditions, we introduce AutoBio+, a simulation benchmark and framework for dexterous manipulation in realistic biological laboratory environments. AutoBio+ comprises 14 tasks across 4 robot embodiments, spanning functional tool use, precision manipulation, language-conditioned visual grounding, and instrument operation. We introduce controllable dynamic experimental phenomena, including flames, vapor, and bubbles, to capture the evolving visual conditions of laboratory procedures. To further enhance environmental realism, we develop a hybrid rendering pipeline that integrates interactive simulated foreground objects with Gaussian-splatting backgrounds reconstructed from 6 real laboratory scenes. For efficient large-scale data generation and evaluation, we further build a parallel simulation framework on top of mjlab, proposing Parallel Finite-State Machine (PFSM) and integrating batched rendering with decoupled trajectory replay for demonstration generation and model-independent closed-loop policy evaluation. Our evaluation reveals substantial variation in policy performance across tasks, exposing distinct strengths and weaknesses of current manipulation policies. Real-world experiments further demonstrate a reduced sim-to-real gap, supporting the validity of AutoBio+ for realistic manipulation evaluation. Supplementary materials are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [188] [Cooperative Aerial Transportation of a Payload with a Sliding Cable Attachment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13665)
*Sebastian Tilaguy Lezama,Diego S D'Antonio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 双四旋翼通过单根可滑动缆绳绕过载荷挂钩实现协同吊运，提出基于期望载荷位姿、缆绳分配与系统形状的可行参考轨迹生成框架，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 载荷非刚性固定于缆绳某一点，缆绳可在挂钩处滑动，引入缆绳分配变量耦合四旋翼运动、缆绳几何与载荷动力学，使协同吊运的参考轨迹生成与约束满足更加困难。

Method: 双四旋翼由单根不可伸长缆绳连接，缆绳穿过载荷上无摩擦挂钩并可在此滑动，形成两段缆绳的分配变量；基于期望载荷位姿、缆绳分配与系统形状，在缆绳长度约束和椭圆工作空间约束下构造可行参考生成框架；四旋翼跟踪几何生成的轨迹，同时保持缆绳正张力并满足执行器限制；载荷位姿由系统几何估计，实测位置仅用于验证期望轨迹。

Result: 通过实验演示对方法进行了评估；载荷位姿估计由系统几何得到，实测位置仅用于验证期望轨迹。

Conclusion: 该工作为缆绳可滑动的双四旋翼吊运提供了一种可行参考生成与轨迹跟踪方案，实验表明可在张力为正、满足执行器限制的条件下实现协同载荷运输。

Abstract: This paper presents a dual-quadrotor hook- suspended transport system for cooperative aerial manipulation of a payload that is not rigidly attached to a fixed point along the cable. Two quadrotors are connected by a single inextensible cable that passes through a frictionless hook mounted on the payload, allowing the cable to slide between its two segments. This introduces a cable-distribution variable that couples the quadrotor motion, cable geometry, and payload dynamics. A feasible reference-generation framework is formulated using the desired payload pose, cable distribution, and system shape while satisfying the cable-length constraint and elliptical workspace. The quadrotors track the geometrically generated trajectories while maintaining positive cable tension and respecting actuator limits. The payload pose is estimated from the system geometry, and its measured position is used only to validate the desired trajectory. The approach is evaluated through experimental demonstrations.

</details>


### [189] [Towards a Unified Taxonomy of Agricultural Harvesting Technologies: Linking Physical Interaction, Automation, and Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13688)
*Renato Cristiano Torres,Fabiano Mariath D Oliveira,Claudio Gottschalg Duque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以"物理作用原理"与"自动化程度"为两轴的收获技术统一分类框架，并将选择性视为感知—决策影响物理执行时所涌现的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收获系统按作物类型、脱除原理、感知技术、机器人构型或自动化程度等多种维度描述，标签体系混杂，导致不同系统难以直接比较，缺乏共同概念基础。

Method: 概念性分类研究：构建双轴分类体系（植物—机器相互作用的物理原理 × 自动化水平），将选择性定义为涌现功能属性，用以表达感知与决策起作用的生物与空间尺度；据此对切割、拔取、振动、冲击、刷取、气动及组合式机械化方法，与半自动和机器人收获方法进行统一归类分析。

Result: 分析表明自动化程度提高并不必然带来更高选择性；当感知与决策直接作用于"单产品级"执行时发生质性转变，收获由分布式/整体式相互作用转向局部化、选择性操作。

Conclusion: 该框架为定位与比较机械化、自动化及机器人收获技术提供共同概念基础，通过分离物理脱除机制、自动化水平与由此产生的选择性，厘清技术边界、识别中间构型，支撑未来收获系统的分析与开发。

Abstract: Harvesting technologies have evolved from conventional mechanized systems based on bulk mechanical interaction toward increasingly automated and robotic systems capable of perception, decision-making, and localized actuation. This evolution has also created a classification problem, as harvesting systems are commonly described according to crop type, mechanical removal principle, sensing technology, robotic architecture, or degree of automation, making direct comparison difficult. This paper proposes a unified taxonomy for agricultural harvesting technologies based on two primary classification axes: the physical principle governing plant-machine interaction and the level of automation incorporated into the harvesting process. Selectivity is treated as an emergent functional attribute that expresses the biological and spatial scale at which perception and decision-making influence physical actuation. Conventional mechanized methods, including cutting, pulling, vibration, impact, brushing, pneumatic action, and combined systems, are analyzed together with semi-automated and robotic harvesting approaches. The analysis shows that increasing automation does not necessarily result in greater selectivity. A qualitative transition occurs when perception and decision-making directly influence product-level actuation, shifting harvesting from distributed or bulk interaction toward localized and selective manipulation. The proposed framework provides a common conceptual basis for positioning and comparing mechanized, automated, and robotic harvesting technologies. By separating the physical mechanism of product removal from the automation level and resulting selectivity, the taxonomy clarifies technological boundaries, identifies intermediate configurations, and supports the analysis and development of future harvesting systems.

</details>


### [190] [SmolWAM: A Latent World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13740)
*Timi Owolabi,Hikmah Olawore,Frances Adelakun,Ayotomiwa Oyewumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmolWAM 从 2B 参数的 Cosmos Policy 蒸馏出一个轻量潜空间世界-动作模型（WAM），通过重构推理接口实现边缘端高效动作与未来视觉潜变量的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型依赖视频扩散 Transformer，计算成本过高，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，因此需要一种保留预训练主干能力但大幅降低推理开销的轻量方案。

Method: 以 2B Cosmos Policy 为教师，保留预训练主干并重建推理接口：采用单帧视觉潜变量、保留主干注意力汇（attention sinks）的缓存文本特征、仅使用 28 个 Transformer 块中的 12 个，以及 1,008 个 token，通过单步 rectified flow 读出动作与未来视觉潜变量；训练仅用 3.6 GPU 小时。

Result: 在 400 个 LIBERO 回合中成功率达 95.5%；每 16 动作块耗时 57 ms、2.3 TFLOP，仅需 2.4 GiB 显存；相比教师模型快 11 倍、FLOPs 减少 18.5 倍、显存降低 3.2 倍，且世界头仍能预测场景运动。

Conclusion: SmolWAM 表明通过精心重构推理接口与裁剪主干，可在极低训练与推理成本下维持世界-动作模型的高任务成功率与未来预测能力，为边缘端具身智能部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict a robot's actions together with the observations those actions will produce. However, the strongest models rely on video diffusion transformers that are far too computationally expensive for resource-constrained edge devices. We present SmolWAM, a latent WAM that we derive from the 2B-parameter Cosmos Policy by keeping its pretrained trunk and rebuilding its inference interface: single-frame visual latents, cached text features that preserve the trunk's attention sinks, 12 of 28 transformer blocks, and 1,008 tokens from which actions and future visual latents are read out in one rectified-flow step. Trained in 3.6 GPU-hours, SmolWAM succeeds in 95.5% of 400 LIBERO episodes at 57 ms and 2.3 TFLOP per 16-action chunk with 2.4 GiB of GPU memory, 11x faster than its teacher with 18.5x fewer FLOPs and 3.2x less memory, and its world head still forecasts scene motion.

</details>


### [191] [When LiDAR Fades, Radar Takes Over: LiDAR Reliability-Aware Adaptive Localization Under Airborne Obscurants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13788)
*Moumita Mukherjee,Avijit Banerjee,Anton Koval,George Nikolakopoulo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单类时序学习框架，用LSTM自编码器检测LiDAR退化并实现LiDAR-雷达惯性里程计自适应切换，在GNSS拒止烟尘隧道中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR惯性里程计在地下烟尘等悬浮遮蔽物环境中可靠性显著下降，而雷达相对鲁棒，需要一种无需退化标签的可靠性感知定位方案。

Method: 构建空间、跨模态、几何/运动、强度、测量时序新鲜度等多类退化敏感特征，在滑窗上用仅以正常工况训练的LSTM自编码器建模其时间演化，实现无标签退化检测；由LiDAR可靠性估计驱动LIO与雷达惯性里程计RIO的自适应切换。

Result: 在真实地面机器人的GNSS拒止地下隧道（正常与浓烟条件）数据上完成退化检测、模态自适应切换与严重退化下持续定位输出；特征族消融显示精简特征表示在浓烟场景下可提升退化感知定位性能。

Conclusion: 单类时序学习可有效支撑LiDAR退化检测与可靠性感知的多模态里程计切换，特征简约性对单类传感器退化检测至关重要，代码将开源。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is widely used for real-time localization and mapping, but its reliability can degrade substantially in subterranean environments affected by smoke, dust, and other airborne obscurants, whereas radar remains comparatively robust. This article presents a one-class temporal learning framework for LiDAR degradation detection and reliability-aware localization under such conditions. A broad set of degradation-sensitive cues, including spatial, cross-modal, geometric/motion, intensity, and measurement-recency features, is evaluated to characterize changes in LiDAR sensing quality. Their temporal evolution is modelled over sliding windows using an LSTM autoencoder trained exclusively on nominal operation, enabling deviations from healthy sensor behaviour to be detected without requiring labelled degradation data. The resulting LiDAR reliability estimate governs adaptive selection between LIO and radar inertial odometry (RIO), retaining LIO during reliable sensing and transferring localization authority to RIO when degradation is detected. The framework is evaluated using real-world ground robot data collected in GNSS-denied subterranean tunnels under nominal and dense-smoke conditions. Experimental results demonstrate degradation detection, adaptive modality switching, and continued localization output under severe LiDAR degradation. A feature family ablation study further shows that a reduced feature representation can improve degradation-aware localization performance in the evaluated dense-smoke scenarios, highlighting the importance of feature parsimony in one-class sensor degradation detection. The code will be released as open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [192] [Dream to Remember: Continual Steering of Generalist Robot Policies with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13816)
*Bahey Tharwat,Iman Nematollahi,Marvin Alles,Xingyuan Zhang,Akshay L Chandra,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 D2R：冻结通用机器人策略与世界模型，用可训练"转向模块"持续适配新任务，通过想象回放排练旧任务以避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略受限于示范数据质量与覆盖度，而顺序任务下微调或噪声转向都会导致灾难性遗忘：微调破坏旧任务，转向只是把遗忘转移到共享模块。

Method: 冻结通用策略与世界模型，仅训练小型转向模块调节输入噪声；学习新任务时用冻结世界模型的想象 rollout 排练旧任务，无需 replay buffer 或新数据；世界模型足够可靠时全在想象中学习，否则在真实环境中学习新任务、仅用世界模型排练旧任务。

Result: CALVIN 顺序八任务全部在想象中学习，平均成功率 63.7%→74.8%，而 LoRA 微调降至 11.7%；用现成 π0 与 A2World 在 LIBERO-90 学未见任务并保持原微调套件性能；真实 Franka 上顺序三任务全想象提升并保留先前改进。

Conclusion: 冻结策略+世界模型想象排练的持续转向范式可在不存储数据、不更新主干的前提下兼顾新任务学习与旧技能保持，且在仿真与真机上均有效。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained with imitation learning and are bounded by the quality and coverage of their demonstrations. Reinforcement learning can improve and extend them, either by fine-tuning the policy weights or by training a small module to steer the input noise of a frozen generative policy. When tasks arrive sequentially, both approaches suffer from catastrophic forgetting. Fine-tuning on a task degrades prior tasks, and steering only shifts forgetting to the shared steering module. We propose Dream to Remember (D2R), which continually steers a frozen generalist policy toward new tasks, while retaining its earlier skills. As the steering module learns a new task, a frozen pretrained world model rehearses prior tasks through imagined rollouts, anchoring the module to its earlier behavior without a replay buffer or new data. When the world model is faithful enough, D2R learns the new task entirely in imagination. Otherwise, it learns the task in the environment while using the world model only to rehearse prior tasks. On CALVIN, D2R improves eight tasks in sequence entirely in imagination, raising average success from 63.7% to 74.8%, while LoRA fine-tuning on the same sequence drops it to 11.7%. With a frozen off-the-shelf generalist policy and world model, $\pi_0$ and A2World, D2R learns unseen LIBERO-90 tasks in the environment while keeping $\pi_0$'s performance on the LIBERO suites it was fine-tuned on, even though the world model is not accurate enough for policy optimization. Finally, on a real Franka robot, D2R improves three tasks in sequence entirely in imagination while retaining the earlier improvements.

</details>


### [193] [MC-EvoBench: Auditing Experience-Driven Self-Improvement in Minecraft Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13829)
*Jingwei Song,Zhisheng Chen,Bingfan Zeng,Jing Tang,Yubo Hou,Jinhao Jing,Zhengwei Xie,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MC-EvoBench：受控的Minecraft基准，含19条注册式进化链（12条主评测链、7条校准/探针链）与3组图对齐配对探针，用于分离"能力增益"与"资源携带/来源暴露不均/早期执行失败"等混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身递归自我改进的核心难点是让智能体把交互历史转化为可跨世界持久、可迁移的能力；但保存的记忆或某次成功动作本身不能证明获得了可复用能力，评测须排除资源携带、来源暴露不均与早期执行失败等干扰。

Method: 构建MC-EvoBench受控Minecraft基准：固定任务目录19条进化链；每任务表示为任务-状态图，定义阶段级环境验证器；分配匹配的source/target角色，重置target世界，仅允许声明的agent artifact跨越source-target边界。每个探针在匹配环境步预算与5个随机种子下评测no-prior、related、unrelated、shuffled四种来源条件，报告TSR、WGP、VMP并保留溯源记录。

Result: 揭示controller与planner-backed变体间存在稳定直接能力差距；相关经验未产生稳定迁移——两个tool探针均无提升，wood-species的小幅增益依赖单一seed且所有置信区间含零。阶段审计显示早期采木与合成瓶颈常导致后期能力未被观测到。

Conclusion: MC-EvoBench为持久且可迁移的具身能力提供了受控测试台，并表明当前方法尚缺乏稳定迁移证据；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: A central challenge in embodied recursive self-improvement is enabling agents to convert interaction history into capabilities that persist beyond one world and transfer to later tasks. A saved memory or successful final action does not by itself establish that a reusable capability was acquired; evaluation must separate capability change from carried-over resources, unequal source exposure, and early execution failures. We construct MC-EvoBench as a controlled Minecraft benchmark with a fixed catalog of 19 registered evolution chains, including 12 primary evaluation chains and seven calibration or probe chains, together with three graph-aligned paired probe families for transfer. The benchmark represents each task as a task-state graph, defines stage-level environment verifiers, assigns matched source and target roles, resets the target world, and permits only a declared agent artifact to cross the source-target boundary. For each probe, no-prior, related, unrelated, and shuffled source conditions are evaluated under matched environment-step budgets and five seeds; we report TSR, WGP, and VMP and retain provenance records. The benchmark reveals a consistent direct-capability gap between controller and planner-backed variants, while related experience yields no stable transfer: it does not improve either tool probe, its small wood-species gain hinges on one seed, and all intervals include zero. Stage audits further show that early wood-collection and crafting bottlenecks often leave later capabilities unobserved. MC-EvoBench thus offers a controlled testbed for durable, transferable embodied capabilities. Code and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [194] [Mapless, Stabilizing, and Anti-Stuck Flipper Control for Articulated Tracked Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13838)
*Valentýn Číhala,Pavel Sarga,Martin Pecka,Tomáš Svoboda,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无地图式履带机器人翻板控制器SAS，用IMU/关节角/短时运动提示的组合控制替代需高程地图的方案，仿真与实机穿越质量与SOTA地图法相当，且能通过LiDAR不可见的草丛隐藏原木。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主/半自主翻板(flipper)控制器普遍依赖机器人下方及前方地形的高程图。底盘下方地形被传感器遮挡，只能靠里程计积分记忆，漂移会污染地图，导致地形表示不可靠。

Method: SAS为无地图控制器，包含：1) 车身横滚与俯仰的稳定项；2) 卡死时的脱困项。输入仅用IMU、翻板关节角，以及一个只表示"是否在动"的运动提示；该提示在短窗口内计算且从不累积，因此不继承里程计积分漂移。在四翻板履带机器人上部署，并与人工遥操作及既有翻板控制器在仿真和真实障碍上做大规模对比。

Result: 仿真与实机上，SAS的穿越质量与SOTA地图式控制器相当；在藏于高草中、LiDAR不可见的原木上，最佳地图式控制器卡死，而SAS顺利通过该隐藏障碍。

Conclusion: 免地图、免里程计累积的轻量感知—控制范式可在翻板机器人越障中达到地图式SOTA水平，并具备对传感器不可见隐藏障碍的鲁棒性，为越野移动机器人提供了更稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Nearly every autonomous or semi-autonomous flipper controller assumes access to an elevation map of the terrain under and in front of the robot. The terrain beneath the chassis is occluded in the sensors, so the map is remembered through odometry integration, whose drift corrupts it. We present Stabilizing Anti-Stuck (SAS), a mapless controller that combines a stabilization term for body roll and pitch with an anti-stuck term that frees the robot when it gets stuck. As input, SAS uses the IMU, the flipper joint angles, and a motion cue that only tells whether the robot is moving. The cue is computed over a short window and never accumulated, so SAS does not inherit the odometry-integration drift that corrupts the map. We deploy SAS on a tracked robot with four flippers and evaluate it against manual teleoperation and prior flipper controllers in a large comparison, both in simulation and on a real obstacle. In simulation and on the real robot, SAS achieves traversal quality comparable to state-of-the-art map-based controllers. On a log hidden in tall grass, invisible to the LiDAR, the best map-based controller gets stuck while SAS traverses the hidden obstacle without a problem.

</details>


### [195] [HorizonNav: Geometry-Guided Video Diffusion for Anticipatory 3D Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13849)
*Hajira Amjad,Valerii Serpiva,Viacheslav Liubimov,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HorizonNav将单张RGB图像与文本提示经VLM生成化提示驱动视频扩散模型，预测未来观测，再用ORB-SLAM3与Video Depth Anything做位姿与稠密深度估计，并提出几何引导的深度对齐，提升多视角稠密深度一致性，从而重建可用于规划的3D环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人前瞻性导航需要几何一致的可通行3D空间表示，而单目生成视频虽时序连贯，却缺乏多视角几何约束，导致重建深度不一致、自由空间与碰撞约束不可靠，影响下游规划可行性。

Method: 以VLM按指令解析初始RGB与文本目标并构造结构化提示，驱动视频扩散生成未来观测序列；用单目ORB-SLAM3估计相机位姿、Video Depth Anything预测稠密深度；核心是几何引导深度对齐，将位姿与三角化视觉地标结合，约束多视角稠密深度，改善重建几何一致性；随后在想象3D环境中做RRT导航与碰撞约束验证。

Result: 19场景地平线研究中，可规划场景从P20的13/19提升到P81的15/19，RRT成功从62/90增至75/90；走廊研究中25条可评估生成相机轨迹有6条违反重建碰撞约束，RRT在5/6情况恢复可行替代；67次尝试中58次可用重建，36次支持下游规划、22次不支持，暴露重建与自由空间失败模式；15次走廊重建在匹配结构地标处平均误差约0.30 m。

Conclusion: 几何引导深度对齐显著提升想象环境重建的几何一致性与规划可行性，并把重建与自由空间失败模式显式暴露而非掩盖；但约0.30 m的重建误差与部分不可规划重建说明该方法仍受生成视频与单目深度精度限制。

Abstract: We present HorizonNav, a video diffusion-based framework for anticipatory robot navigation that transforms a single RGB image and a text prompt into a geometrically aligned representation of plausible surrounding 3D space. Given the initial observation and a textual description of the reconstruction objective, a vision-language model, guided by designed instructions, interprets the scene and constructs structured prompts for a video diffusion model. The resulting video sequence provides plausible future observations of the environment, which are subsequently processed using monocular ORB-SLAM3 for camera pose estimation and Video Depth Anything for dense depth prediction. Our central contribution is a geometry-guided depth alignment stage that combines the estimated camera poses with triangulated visual landmarks to constrain dense depth predictions across multiple views. This alignment improves the geometric consistency of the reconstructed 3D environment, addressing inconsistencies that temporal coherence of the generated video alone cannot resolve. The experimental evaluation of navigation in imagined 3D environment shows that across a 19-scene horizon study, planning-feasible scenes increase from 13/19 at P20 to 15/19 at P81, while successful RRT trials increase from 62/90 to 75/90. In a separate corridor study, 6 of 25 evaluable generated-camera trajectories violate the reconstructed collision constraints; RRT planner recovers feasible alternatives in 5 of those 6 cases. A larger archive contains 58 usable reconstructions from 67 attempted cases, of which 36 support a downstream plan and 22 do not, exposing reconstruction and free-space failure modes rather than hiding them without lidar. Across 15 corridor reconstructions, the mean reconstruction error is approximately 0.30 m at matched structural landmarks.

</details>


### [196] [BT-Agent: Deploying Onboard Robot Agents with Behavior Trees and Multi-Fidelity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13862)
*Fabio Hübel,Adam Lilja,Yue Yin,Siming He,Claire Tomlin,Shankar Sastry,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BT-Agent：在机载端将 LLM 任务决策与预定义行为树（BT）技能结合，并用结构化失败反馈实现在机任务推理与响应式执行；在四足机器人 9B 模型上完成全机载验证，并给出 161 任务多保真度基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在有限算力与内存下部署语言模型智能体，需要同时满足任务推理与实时响应的要求。纯端到端 LLM 决策往往延迟高、失败处理弱，因此需要一种能在机载约束下兼顾推理与执行控制的架构。

Method: BT-Agent 采用 LLM 驱动的高层任务决策叠加预定义行为树技能的组合式架构：行为树技能强制检查执行前置条件，并在失败时返回结构化失败信息；同时构建 161 任务多保真度基准，全部任务做纯文本评测，其中 39 个任务在 Habitat 仿真中评测。

Result: 在四足机器人上以 9B 模型实现全机载运行，覆盖五类任务；干预测试显示本地检查可立即中止正在进行的导航而无需等待模型再次决策，智能体仍可重试或报告约束。文本评测吞吐量比仿真高 10.9 倍，仿真揭示感知与运动失败如何改变任务结果。相较最强 Act/ReAct 基线，4B 与 9B 模型上成功率提升约 10 与 5 个百分点；消融表明结构化工具调用与信息性失败反馈最为关键。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 决策与行为树技能、结构化失败反馈结合，可在机载算力下实现具备实时反应与恢复能力的机器人智能体，且高效的多保真度评测有助于快速迭代智能体设计。

Abstract: Deploying language-model agents onboard mobile robots requires task reasoning and responsive execution within limited compute and memory. We propose BT-Agent, an onboard architecture combining LLM-driven task decisions with predefined behavior-tree (BT) skills. These skills enforce execution conditions and return structured failure feedback. We demonstrate fully onboard operation with a 9B model on a quadruped across five task categories. Intervention tests show that local checks stop ongoing navigation without waiting for another model decision, while the agent remains active to retry or report constraints. To compare agent designs efficiently, we introduce a 161-task multi-fidelity benchmark, evaluated in text on all tasks and in Habitat on 39. Text supports 10.9$\times$ higher evaluation throughput, while simulation reveals how perception and motion failures change task outcomes. BT-Agent's largest success gains over the strongest Act/ReAct baseline occur at 4B and 9B: approximately 10 and 5 percentage points. Ablations identify structured tool calls and informative failure feedback as the most useful components.

</details>


### [197] [Chronos Policy: Learning Robot Policies on Physical Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13929)
*Changhao Wang,Jaden Clark,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Chronos Policy 将物理时间显式建模进机器人扩散策略：以观测捕获时刻与期望动作执行时刻为条件，对比时间戳嵌入与时间感知 RoPE 两种方案，并通过时序随机化训练支持异步多速率数据，在动态与多传感器任务上提升性能且保持准静态任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流机器人策略把感知与控制建模成固定速率的「观测-动作」序列，物理时间被隐式编码在序列位置上。一旦观测速率或延迟变化、推理时延波动，这一假设即失效，策略的时间语义随之错位。

Method: 以观测时间戳和期望动作执行时刻作为显式条件输入扩散 Transformer。比较两条融合路径：在 token 特征上加时间戳嵌入，或通过时间感知 RoPE 把物理时间编码进位置编码。实验发现后者更鲁棒，因为它让注意力直接以物理时间为基准，而不改变 token 内容表示。训练阶段对采样哪些观测、动作块起始时刻做时间随机化，使策略暴露于更广的感知与执行调度分布，从而无需把异步、多速率测量强行对齐到固定时间网格。

Result: 在仿真与真实实验中，Chronos Policy 在观测到动作时序至关重要的动态任务、以及观测间相对时序起作用的多传感器任务上均取得性能提升；在标准准静态任务上保持原有性能，同时提升了对感知与推理时序变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式物理时间条件化优于隐式序列位置假设，时间感知 RoPE 是更稳健的实现方式，时序随机化训练使策略能直接消费异步多速率数据，为时间敏感的机器人策略学习提供了一条通用且部署友好的路径。

Abstract: Robot policies often convert sensing and control into fixed-rate observation-action sequences, leaving physical time implicit in sequence position. This assumption breaks when observation rates or delays vary, or when inference latency changes. We introduce Chronos Policy, which makes physical time explicit by conditioning on observation capture times and desired action execution times. We study two ways to incorporate time into a diffusion transformer: adding timestamp embeddings to token features, or encoding physical time in positional encoding through time-aware rotary positional encodings (time-aware RoPE). We find that time-aware RoPE is more robust, allowing attention to depend directly on physical time while keeping token content unchanged. During training, we temporally randomize which observations are sampled and when the action chunk begins, exposing the policy to a wider range of sensing and execution schedules that may occur during deployments. This also enables learning from asynchronous, multi-rate data without forcing all measurements onto a fixed temporal grid. Across simulation and real-world experiments, Chronos Policy improves performance on dynamic tasks, where observation-to-action timing is critical, and on multisensory tasks, where the relative timing between observations matters. On standard quasi-static tasks, it preserves nominal performance while improving robustness to changes in sensing and inference timing.

</details>


### [198] [Noise Space Learning for VLA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13943)
*Junjie Lu,Chuheng Zhang,Yucen Wang,Yuhua Jiang,Xinyao Qin,Min Xu,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将噪声空间学习（NSL）形式化为一个VLA适应接口，通过在固定预训练流匹配VLA的前提下学习上下文相关的噪声分布，并实例化为PPO/SAC/AWR三种算法，给出信息论分析及跨benchmark与真机的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA编码了通用操作先验，但适配具体任务时需在有限的机器人交互、算力与安全约束下权衡。直接微调大模型代价高且易破坏先验，因此需要一个既能复用预训练能力、又能高效适应的高效接口。

Method: 核心思路为NSL：保持预训练flow-matching VLA参数冻结，转而学习一个上下文条件化的噪声分布（即在去噪采样起点/噪声空间施加策略化调节），从而把适配问题降维到小型可训练模块。作者在此基础上引入PPO、SAC、AWR三类学习算法，将NSL实现为统一接口；并补充了信息论层面的分析，解释NSL为何能实现高效适配。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin及四个真实机器人操作任务上，围绕交互预算、计算开销与执行约束进行系统实验，验证了多种NSL变体的有效性，并给出了不同资源与部署场景下的算法选择建议。

Conclusion: NSL是一个通用且实用的VLA适配接口：不牺牲预训练先验、训练参数少、可与多种RL/IL算法兼容，适合受限于交互预算与算力的真实部署。后续价值在于将噪声空间作为轻量适配层，并依据任务约束选择具体算法。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models encode broad manipulation priors, yet adapting them to specific tasks requires balancing limited robot interaction, computational resources, and safety constraints. Noise space learning (NSL) adapts flow-matching-based VLAs by learning a context-conditioned distribution over noise while keeping the pretrained VLA fixed. Building on prior noise-steering methods, we formulate NSL as an interface for VLA adaptation that accommodates different learning algorithms and instantiate it with PPO, SAC, and AWR. Theoretically, we provide an information-theoretic analysis of how NSL enables efficient adaptation. Empirically, we study these NSL algorithms on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and four real-robot manipulation tasks under interaction, computation, and execution constraints. Across these settings, we develop a suite of effective NSL variants and derive practical recommendations for choosing among them under different resource and deployment regimes.

</details>


### [199] [Splat2Act: From Captured Scenes to Personalized VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13945)
*Xiaomeng Ye,Krishna Jaganathan,Gyubeum Lim,Gunjan Chhablani,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Splat2Act是一个全自动real-to-sim-to-real流水线，仅用手持手机扫描和物体图片，将预训练VLA策略（π_0.5）个性化到特定场景与物体集：基于3DGS构建建筑尺度真实感数字孪生与碰撞几何，自动发现台面/非台面承载面并生成抓取任务，由特权仿真oracle产出示范，两阶段微调后在目标场景达到62.5%成功率，真机Stretch 3上12任务子集成功率为63.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略进入用户家庭/办公室后需抓取货架、柜台、桌面上的物体，但即使π_0.5这类强通用策略在未适配的部署场景中抓取成功率为0%，而在每个用户环境中采集真实示范成本极高。现有real-to-sim工作多只重建单张桌面，无法覆盖建筑尺度室内环境。作者希望仅凭场景与物体的被动采集，实现策略的自动化场景个性化。

Method: 方法为自动化real-to-sim-to-real流水线：（1）用手持手机扫描，以3D Gaussian Splatting构建真实感数字孪生并附加碰撞几何；（2）自动发现桌面与非桌面承载面（receptacles），构建可行抓取任务；（3）用特权仿真oracle生成成功示范；（4）两阶段微调π_0.5——Stage 1在公开物体资产上适配（仅部署场景或全部101个场景），Stage 2在部署场景承载面上用用户自有物体进行个性化。人类仅需提供场景与物体采集。

Result: 在101个扫描的校园环境中（中位可导航面积104.87 m²、7个承载面），流水线扩展出852个承载面与超过68,000条抓取示范。最强个性化策略在目标场景取得62.5%总体抓取成功率；部署于真机Stretch 3上，在12任务子集上达到63.9%成功率，与仿真中同任务的66.7%接近。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅使用仿真oracle数据即可通过自动化real-to-sim-to-real训练，将VLA操作策略个性化到用户场景与物体，且仿真成功率能较好预测真机表现，为免真实示范的策略个性化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A user who brings a generalist manipulation policy into their home or office wants it to pick up their objects from their shelves, counters, and tables. Yet even a strong generalist like $\pi_{0.5}$ achieves 0% pickup success in our captured deployment scenes without adaptation, and collecting real demonstrations in every user's environment is prohibitively expensive. We present Splat2Act, an automated real-to-sim-to-real pipeline that personalizes a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a specific scene and object set using only a handheld phone scan and images of the user's objects. Unlike prior real-to-sim work that reconstructs individual tabletops, Splat2Act operates on building-scale indoor environments: it builds a photorealistic digital twin with 3D Gaussian Splatting and collision geometry, automatically discovers tabletop and non-tabletop receptacles, constructs feasible pickup tasks, and generates successful demonstrations with a privileged simulation oracle, with only scene and object capture required from a human. Applied to 101 scanned campus environments with a median navigable area of 104.87 m$^2$ and 7 receptacles, the pipeline yields 852 receptacles and over 68,000 pickup demonstrations. We fine-tune $\pi_{0.5}$ in two stages: Stage 1 adapts to hundreds of public object assets, drawn either from the deployment scene alone or from all 101 scenes; Stage 2 personalizes to the user's own objects on the deployment scene's receptacles. Our strongest personalized policy reaches 62.5% aggregate pickup success in the target scene. Deployed on a real Stretch 3, the same policy achieves 63.9% success on a 12-task subset, closely tracking its 66.7% simulated success on those tasks. These results suggest that automated real-to-sim-to-real training can personalize VLA manipulation policies using only simulation-oracle data.

</details>
